def crd (y : (VS D).V) : Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E := crdOf X.B.I X.ιE X.bas y

def eR : Fin D.R.rS → Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E := fun a => X.crd (X.eX a)
def fR : Fin D.R.rS → Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E := fun a => X.crd (X.fY a)
def zR (u : D.what₂.Word) : Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E := X.crd (X.zu u)

def SinfU (u : D.what₂.Word) : Set X.B.I.Ginf := Sinf X.B.I X.eR (X.zR u)
def NinfU (u : D.what₂.Word) : Set X.B.I.Ginf := Ninf X.B.I X.eR (X.zR u)
def NfinU (u : D.what₂.Word) : Set X.B.I.Gf := Nfin X.B.I X.eR (X.zR u)

def psiInfU (u : D.what₂.Word) : X.B.I.Ginf → ℂ := psiInf X.B.I X.B.g X.eR X.fR (X.zR u)
def psiFinU (u : D.what₂.Word) : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ := psiFin X.B.I X.psiEf X.B.g X.eR X.fR (X.zR u)

def K2 : Set X.B.I.Gf := K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw
def chi2 : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ := chi2f X.B.I X.psiEf X.B.g X.e0 X.f0 X.z0 X.bw X.bwd

def volK2 : ℝ := (X.B.μf X.K2).toReal

def dv (v : InfinitePlace X.B.I.F) : ℝ := fdegOf (Formalism.REAL.muG (X.B.I.ε v)) (X.B.σ v) (X.B.w v)
def ph : X.B.I.Ginf → ℂ := Formalism.L2.SG.ProjRows.phInf X.B.I X.B.σ X.B.w X.dv

def LevelSubgroupCharacter : Prop :=

def IsAdmIdem (μf : Measure I.Gf) (g g₀ : ℕ) (e : I.Gf → ℂ) : Prop :=
  IsLocallyConstant e ∧ I.convF μf e e = e ∧ (∀ x, e x⁻¹ = star (e x)) ∧ I.IsPosType e ∧
    (∀ x, e x ≠ 0 → x ∈ I.Kset) ∧ (∀ x, ∀ k ∈ I.Kg g, ∀ k' ∈ I.Kg g, e (k * x * k') = e x) ∧
    ∀ x, e x ≠ 0 → I.UnipMod g₀ x

def f : ℕ := Module.finrank ℚ I.F

def rd : ℝ := |(discr I.E : ℝ)| ^ ((1 : ℝ) / (2 * (I.f : ℝ)))

def pAt (v : InfinitePlace I.F) : ℕ := (univ.filter fun a => I.ε v a = true).card
def qAt (v : InfinitePlace I.F) : ℕ := (univ.filter fun a => I.ε v a = false).card

end AutInst

structure RowsAut : Type 1 where
  I : AutInst
  μf : Measure I.Gf
  [haar : μf.IsHaarMeasure]
  [sf : SigmaFinite μf]
  fund : Set I.GA
  fund_spec : I.IsFundDom (I.muA μf) fund
  lam : InfinitePlace I.F → Fin I.n → ℝ
  H : InfinitePlace I.F → Type
  [hN : ∀ v, NormedAddCommGroup (H v)]
  [hI : ∀ v, InnerProductSpace ℂ (H v)]
  [hC : ∀ v, CompleteSpace (H v)]
  σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)
  w : ∀ v, H v
  Hs : Type
  [sN : NormedAddCommGroup Hs]
  [sI : InnerProductSpace ℂ Hs]
  [sC : CompleteSpace Hs]
  σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)
  tens : (∀ v, H v) → Hs
  tens_spec : I.IsHilbTensor σ σs tens
  R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund)
  R_spec : I.IsRightReg μf fund R
  g : ℕ
  g₀ : ℕ
  e : I.Gf → ℂ
  e_adm : I.IsAdmIdem μf g g₀ e
  Re : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund
  Re_spec : I.IsRConvF μf fund R e Re

attribute [instance] RowsAut.haar RowsAut.sf RowsAut.hN RowsAut.hI RowsAut.hC RowsAut.sN RowsAut.sI RowsAut.sC

def RowsAut.D (B : RowsAut) : ℕ := B.I.Dcount B.σs B.μf B.fund B.R B.Re

end Formalism.MainNotions.S

abbrev wLf (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) :
    Submodule (IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E) (Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) :=
  lamAf (F := X.B.I.F) (wLat X w)

def IsRedBasis (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) (b : Fin D.R.r → (Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E)) :
    Prop :=
  (∀ i, b i ∈ X.B.I.L) ∧ ∀ y ∈ X.B.I.L, ∃ c : Fin D.R.r → ℤ, y - ∑ i, (c i : X.B.I.E) • b i ∈ wLat X w

def RedMat (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) (g : ℕ) (x : X.B.I.Gf)
    (M : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod g)) : Prop :=
  ∃ b, IsRedBasis X w b ∧ ∀ i, X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ incl X.B.I (b i) -
    ∑ j, (((M j i).val : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) • incl X.B.I (b j) ∈ wLf X w

def GoodAt (x : X.B.I.Gf) (γ : G0g D.nd) : Prop :=
  ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k),
    RedMat X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2) (D.host.g kv.1) x ((γ kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
      Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)))

def eg (x : X.B.I.Gf) : ℂ :=
  if h : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset ∧ ∃ γ : G0g D.nd, GoodAt X x γ then
    D.ntypes.e0.coeff (Classical.choose h.2) / ((X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal : ℂ)
  else 0

def RedExists : Prop := ∀ x ∈ X.B.I.Kset, ∃ γ : G0g D.nd, GoodAt X x γ

def EgWellDef : Prop :=
  ∀ x (γ γ' : G0g D.nd), GoodAt X x γ → GoodAt X x γ' → D.ntypes.e0.coeff γ = D.ntypes.e0.coeff γ'

def IdempotentRealFixed (D : D7) : Prop := ∀ γ : G0g D.nd, star (D.ntypes.e0.coeff γ) = D.ntypes.e0.coeff γ

structure GoodOp where
  Rg : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund
  Rg_spec : X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (eg X) Rg

end Good

section Rows

open Formalism.L2.SG.ProjRows

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

abbrev L2X : Type := X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund

abbrev muX : Measure X.B.I.GA := (X.B.I.muA X.B.μf).restrict X.B.fund

def goodSub (G : GoodOp X) : Submodule ℂ (L2X X) :=
  HHX X ⊓ LinearMap.ker ((G.Rg : L2X X →ₗ[ℂ] L2X X) - LinearMap.id)

def EEC (G : GoodOp X) : Submodule ℂ (L2X X) :=
  (goodSub X G).comap (conjL (muX X)).toLinearEquiv.toLinearMap

theorem mem_EEC (G : GoodOp X) (F : L2X X) : F ∈ EEC X G ↔ star F ∈ goodSub X G := Iff.rfl

def conjHH (G : GoodOp X) : EEC X G →ₗᵢ⋆[ℂ] HHX X where
  toFun F := ⟨star (F : L2X X), ((mem_EEC X G F).1 F.2).1⟩
  map_add' F F' := Subtype.ext (by
    show star ((F : L2X X) + F') = star (F : L2X X) + star (F' : L2X X)
    exact (conjL (muX X)).map_add _ _)
  map_smul' c F := Subtype.ext (by
    show star (c • (F : L2X X)) = (starRingEnd ℂ c) • star (F : L2X X)
    exact (conjL (muX X)).map_smulₛₗ c _)
  norm_map' F := by
    show ‖star (F : L2X X)‖ = ‖(F : L2X X)‖
    exact (conjL (muX X)).norm_map _

theorem conjHH_apply (G : GoodOp X) (F : EEC X G) : ((conjHH X G F : HHX X) : L2X X) = star (F : L2X X) := rfl

end Rows

def IsConjOnto {E W H : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [InnerProductSpace ℂ E] [NormedAddCommGroup W]
    [InnerProductSpace ℂ W] [NormedAddCommGroup H] [InnerProductSpace ℂ H] (s : E → H) (c : W →ₗᵢ⋆[ℂ] H) : Prop :=
  ∃ j : E ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] W, ∀ F, s F = c (j F)

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

def RowDictTie (G : GoodOp X) (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X) : Prop :=
  IsConjOnto (fun F : D.what₂.EE => ι (D.sEE F)) (conjHH X G)

theorem finiteDimensional_HHX (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X) : FiniteDimensional ℂ (HHX X) := LinearEquiv.finiteDimensional ι.toLinearEquiv

theorem dall_eq (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X) : D.what₂.Dall = Module.finrank ℂ (HHX X) := by
  rw [← D.s_HH]
  exact ι.toLinearEquiv.finrank_eq

theorem drows_eq {G : GoodOp X} {ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X} (h : RowDictTie X G ι) :
    D.what₂.Drows = Module.finrank ℂ (EEC X G) := by
  obtain ⟨j, -⟩ := h
  rw [← j.toLinearEquiv.finrank_eq, Module.finrank_eq_card_basis D.what₂.onb.toBasis, Fintype.card_fin]

theorem range_sEE {G : GoodOp X} {ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X} (h : RowDictTie X G ι) (y : HHX X) :
    (y : L2X X) ∈ goodSub X G ↔ ∃ F : D.what₂.EE, ι (D.sEE F) = y := by

  · intro hy
    have hmem : star (y : L2X X) ∈ EEC X G := by

variable (I : AutInst) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E))

def RedMatB (p : ℕ) {m : ℕ} (b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E) (x : I.Gf) (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)) : Prop :=
  ∀ i, I.fmat x *ᵥ iv I (b i) - ∑ j, (((M j i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) ∈ Lq I w

theorem redMat0_of {p m : ℕ} {b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hb : IsRedBasis0 I w b) {x : I.Gf}
    {M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)} (h : RedMatB I w p b x M) : RedMat0 I w p x M := ⟨b, hb, h⟩

theorem natSmul_Lq (k : ℕ) {z : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hz : z ∈ Lq I w) :
    (k : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • z ∈ Lq I w := by
  rw [Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul]; exact nsmul_mem hz k

theorem redMatB_exists {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) {m : ℕ}
    {b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hb : IsRedBasis0 I w b) {x : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset) :
    ∃ M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p), RedMatB I w p b x M := by
  have : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  choose c hc using fun i => coords I w hp hcard hb (K_mv I hx (DictLat.iv_mem I (hb.1 i)))
  refine ⟨Matrix.of fun j i => ((c i j : ℤ) : ZMod p), fun i => ?_⟩
  have h2 : ∑ j, ((((c i j : ℤ) : ZMod p).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) -
      ∑ j, (c i j : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) ∈ Lq I w := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    exact sum_mem fun j _ => val_sub I w hp hcard (hb.1 j) (c i j)
  have := sub_mem (hc i) h2
  convert this using 1
  simp only [Matrix.of_apply]
  abel

theorem mkQ_natSmul (k : ℕ) (v : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) :
    (Lq I w).mkQ ((k : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • v) = ((k : ℤ)) • (Lq I w).mkQ v := by
  rw [Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul, map_nsmul, natCast_zsmul]

theorem redMatB_unique {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) {m : ℕ} (hm : m = I.n)
    {b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hb : IsRedBasis0 I w b) {x : I.Gf} {M M' : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)}
    (h : RedMatB I w p b x M) (h' : RedMatB I w p b x M') : M = M' := by
  subst hm
  have : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  ext j i
  have hdiff : ∑ l, (((M' l i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b l) -
      ∑ l, (((M l i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b l) ∈ Lq I w := by
    have := sub_mem (h i) (h' i)
    convert this using 1
    abel
  have hz : ∑ l, ((((M' l i).val : ℕ) : ℤ) - (((M l i).val : ℕ) : ℤ)) • (Lq I w).mkQ (iv I (b l)) = 0 := by
    have h0 : (Lq I w).mkQ (∑ l, (((M' l i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b l) -
        ∑ l, (((M l i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b l)) = 0 :=
      (Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero _).2 hdiff
    rw [map_sub, map_sum, map_sum] at h0
    simp only [mkQ_natSmul] at h0
    rw [← h0, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun l _ => sub_smul _ _ _
  have hj := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedConj0Pf.zcoef_A I w p hp hcard hb _ hz j
  have hj' : ((M' j i).val : ZMod p) - ((M j i).val : ZMod p) = 0 := by exact_mod_cast hj
  rw [ZMod.natCast_zmod_val, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val, sub_eq_zero] at hj'
  exact hj'.symm

theorem redMatB_mul {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) {m : ℕ}
    {b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hb : ∀ i, b i ∈ I.L) {x y : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset)
    {M N : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)} (hM : RedMatB I w p b x M) (hN : RedMatB I w p b y N) :
    RedMatB I w p b (x * y) (M * N) := by
  have : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  intro i
  have hAB : I.fmat (x * y) = I.fmat x * I.fmat y := rfl
  have s1 : I.fmat x *ᵥ (I.fmat y *ᵥ iv I (b i)) -
      ∑ k, (((N k i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • (I.fmat x *ᵥ iv I (b k)) ∈ Lq I w := by
    have := stab_w I w hx (hN i)
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_sub, Matrix.mulVec_sum] at this
    simpa only [Matrix.mulVec_smul] using this
  have s2 : ∑ k, (((N k i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • (I.fmat x *ᵥ iv I (b k)) -
      ∑ k, (((N k i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) •
        ∑ j, (((M j k).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) ∈ Lq I w := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    refine sum_mem fun k _ => ?_
    rw [← smul_sub]
    exact natSmul_Lq I w _ (hM k)
  have e3 : ∑ k, (((N k i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) •
        ∑ j, (((M j k).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) =
      ∑ j, (((∑ k, (M j k).val * (N k i).val : ℕ) : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) := by
    simp only [Finset.smul_sum, smul_smul]
    rw [Finset.sum_comm]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [Int.cast_natCast, Nat.cast_sum, Finset.sum_smul]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
    rw [Nat.cast_mul, mul_comm]
  have s3 : ∑ j, (((∑ k, (M j k).val * (N k i).val : ℕ) : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) -
      ∑ j, ((((M * N) j i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) ∈ Lq I w := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    refine sum_mem fun j _ => ?_
    have hc : (((∑ k, (M j k).val * (N k i).val : ℕ) : ℤ) : ZMod p) = (M * N) j i := by
      rw [Int.cast_natCast, Nat.cast_sum, Matrix.mul_apply]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
      rw [Nat.cast_mul, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val, ZMod.natCast_zmod_val]
    have := val_sub I w hp hcard (hb j) ((∑ k, (M j k).val * (N k i).val : ℕ) : ℤ)
    rw [hc] at this
    have := neg_mem this
    rwa [neg_sub] at this
  rw [e3] at s2
  have := add_mem (add_mem s1 s2) s3
  rw [hAB, ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec]
  convert this using 1
  abel

theorem redMatB_one {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) {m : ℕ} (b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E) :
    RedMatB I w p b 1 (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)) := by
  have : Fact (1 < p) := ⟨hp.one_lt⟩
  intro i
  have e : ∑ j, ((((1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)) j i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) =
      iv I (b i) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
    · simp only [Matrix.one_apply_eq, ZMod.val_one, Nat.cast_one, one_smul]
    · intro j _ hj; simp [Matrix.one_apply_ne hj]
    · simp
  rw [e, fmat_one, Matrix.one_mulVec, sub_self]
  exact zero_mem _

structure RedB (I : AutInst) (m : ℕ) where
  w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)
  p : ℕ
  hp : p.Prime
  hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p
  hm : m = I.n
  b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E
  hb : IsRedBasis0 I w b

variable {I} {m : ℕ}

open Classical in

def matB (R : RedB I m) (x : I.Gf) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p) :=
  if hx : x ∈ I.Kset then Classical.choose (redMatB_exists I R.w R.hp R.hcard R.hb hx) else 0

theorem matB_spec (R : RedB I m) {x : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset) : RedMatB I R.w R.p R.b x (matB R x) := by
  unfold matB; rw [dif_pos hx]; exact Classical.choose_spec (redMatB_exists I R.w R.hp R.hcard R.hb hx)

theorem matB_eq (R : RedB I m) {x : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset) {M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)}
    (h : RedMatB I R.w R.p R.b x M) : matB R x = M :=
  redMatB_unique I R.w R.hp R.hcard R.hm R.hb (matB_spec R hx) h

theorem matB_mul (R : RedB I m) {x y : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset) (hy : y ∈ I.Kset) :
    matB R (x * y) = matB R x * matB R y :=
  matB_eq R (K_mul I hx hy) (redMatB_mul I R.w R.hp R.hcard R.hb.1 hx (matB_spec R hx) (matB_spec R hy))

theorem matB_one (R : RedB I m) : matB R 1 = 1 := matB_eq R (K_one I) (redMatB_one I R.w R.hp R.b)

def rhoB (R : RedB I m) : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.KSub I →* GL (Fin m) (ZMod R.p) :=
  MonoidHom.toHomUnits
    { toFun := fun x => matB R x
      map_one' := matB_one R
      map_mul' := fun x y => matB_mul R x.2 y.2 }

theorem rhoB_val (R : RedB I m) (x : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.KSub I) :
    ((rhoB R x : GL (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) = matB R x := rfl

theorem rhoB_spec (R : RedB I m) (x : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.KSub I) :
    RedMatB I R.w R.p R.b x ((rhoB R x : GL (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) :=
  matB_spec R x.2

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRho


[ARO_reduction_02] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/AllRowsRhoX.lean:1-140

import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.AllRowsRho
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.AllRowsRedKer

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRhoX

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting Formalism.L2.SG.RowDict Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf PIW.AdelClassFin
  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKerA Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRho
  Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf Formalism.MainNotions.ArithmeticCylinders

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem exists_b (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) :
    ∃ b : Fin D.R.r → Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E, IsRedBasis0 X.B.I (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)) b := by
  rw [← X.n_eq]
  exact exists_redBasis (I := X.B.I) (w := wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)) (hp := D.host.g_prime kv.1)
    (hcard := by rw [card_wB, card_res_host])

def redBX (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : RedB X.B.I D.R.r where
  w := wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)
  p := D.host.g kv.1
  hp := D.host.g_prime kv.1
  hcard := by rw [card_wB, card_res_host]
  hm := X.n_eq.symm
  b := Classical.choose (exists_b X kv)
  hb := Classical.choose_spec (exists_b X kv)

def rhoX : KSub X.B.I →* G0g D.nd := MonoidHom.pi fun kv => rhoB (redBX X kv)

theorem rhoX_apply (x : KSub X.B.I) (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : rhoX X x kv = rhoB (redBX X kv) x := rfl

theorem goodAt_rhoX (x : KSub X.B.I) : GoodAt X x (rhoX X x) := by
  intro kv
  exact (redMat_iff X _ _ _ _).mpr (redMat0_of (I := X.B.I) (hb := (redBX X kv).hb) (h := rhoB_spec (redBX X kv) x))

theorem level_mem_wB (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) :
    ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal := by
  have hc : Nat.card (𝓞 X.B.I.E ⧸ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal) = D.host.g kv.1 :=
    (redBX X kv).hcard
  have h0 : ((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E ⧸ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal) = 0 := by
    have h1 := card_nsmul_eq_zero' (G := 𝓞 X.B.I.E ⧸ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal) (x := 1)
    rw [hc] at h1
    simpa using h1
  have hk : ((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal := by
    rw [← Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem, map_natCast]
    exact h0
  have key : ∀ k : Fin 2, ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal →
      ((D.host.g 0 * D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wB X (D.naut.plGw kv.1 kv.2)).asIdeal := by
    intro k hk'
    fin_cases k
    · simpa [Nat.cast_mul] using Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ hk'
    · simpa [Nat.cast_mul] using Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hk'
  rw [X.g_eq]
  exact key kv.1 hk

theorem redMatB_one_of {I : AutInst} {m : ℕ} (R : RedB I m) {x : I.Gf}
    (h : ∀ i, I.fmat x *ᵥ iv I (R.b i) - iv I (R.b i) ∈ Lq I R.w) :
    RedMatB I R.w R.p R.b x (1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) := by
  haveI : Fact (1 < R.p) := ⟨R.hp.one_lt⟩
  intro i
  have hsum : ∑ j, ((((1 : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod R.p)) j i).val : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) •
      iv I (R.b j) = iv I (R.b i) := by
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
    · simp [Matrix.one_apply_eq, ZMod.val_one]
    · intro j _ hj
      simp [Matrix.one_apply_ne hj]
    · intro hi
      exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ i) hi
  rw [hsum]
  exact h i

theorem rhoX_eq_one_iff (x : KSub X.B.I) : rhoX X x = 1 ↔ (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g := by
  have hRK : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.RedKer X :=
    Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.redKer_of (X := X) (Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.redKerOne_of (X := X)
      (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 0) (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 1))
  constructor
  · intro h
    have h1 := goodAt_rhoX X x
    rw [h] at h1
    exact (hRK x x.2).1 h1
  · intro hx
    have hK : (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kset := x.2
    have hG := Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.goodAt_one_of_mem_Kg (X := X) hx
    funext kv
    rw [rhoX_apply]
    apply Units.ext
    have hR0 := (redMat_iff X _ _ _ _).mp (hG kv)
    have hR1 := hR0
    simp only [Pi.one_apply, Units.val_one] at hR1
    have hA := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKerA.stepA (I := X.B.I) (redBX X kv).hp (redBX X kv).hcard hK hR1
    have hB : RedMatB X.B.I (redBX X kv).w (redBX X kv).p (redBX X kv).b x 1 :=
      redMatB_one_of (redBX X kv) fun i => by
        have h1 := hA _ (PIW.AdelClassFin.DictLat.iv_mem X.B.I ((redBX X kv).hb.1 i))
        rwa [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec] at h1
    have h2 := matB_eq (redBX X kv) hK hB
    show ((rhoB (redBX X kv) x : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (redBX X kv).p)) :
        Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (redBX X kv).p)) =
      ((1 : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (redBX X kv).p)) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (redBX X kv).p))
    rw [Units.val_one]
    exact h2

theorem ES_conj (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (γ P : G0g D.nd) : (ES S).coeff (P⁻¹ * γ * P) = (ES S).coeff γ := by
  unfold ES
  simp only [Finsupp.coe_equivFunOnFinite_symm]
  refine finsum_congr fun a => ?_
  split_ifs
  · refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
    exact coeff_conj_of_central (D.ntypes.eSer_central kv.1 _) (γ kv) (P kv)
  · rfl

theorem egA_rhoX (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd)) (ha : ∀ γ P : G0g D.nd, a.coeff (P⁻¹ * γ * P) = a.coeff γ)
    (x : KSub X.B.I) : egA X a x = a.coeff (rhoX X x) / ((X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal : ℂ) := by
  have h : (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kset ∧ ∃ γ : G0g D.nd, GoodAt X x γ :=
    ⟨x.2, rhoX X x, goodAt_rhoX X x⟩
  unfold egA
  rw [dif_pos h]
  congr 1
  have hγ := Classical.choose_spec h.2
  have hP : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), ∃ P : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)),
      (Classical.choose h.2) kv = P⁻¹ * rhoX X x kv * P := by
    intro kv
    have h1 := (redMat_iff X _ _ _ _).mp (goodAt_rhoX X x kv)
    have h2 := (redMat_iff X _ _ _ _).mp (hγ kv)
    exact Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedConj0Pf.reduction_conjugacy X.B.I _ _ (redBX X kv).hp (redBX X kv).hcard D.R.r
      (redBX X kv).hm x _ _ h1 h2
  choose P hP using hP
  have e1 : Classical.choose h.2 = P⁻¹ * rhoX X x * P := by
    funext kv
    rw [hP kv]
    rfl
  rw [e1, ha]

def RhoSurj : Prop := ∀ (D : D7) (X : HidaSetting D), Function.Surjective (rhoX X)

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRhoX


[ARO_reduction_03] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/AllRowsRedKer.lean:1-165

import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.AllRowsOf

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting Formalism.L2.SG.RowDict Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKerA
  Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma

variable {D : D7}

def sigH (D : D7) : 𝓞 D.host.E ≃+* 𝓞 D.host.E := RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv D.host.conj.toRingEquiv

theorem sigH_coe (a : 𝓞 D.host.E) : ((sigH D a : 𝓞 D.host.E) : D.host.E) = D.host.conj (a : D.host.E) := rfl

def conjH (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E) where
  asIdeal := w.asIdeal.comap (sigH D)
  isPrime := by have := w.isPrime; exact Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _
  ne_bot := by
    intro h
    apply w.ne_bot
    have := Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective _ (sigH D).surjective w.asIdeal
    rw [← this, h, Ideal.map_bot]

theorem conj_conj (X : HidaSetting D) (y : D.host.E) : D.host.conj (D.host.conj y) = y := by
  obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := X.ιE.surjective y
  rw [← X.ιE_conj, ← X.ιE_conj, star_star]

theorem sigH_sigH (X : HidaSetting D) (a : 𝓞 D.host.E) : sigH D (sigH D a) = a := by
  apply RingOfIntegers.ext
  rw [sigH_coe, sigH_coe]
  exact conj_conj X _

theorem conjH_conjH (X : HidaSetting D) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : conjH (conjH w) = w := by
  apply HeightOneSpectrum.ext
  show (w.asIdeal.comap (sigH D)).comap (sigH D) = w.asIdeal
  ext a
  simp only [Ideal.mem_comap]
  rw [sigH_sigH X]

theorem isConjPl_conjH (X : HidaSetting D) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : IsConjPl D.host w (conjH w) := by
  intro x y hxy
  have hs : sigH D y = x := by
    apply RingOfIntegers.ext
    rw [sigH_coe, hxy, conj_conj X]
  show x ∈ w.asIdeal ↔ sigH D y ∈ w.asIdeal
  rw [hs]

theorem natCast_mem_conjH {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)} {p : ℕ} (h : (p : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) :
    (p : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ (conjH w).asIdeal := by
  show sigH D (p : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ w.asIdeal
  rw [map_natCast]
  exact h

theorem wB_conjH (X : HidaSetting D) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : wB X (conjH w) = wc X.B.I (wB X w) := by
  apply HeightOneSpectrum.ext
  ext a
  rw [mem_wc]
  simp only [wB, conjH, Ideal.mem_comap]
  have hc : sigH D (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE a) =
      RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE (sigO X.B.I a) := by
    apply RingOfIntegers.ext
    rw [sigH_coe]
    simp [RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv, sigO_coe, X.ιE_conj]
  rw [hc]

theorem finrank_cmField (D : D7) : Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E = 2 * D.R.f := by
  haveI := D.host.galois
  rw [← IsGalois.card_aut_eq_finrank, Nat.card_congr D.host.gal.toEquiv, Formalism.HC.Frame.natCard_GG]
  simp only [Formalism.BasicNotions.Ranks.f, Formalism.BasicNotions.Ranks.n]
  ring

theorem cover (X : HidaSetting D) (k : Fin 2) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E))
    (hw : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) :
    ∃ v, w = D.naut.plGw k v ∨ w = conjH (D.naut.plGw k v) := by
  have hp := D.host.g_prime k
  set A : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) :=
    Set.range (D.naut.plGw k) ∪ Set.range (fun v => conjH (D.naut.plGw k v)) with hA
  set T : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) := placesOver D.host.E (D.host.g k) with hT
  have hAT : A ⊆ T := by
    rintro _ (⟨v, rfl⟩ | ⟨v, rfl⟩)
    · exact D.naut.plGw_over k v
    · exact natCast_mem_conjH (D.naut.plGw_over k v)
  have hTfin : T.Finite := finite_plOver D.host.E hp
  have hinj2 : Function.Injective (fun v => conjH (D.naut.plGw k v)) := by
    intro v v' h
    apply D.naut.plGw_inj k
    have := congrArg conjH h
    simpa only [conjH_conjH X] using this
  have hdisj : Disjoint (Set.range (D.naut.plGw k)) (Set.range (fun v => conjH (D.naut.plGw k v))) := by
    rw [Set.disjoint_left]
    rintro _ ⟨v, rfl⟩ ⟨v', hv'⟩
    apply D.naut.plGw_nconj k v' v
    have := isConjPl_conjH X (D.naut.plGw k v')
    have hv'' : conjH (D.naut.plGw k v') = D.naut.plGw k v := hv'
    rw [hv''] at this
    exact this
  have hcA : A.ncard = 2 * D.R.f := by
    rw [hA, Set.ncard_union_eq hdisj (Set.finite_range _) (Set.finite_range _),
      Set.ncard_range_of_injective (D.naut.plGw_inj k), Set.ncard_range_of_injective hinj2,
      Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, D.nd.card_PlG k]
    ring
  have hcT : T.ncard = 2 * D.R.f := by rw [hT, D.nd.g_split k, finrank_cmField]
  have hEq : A = T := Set.eq_of_subset_of_ncard_le hAT (by rw [hcA, hcT]) hTfin
  have : w ∈ A := by rw [hEq]; exact hw
  rcases this with ⟨v, rfl⟩ | ⟨v, rfl⟩
  · exact ⟨v, Or.inl rfl⟩
  · exact ⟨v, Or.inr rfl⟩

theorem unram (X : HidaSetting D) (k : Fin 2) (P : Ideal (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) (hPm : P.IsMaximal)
    (hgP : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P) : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∉ P ^ 2 := by
  have hp := D.host.g_prime k
  have := span_isMaximal hp
  have hspl := splitsCompl_of_ncard D.host.E (D.host.g k) hp (by
    rw [← IsDedekindDomain.coe_primesOverFinset (span_ne_bot hp) (𝓞 D.host.E), ← image_plOver _ hp,
      (injOn_asIdeal _ _).ncard_image]
    exact D.nd.g_split k)
  set e : 𝓞 X.B.I.E ≃+* 𝓞 D.host.E := RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE
  have hQm : (P.map e).IsMaximal := Ideal.map_isMaximal_of_equiv e
  have hgQ : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ P.map e := by
    have := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem e hgP
    rwa [map_natCast] at this
  obtain ⟨-, hsq⟩ := hspl _ hQm hgQ
  intro h
  apply hsq
  have := Ideal.mem_map_of_mem e h
  rwa [map_natCast, Ideal.map_pow] at this

def toH (X : HidaSetting D) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E) where
  asIdeal := w.asIdeal.comap (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).symm
  isPrime := by have := w.isPrime; exact Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _
  ne_bot := by
    intro h
    apply w.ne_bot
    have := Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective _ (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).symm.surjective w.asIdeal
    rw [← this, h, Ideal.map_bot]

theorem wB_toH (X : HidaSetting D) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) : wB X (toH X w) = w := by
  apply HeightOneSpectrum.ext
  ext a
  simp [wB, toH, Ideal.mem_comap]

theorem redKerP_holds (X : HidaSetting D) (k : Fin 2) : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.RedKerP X k := by
  intro x hx hred
  have hp := D.host.g_prime k
  have hA : ∀ v, ∀ y ∈ X.B.I.Lf, (X.B.I.fmat x - 1) *ᵥ y ∈ Lq X.B.I (wB X (D.naut.plGw k v)) := by
    intro v
    have h0 := (redMat_iff X _ _ x _).1 (hred v)
    exact stepA X.B.I hp (by rw [card_wB, card_res_host]) hx h0
  refine glue X.B.I hp (unram X k) hx fun w hw => ?_
  have hwH : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ (toH X w).asIdeal := by
    show (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).symm ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ w.asIdeal
    rw [map_natCast]; exact hw
  obtain ⟨v, hv | hv⟩ := cover X k (toH X w) hwH
  · rw [← wB_toH X w, hv]
    exact hA v
  · rw [← wB_toH X w, hv, wB_conjH X]
    exact stepB X.B.I hx (hA v)

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer

theorem vecV_sub (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.F))
    (a b : Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Formalism.MainNotions.S.Af X.B.I.F) :
    PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I v (a - b) = PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I v a - PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I v b := by
  funext i
  simp [PI.FinKW.vecV, map_sub]

theorem vecV_nsmul (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.F)) (c : ℕ)
    (a : Fin X.B.I.n → X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Formalism.MainNotions.S.Af X.B.I.F) :
    PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I v (c • a) = c • PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I v a := by
  funext i
  simp [PI.FinKW.vecV]

theorem exists_cofactor (p : TypX X) : ∃ c : ℕ, (X.B.g : ℕ) = gOf p * c := by
  rw [X.g_eq]
  unfold gOf
  split_ifs
  · exact ⟨_, rfl⟩
  · exact ⟨D.host.g 0, mul_comm _ _⟩

theorem piV_mem_KgX (x : X.B.I.Gf) (hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g) (p : TypX X) :
    piV X.B.I p.1 (tf X.B.I hb x) ∈ MassDataIdxSteps.KgX X p := by
  have hKf := (Bridge_mem_Kf_iff X.B.I hb x).2 hx.1
  rw [Kf, Subgroup.mem_iInf] at hKf
  refine ⟨hKf p.1, ?_⟩
  intro y hy
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := exists_cofactor X p
  obtain ⟨u, huv, huw⟩ := PI.FinKW.local_vector_lift X.B.I p.1 y
  have huL : u ∈ X.B.I.Lf := PI.FinKW.memLocFrom_of PI.FinKW.local_vector_injective X.B.I u fun w => by
    by_cases hw : w = p.1
    · subst hw
      rw [huv]
      exact hy
    · rw [huw w hw]
      exact zero_mem _
  obtain ⟨z, hzL, hz⟩ := hx.2 u huL
  have hfx : X.B.I.fmat x = ofHAFin X.B.I (toHA X.B.I hb (1, x)) := (toHA_isBridge X.B.I hb (1, x)).2
  refine ⟨c • PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I p.1 z, nsmul_mem (PI.FinKW.LocTo.mem_local_lattice_iff X.B.I z hzL p.1) c, ?_⟩
  have e := congrArg (PI.FinKW.vecV X.B.I p.1) hz
  rw [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec, vecV_sub, hfx, PI.FinKW.vecV_mulVec, huv, Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul,
    vecV_nsmul] at e
  rw [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec, Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul, smul_smul, ← hc]
  exact e

end Loc

theorem card_target :
    Nat.card (∀ p : TypX X, GL (Formalism.FourierDecayAlt.S3.Idx D.R.rS D.R.k) (ZMod (gOf p))) = Nat.card (G0g D.nd) := by
  classical
  have hT : (Nat.card (∀ p : TypX X, GL (Formalism.FourierDecayAlt.S3.Idx D.R.rS D.R.k) (ZMod (gOf p))) : ℝ) =
      (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f *
      (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f := by
    rw [Nat.card_pi, Nat.cast_prod]
    rw [← Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.DstarIdxPf.prod_typ X (fun g => (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod g)) : ℝ))]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun p _ => ?_
    rw [← Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.DstarIdxPf.ggC_eq D.R (gOf p)]
    rfl
  have hG : (Nat.card (G0g D.nd) : ℝ) =
      (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f *
      (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f := by
    rw [Nat.card_pi, Nat.cast_prod, Fintype.prod_sigma, Fin.prod_univ_two]
    have e : ∀ k : Fin 2, ∏ _v : D.nd.PlG k, (Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k))) : ℝ) =
        (Nat.card (GL (Fin (D.R.k + D.R.m - 1)) (ZMod (D.host.g k))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f := fun k => by
      rw [Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, D.nd.card_PlG]
      rfl
    exact congrArg₂ (· * ·) (e 0) (e 1)
  exact_mod_cast hT.trans hG.symm

end Count

theorem reduction_map_surjective : RhoSurj := by
  intro D X
  classical
  let hb : AdBridgeIso X.B.I := (Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.adelic_isomorphism_statement X.B.I).1
  have hK : ∀ p : TypX X, ((KvS X p : Subgroup _) : Set (MassDataIdxSteps.HAp X p)) = MassDataIdxSteps.KvX X p := by
    intro p
    obtain ⟨K, hKs, -, -, -⟩ := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.MassDataX.stK_holds X p
    have e : KvS X p = K := by
      have e : (K : Set _) = Kv X.B.I.cm.sig X.B.I.J (lamS X) p.1 := hKs
      rw [KvS, ← e, Subgroup.closure_eq]
    rw [e]
    exact hKs
  choose ρ hρ hker using fun p : TypX X => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.MassDataX.stRed_holds X p (KvS X p) (hK p)
  let ψ : KsetSub X.B.I →* ∀ p : TypX X, GL (Formalism.FourierDecayAlt.S3.Idx D.R.rS D.R.k) (ZMod (gOf p)) :=
    (MonoidHom.pi fun p => (ρ p).comp (Pi.evalMonoidHom (fun p => KvS X p) p)).comp (phiK X hb)
  have hψ : Function.Surjective ψ := by
    intro t
    choose a ha using fun p => hρ p (t p)
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := phiK_surjective X hb single_component_surjective a
    refine ⟨k, funext fun p => ?_⟩
    show ρ p (phiK X hb k p) = t p
    rw [hk]
    exact ha p
  let ι : KSub X.B.I → KsetSub X.B.I := fun x => ⟨x.1, x.2⟩
  have hψι : ∀ x y : KSub X.B.I, rhoX X x = rhoX X y → ψ (ι x) = ψ (ι y) := by
    intro x y hxy
    have h1 : rhoX X (x⁻¹ * y) = 1 := by rw [map_mul, map_inv, hxy, inv_mul_cancel]
    have hg := (rhoX_eq_one_iff X _).1 h1
    have h2 : ψ (ι (x⁻¹ * y)) = 1 := by
      funext p
      show ρ p (phiK X hb (ι (x⁻¹ * y)) p) = 1
      exact (hker p _).2 (RhoSurjW.piV_mem_KgX X hb _ hg p)
    have h3 : ι (x⁻¹ * y) = (ι x)⁻¹ * ι y := rfl
    rw [h3, map_mul, map_inv, inv_mul_eq_one] at h2
    exact h2
  have : ∀ k : Fin 2, NeZero (D.host.g k) := fun k => ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  let f : (rhoX X).range → (∀ p : TypX X, GL (Formalism.FourierDecayAlt.S3.Idx D.R.rS D.R.k) (ZMod (gOf p))) :=
    fun r => ψ (ι (MonoidHom.mem_range.1 r.2).choose)
  have hf : Function.Surjective f := by
    intro t
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hψ t
    let x : KSub X.B.I := ⟨k.1, k.2⟩
    let r : (rhoX X).range := ⟨rhoX X x, x, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨r, (hψι _ x (MonoidHom.mem_range.1 r.2).choose_spec).trans hk⟩
  have hle : Nat.card (G0g D.nd) ≤ Nat.card (rhoX X).range := by
    rw [← RhoSurjW.card_target X]
    exact Nat.card_le_card_of_surjective f hf
  exact MonoidHom.range_eq_top.1 (Subgroup.eq_top_of_le_card _ hle)

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RhoSurjW

def wB (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E) where
  asIdeal := w.asIdeal.comap (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE)
  isPrime := by have := w.isPrime; exact Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _
  ne_bot := by
    intro h
    apply w.ne_bot
    have := Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective _ (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).surjective w.asIdeal
    rw [← this, h, Ideal.map_bot]

theorem wLat_eq (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) : wLat X w = wLat0 X.B.I (wB X w) := by
  unfold wLat wLat0
  congr 1
  ext z
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨a, ⟨b, hb, hab⟩, y, hy, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨a, ?_, y, hy, rfl⟩
    show RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE a ∈ w.asIdeal
    have : RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE a = b := RingOfIntegers.ext (by simpa using hab)
    rw [this]
    exact hb
  · rintro ⟨a, ha, y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨a, ⟨RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE a, ha, by simp⟩, y, hy, rfl⟩

theorem card_res_host (k : Fin 2) (v : D.nd.PlG k) :
    Nat.card (𝓞 D.host.E ⧸ (D.naut.plGw k v).asIdeal) = D.host.g k := by
  have hp := D.host.g_prime k
  have := span_isMaximal hp
  have hspl : SplitsCompl D.host.E (D.host.g k) := by
    apply splitsCompl_of_ncard _ _ hp
    rw [← IsDedekindDomain.coe_primesOverFinset (span_ne_bot hp) (𝓞 D.host.E), ← image_plOver _ hp,
      (injOn_asIdeal _ _).ncard_image]
    exact D.nd.g_split k
  obtain ⟨hN, -⟩ := hspl _ ((D.naut.plGw k v).isPrime.isMaximal (D.naut.plGw k v).ne_bot) (D.naut.plGw_over k v)
  rw [← hN, Ideal.absNorm_apply, Submodule.cardQuot_apply]

theorem card_wB (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) :
    Nat.card (𝓞 X.B.I.E ⧸ (wB X w).asIdeal) = Nat.card (𝓞 D.host.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) :=
  Nat.card_congr (Ideal.quotientEquiv (wB X w).asIdeal w.asIdeal (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE)
    (Ideal.map_comap_of_surjective _ (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).surjective w.asIdeal).symm).toEquiv

theorem redMat_iff (w : IsDedekindDomain.HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)) (p : ℕ) (x : X.B.I.Gf)
    (M : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)) : RedMat X w p x M ↔ RedMat0 X.B.I (wB X w) p x M := by
  unfold RedMat RedMat0 IsRedBasis IsRedBasis0 wLf
  rw [wLat_eq X w]
  exact Iff.rfl



[ARO_haar_inflation_01] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/AllRowsConv.lean:1-92
Range SHA256 2c507efe94bc486d45de6275d77da61f20a1f13c51d0455cce14cb67b5867575
import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.AllRowsOf

noncomputable section

open MeasureTheory

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsConv

open Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence

variable (I : AutInst)

def coset (m : ℕ) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) : Set I.Gf := {y | ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * y ∈ I.Kg m}

theorem coset_sub (m : ℕ) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) : coset I m q ⊆ I.Kset := by
  intro y hy
  have h1 : ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf) * (((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * y) ∈ I.Kset :=
    K_mul I (q.out : KSub I).2 hy.1
  simpa using h1

theorem mem_coset_iff (m : ℕ) {y : I.Gf} (hy : y ∈ I.Kset) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) :
    y ∈ coset I m q ↔ q = (QuotientGroup.mk (⟨y, hy⟩ : KSub I) : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    rw [← QuotientGroup.out_eq' q]
    exact QuotientGroup.eq.2 h
  · intro h
    subst h
    have := QuotientGroup.eq.1 (QuotientGroup.out_eq' (QuotientGroup.mk (⟨y, hy⟩ : KSub I) : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m))
    exact this

theorem isOpen_coset (m : ℕ) (hm : 1 ≤ m) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) : IsOpen (coset I m q) :=
  (I.topf_open m hm).preimage (continuous_const.mul continuous_id)

theorem measure_coset (μf : Measure I.Gf) [μf.IsMulLeftInvariant] (m : ℕ) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) :
    μf (coset I m q) = μf (I.Kg m) :=
  measure_preimage_mul μf _ (I.Kg m)

theorem term_eq {m : ℕ} {f₁ f₂ : I.Gf → ℂ} (h1N : ∀ y, ∀ n ∈ I.Kg m, f₁ (y * n) = f₁ y)
    (h2N : ∀ z, ∀ n ∈ I.Kg m, f₂ (n * z) = f₂ z) (x : I.Gf) (q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m) {y : I.Gf}
    (hyq : y ∈ coset I m q) :
    f₁ y * f₂ (y⁻¹ * x) = f₁ ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf) * f₂ (((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * x) := by
  have hn : ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * y ∈ I.Kg m := hyq
  generalize ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf) = c at hn ⊢
  have e1 : f₁ y = f₁ c := by
    have := h1N c (c⁻¹ * y) hn
    rwa [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this
  have e2 : f₂ (y⁻¹ * x) = f₂ (c⁻¹ * x) := by
    have := h2N (y⁻¹ * x) (c⁻¹ * y) hn
    rw [← this]
    congr 1
    group
  rw [e1, e2]

theorem convF_fin (μf : Measure I.Gf) [μf.IsMulLeftInvariant] {m : ℕ} (hm : 1 ≤ m) (hfin : μf (I.Kg m) < ⊤)
    [Fintype (KSub I ⧸ KgN I m)] {f₁ f₂ : I.Gf → ℂ} (h1K : ∀ y, f₁ y ≠ 0 → y ∈ I.Kset)
    (h1N : ∀ y, ∀ n ∈ I.Kg m, f₁ (y * n) = f₁ y) (h2N : ∀ z, ∀ n ∈ I.Kg m, f₂ (n * z) = f₂ z) (x : I.Gf) :
    I.convF μf f₁ f₂ x = (μf (I.Kg m)).toReal *
      ∑ q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m, f₁ ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf) * f₂ (((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * x) := by
  classical
  have key : (fun y => f₁ y * f₂ (y⁻¹ * x)) = fun y => ∑ q : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m,
      Set.indicator (coset I m q)
        (fun _ => f₁ ((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf) * f₂ (((q.out : KSub I) : I.Gf)⁻¹ * x)) y := by
    funext y
    by_cases hy : y ∈ I.Kset
    · rw [Finset.sum_eq_single (QuotientGroup.mk (⟨y, hy⟩ : KSub I) : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m)]
      · have hmem := (mem_coset_iff I m hy (QuotientGroup.mk (⟨y, hy⟩ : KSub I) : KSub I ⧸ KgN I m)).2 rfl
        rw [Set.indicator_of_mem hmem]
        exact term_eq I h1N h2N x _ hmem
      · intro q _ hq
        rw [Set.indicator_of_notMem]
        intro hmem
        exact hq ((mem_coset_iff I m hy q).1 hmem)
      · intro h; exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h
    · have h0 : f₁ y = 0 := by by_contra h; exact hy (h1K y h)
      rw [h0, zero_mul]
      symm
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun q _ => Set.indicator_of_notMem (fun hmem => hy (coset_sub I m q hmem)) _
  unfold AutInst.convF
  rw [key, integral_finsetSum]
  · rw [Finset.mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun q _ => ?_
    rw [integral_indicator_const _ (isOpen_coset I m hm q).measurableSet, measureReal_def, measure_coset,
      Complex.real_smul]
  · intro q _
    refine (integrable_indicator_iff (isOpen_coset I m hm q).measurableSet).2 ?_
    refine integrableOn_const ?_
    rw [measure_coset]
    exact hfin.ne

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsConv


[ARO_haar_inflation_02] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/AllRowsFin.lean:1-197

import Mathlib

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsFin

  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRhoX Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsConv

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

def V : ℝ := (X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal

theorem g_pos : 1 ≤ X.B.g := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.2 (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.level_ne_zero X)

theorem Kg_fin : X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g) < ⊤ := by
  have hK : IsCompact X.B.I.Kset := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.AutKCompact_holds X.B.I
  exact lt_of_le_of_lt (measure_mono fun x hx => hx.1) hK.measure_lt_top

theorem V_pos : 0 < V X := by
  have hpos : 0 < X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g) :=
    (X.B.I.topf_open X.B.g (g_pos X)).measure_pos X.B.μf ⟨1, Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.one_mem_Kg X⟩
  exact ENNReal.toReal_pos hpos.ne' (Kg_fin X).ne

theorem V_ne : (V X : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Complex.ofReal_ne_zero.2 (V_pos X).ne'

def BiInv (f : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ) : Prop :=
  (∀ x, f x ≠ 0 → x ∈ X.B.I.Kset) ∧ (∀ x, ∀ n ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g, f (x * n) = f x) ∧
    (∀ x, ∀ n ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g, f (n * x) = f x)

theorem biInv_e : BiInv X X.B.e := by
  refine ⟨X.B.e_adm.2.2.2.2.1, fun x n hn => ?_, fun x n hn => ?_⟩
  · have := X.B.e_adm.2.2.2.2.2.1 x 1 (Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.one_mem_Kg X) n hn
    rwa [one_mul] at this
  · have := X.B.e_adm.2.2.2.2.2.1 x n hn 1 (Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.one_mem_Kg X)
    rwa [mul_one] at this

theorem biInv_egA (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd)) (ha : ∀ γ P : G0g D.nd, a.coeff (P⁻¹ * γ * P) = a.coeff γ) :
    BiInv X (egA X a) := by
  refine ⟨fun x hx => by_contra fun h => hx (egA_of_not_mem X a h), fun x n hn => egA_mul X a x hn, fun x n hn => ?_⟩
  by_cases hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset
  · have hnx : n * x ∈ X.B.I.Kset := K_mul X.B.I hn.1 hx
    have hn1 : rhoX X ⟨n, hn.1⟩ = 1 := (rhoX_eq_one_iff X _).2 hn
    have e : rhoX X ⟨n * x, hnx⟩ = rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩ := by
      rw [show (⟨n * x, hnx⟩ : KSub X.B.I) = ⟨n, hn.1⟩ * ⟨x, hx⟩ from rfl, map_mul, hn1, one_mul]
    calc egA X a (n * x) = a.coeff (rhoX X ⟨n * x, hnx⟩) / ((X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal : ℂ) :=
          egA_rhoX X a ha ⟨n * x, hnx⟩
      _ = a.coeff (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩) / ((X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal : ℂ) := by rw [e]
      _ = egA X a x := (egA_rhoX X a ha ⟨x, hx⟩).symm
  · have hnx : n * x ∉ X.B.I.Kset := fun h => hx (by
      have := K_mul X.B.I (K_inv X.B.I hn.1) h
      rwa [inv_mul_cancel_left] at this)
    rw [egA_of_not_mem X a hnx, egA_of_not_mem X a hx]

theorem biInv_eT (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : BiInv X (eT X S) :=
  ⟨fun x hx => by_contra fun h => hx (eT_of_not_mem X S h), fun x n hn => eT_mul X S x hn,
    fun x n hn => eT_mul_left X S x hn⟩

theorem conv_sum [Fintype (KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g)] {f₁ f₂ : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf₁ : BiInv X f₁) (hf₂ : BiInv X f₂) (x : X.B.I.Gf) :
    X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f₁ f₂ x = (V X : ℂ) * ∑ q : KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g,
      f₁ ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) * f₂ (((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * x) := by
  rw [convF_fin X.B.I X.B.μf (g_pos X) (Kg_fin X) hf₁.1 hf₁.2.1 hf₂.2.2 x]
  rfl

instance instNeZeroG (D : D7) (k : Fin 2) : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩

instance finiteReductionGroup (D : D7) : Fintype (G0g D.nd) := by classical infer_instance

variable {X} (hS : Function.Surjective (rhoX X))
include hS

def sec (γ : G0g D.nd) : KSub X.B.I := Classical.choose (hS γ)

theorem rho_sec (γ : G0g D.nd) : rhoX X (sec hS γ) = γ := Classical.choose_spec (hS γ)

def hat (f : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ) (γ : G0g D.nd) : ℂ := (V X : ℂ) * f (sec hS γ)

theorem val_hat {f : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf : BiInv X f) (x : KSub X.B.I) :
    f x = hat hS f (rhoX X x) / (V X : ℂ) := by
  unfold hat
  have hk' : (((sec hS (rhoX X x))⁻¹ * x : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g :=
    (rhoX_eq_one_iff X _).1 (by rw [map_mul, map_inv, rho_sec, inv_mul_cancel])
  have e : (x : X.B.I.Gf) =
      (sec hS (rhoX X x) : X.B.I.Gf) * (((sec hS (rhoX X x))⁻¹ * x : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) := by
    push_cast
    group
  rw [e, hf.2.1 _ _ hk', mul_div_cancel_left₀ _ (V_ne X)]

theorem ker_eq : (rhoX X).ker = KgN X.B.I X.B.g := by
  ext x
  rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, rhoX_eq_one_iff]
  rfl

def qEquiv : (KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g) ≃* G0g D.nd :=
  (QuotientGroup.quotientMulEquivOfEq (ker_eq hS).symm).trans (QuotientGroup.quotientKerEquivOfSurjective _ hS)

theorem qEquiv_mk (k : KSub X.B.I) : qEquiv hS (QuotientGroup.mk k) = rhoX X k := rfl

theorem qEquiv_out (q : KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g) : qEquiv hS q = rhoX X q.out := by
  have h := qEquiv_mk hS q.out
  rwa [QuotientGroup.out_eq'] at h

def instFintypeQ : Fintype (KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g) := Fintype.ofEquiv _ (qEquiv hS).symm.toEquiv

theorem biInv_conv {f₁ f₂ : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf₁ : BiInv X f₁) (hf₂ : BiInv X f₂) :
    BiInv X (X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f₁ f₂) := by
  haveI := instFintypeQ hS
  refine ⟨fun x hx => ?_, fun x n hn => ?_, fun x n hn => ?_⟩
  · by_contra hxK
    apply hx
    rw [conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂]
    refine mul_eq_zero_of_right _ (Finset.sum_eq_zero fun q _ => ?_)
    have : f₂ (((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * x) = 0 := by
      by_contra h
      apply hxK
      have := K_mul X.B.I (q.out : KSub X.B.I).2 (hf₂.1 _ h)
      rwa [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this
    rw [this, mul_zero]
  · rw [conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂, conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂]
    congr 1
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun q _ => ?_
    rw [← mul_assoc, hf₂.2.1 _ n hn]
  · rw [conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂, conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂]
    congr 1
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun q _ => ?_
    set c : X.B.I.Gf := ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)
    have hc : c⁻¹ ∈ X.B.I.Kset := K_inv X.B.I (q.out : KSub X.B.I).2
    have hn' : c⁻¹ * n * c⁻¹⁻¹ ∈ X.B.I.Kg X.B.g := Kg_conj' X.B.I X.B.g hc hn
    have e : c⁻¹ * (n * x) = (c⁻¹ * n * c⁻¹⁻¹) * (c⁻¹ * x) := by group
    rw [e, hf₂.2.2 _ _ hn']

theorem conv_hat {f₁ f₂ : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf₁ : BiInv X f₁) (hf₂ : BiInv X f₂) (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    hat hS (X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f₁ f₂) γ = ∑ δ : G0g D.nd, hat hS f₁ δ * hat hS f₂ (δ⁻¹ * γ) := by
  haveI := instFintypeQ hS
  have key : ∀ q : KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g,
      hat hS f₁ (qEquiv hS q) * hat hS f₂ ((qEquiv hS q)⁻¹ * γ) =
        (V X : ℂ) * ((V X : ℂ) * (f₁ ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) *
          f₂ (((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * (sec hS γ : X.B.I.Gf)))) := by
    intro q
    have hm : ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * (sec hS γ : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kset :=
      K_mul X.B.I (K_inv X.B.I (q.out : KSub X.B.I).2) (sec hS γ).2
    have e2 : rhoX X ⟨_, hm⟩ = (qEquiv hS q)⁻¹ * γ := by
      have : (⟨_, hm⟩ : KSub X.B.I) = (q.out)⁻¹ * sec hS γ := rfl
      rw [this, map_mul, map_inv, rho_sec, qEquiv_out]
    have h1 : hat hS f₁ (rhoX X q.out) = (V X : ℂ) * f₁ ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) := by
      rw [val_hat hS hf₁ q.out]; field_simp [V_ne X]
    have h2 : hat hS f₂ (rhoX X ⟨_, hm⟩) = (V X : ℂ) * f₂ (((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * (sec hS γ : X.B.I.Gf)) := by
      rw [val_hat hS hf₂ ⟨_, hm⟩]; field_simp [V_ne X]
    rw [qEquiv_out, ← qEquiv_out hS q, ← e2, qEquiv_out, h1, h2]
    ring
  calc hat hS (X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f₁ f₂) γ = (V X : ℂ) * X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f₁ f₂ (sec hS γ) := rfl
    _ = (V X : ℂ) * ((V X : ℂ) * ∑ q : KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g, f₁ ((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) *
          f₂ (((q.out : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * (sec hS γ : X.B.I.Gf))) := by rw [conv_sum X hf₁ hf₂]
    _ = ∑ q : KSub X.B.I ⧸ KgN X.B.I X.B.g, hat hS f₁ (qEquiv hS q) * hat hS f₂ ((qEquiv hS q)⁻¹ * γ) := by
      rw [Finset.mul_sum, Finset.mul_sum]
      exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun q _ => (key q).symm
    _ = ∑ δ : G0g D.nd, hat hS f₁ δ * hat hS f₂ (δ⁻¹ * γ) :=
      Fintype.sum_equiv (qEquiv hS).toEquiv _ _ (fun _ => rfl)

theorem hat_inj {f f' : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf : BiInv X f) (hf' : BiInv X f') (h : ∀ γ, hat hS f γ = hat hS f' γ) :
    f = f' := by
  funext x
  by_cases hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset
  · have e1 := val_hat hS hf ⟨x, hx⟩
    have e2 := val_hat hS hf' ⟨x, hx⟩
    simp only at e1 e2
    rw [e1, e2, h]
  · have h1 : f x = 0 := by
      by_contra h0
      exact hx (hf.1 x h0)
    have h2 : f' x = 0 := by
      by_contra h0
      exact hx (hf'.1 x h0)
    rw [h1, h2]

theorem hat_egA (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd)) (ha : ∀ γ P : G0g D.nd, a.coeff (P⁻¹ * γ * P) = a.coeff γ)
    (γ : G0g D.nd) : hat hS (egA X a) γ = a.coeff γ := by
  unfold hat
  rw [egA_rhoX (X := X) a ha, rho_sec]
  show (V X : ℂ) * (a.coeff γ / (V X : ℂ)) = a.coeff γ
  field_simp [V_ne X]

theorem hat_eT (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    hat hS (eT X S) γ = ∑ δ : G0g D.nd, hat hS X.B.e δ * (ES S).coeff (δ⁻¹ * γ) := by
  unfold eT
  rw [conv_hat hS (biInv_e X) (biInv_egA X _ (ES_conj S))]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun δ _ => ?_
  rw [hat_egA hS _ (ES_conj S)]

theorem hat_e_idem (γ : G0g D.nd) : ∑ δ : G0g D.nd, hat hS X.B.e δ * hat hS X.B.e (δ⁻¹ * γ) = hat hS X.B.e γ := by
  rw [← conv_hat hS (biInv_e X) (biInv_e X), X.B.e_adm.2.1]

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsFin


[ARO_haar_inflation_03] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/AllRowsEtAlg.lean:1-135
Range SHA256 e88b767ce3d0b3de226ea637ab31e16b2c92a3cbd59c951d60126d2db7e36e9f
import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.AllRowsES

noncomputable section

open MonoidAlgebra MeasureTheory

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsEtAlg

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.MainNotions.ArithmeticCylinders Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsGA
  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsFin Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRhoX Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsES Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence

variable {D : D7} {X : HidaSetting D} (hS : Function.Surjective (rhoX X))
include hS

def Ehat : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd) := MonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff (Finsupp.equivFunOnFinite.symm (hat hS X.B.e))

theorem Ehat_coeff (γ : G0g D.nd) : (Ehat hS).coeff γ = hat hS X.B.e γ := by
  simp [Ehat, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_ofCoeff]

theorem Ehat_idem : Ehat hS * Ehat hS = Ehat hS := by
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext
  ext γ
  rw [coeff_mul_fin]
  simp only [Ehat_coeff hS]
  exact hat_e_idem hS γ

theorem hat_inv {f : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} (hf : BiInv X f) (hsa : ∀ x, f x⁻¹ = star (f x)) (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    hat hS f γ⁻¹ = star (hat hS f γ) := by
  have h1 : f ((sec hS γ⁻¹ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) = f (((sec hS γ)⁻¹ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) := by
    rw [val_hat hS hf, val_hat hS hf, rho_sec, map_inv, rho_sec]
  have hcoe : (((sec hS γ)⁻¹ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) = ((sec hS γ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ := rfl
  unfold hat
  rw [h1, hcoe, hsa, star_mul', Complex.star_def, Complex.conj_ofReal]

theorem Ehat_selfadj : conjA (Ehat hS) = Ehat hS := by
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext
  ext γ
  rw [conjA_coeff, Ehat_coeff hS, Ehat_coeff hS, hat_inv hS (biInv_e X) X.B.e_adm.2.2.1 γ, star_star]

theorem hat_eT' (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (γ : G0g D.nd) : hat hS (eT X S) γ = (Ehat hS * ES S).coeff γ := by
  rw [hat_eT hS S γ, coeff_mul_fin]
  simp only [Ehat_coeff hS]

theorem prod_idem (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : (Ehat hS * ES S) * (Ehat hS * ES S) = Ehat hS * ES S := by
  rw [mul_assoc, ES_central S (Ehat hS * ES S), mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc (Ehat hS) (Ehat hS), Ehat_idem hS,
    ES_idem]

theorem prod_selfadj (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : conjA (Ehat hS * ES S) = Ehat hS * ES S := by
  rw [conjA_mul, ES_selfadj, Ehat_selfadj hS, ES_central]

theorem eT_idem (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : X.B.I.convF X.B.μf (eT X S) (eT X S) = eT X S := by
  apply hat_inj hS (biInv_conv hS (biInv_eT X S) (biInv_eT X S)) (biInv_eT X S)
  intro γ
  rw [conv_hat hS (biInv_eT X S) (biInv_eT X S) γ]
  simp only [hat_eT' hS S]
  rw [← coeff_mul_fin, prod_idem hS S]

theorem eT_inv (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (x : X.B.I.Gf) : eT X S x⁻¹ = star (eT X S x) := by
  by_cases hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset
  · have key : eT X S x = hat hS (eT X S) (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩) / (V X : ℂ) := val_hat hS (biInv_eT X S) ⟨x, hx⟩
    have k1 : eT X S x⁻¹ = hat hS (eT X S) (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩)⁻¹ / (V X : ℂ) := by
      have := val_hat hS (biInv_eT X S) (⟨x, hx⟩ : KSub X.B.I)⁻¹
      rwa [map_inv] at this
    have hc : ∀ γ, (Ehat hS * ES S).coeff γ⁻¹ = star ((Ehat hS * ES S).coeff γ) := by
      intro γ
      conv_lhs => rw [← prod_selfadj hS S]
      rw [conjA_coeff, inv_inv]
    rw [k1, key, hat_eT' hS S, hat_eT' hS S, hc, star_div₀, Complex.star_def, Complex.conj_ofReal]
  · have hx' : x⁻¹ ∉ X.B.I.Kset := fun h => hx (by simpa using K_inv X.B.I h)
    rw [eT_of_not_mem X S hx, eT_of_not_mem X S hx', star_zero]

open scoped ComplexOrder

omit hS in

theorem posType_of (I : AutInst) (μ : Measure I.Gf) [μ.IsMulLeftInvariant] [IsFiniteMeasureOnCompacts μ]
    {f : I.Gf → ℂ} (hc : Continuous f) {C : Set I.Gf} (hC : IsCompact C) (hCo : IsOpen C)
    (hsupp : ∀ x, f x ≠ 0 → x ∈ C) (hconv : I.convF μ f f = f) (hsa : ∀ x, f x⁻¹ = star (f x)) :
    I.IsPosType f := by
  intro m x c
  have hsub : ∀ i j, f ((x i)⁻¹ * x j) = ∫ z, star (f (z⁻¹ * x i)) * f (z⁻¹ * x j) ∂μ := by
    intro i j
    conv_lhs => rw [← hconv]
    have := integral_mul_left_eq_self (μ := μ) (fun y => f y * f (y⁻¹ * ((x i)⁻¹ * x j))) (x i)⁻¹
    show ∫ y, f y * f (y⁻¹ * ((x i)⁻¹ * x j)) ∂μ = _
    rw [← this]
    congr 1
    funext z
    rw [← hsa (z⁻¹ * x i)]
    congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> group
  have hint : ∀ i j, Integrable (fun z => star (f (z⁻¹ * x i)) * f (z⁻¹ * x j)) μ := by
    intro i j
    refine Continuous.integrable_of_hasCompactSupport ?_ ?_
    · exact ((hc.comp (continuous_inv.mul continuous_const)).star).mul
        (hc.comp (continuous_inv.mul continuous_const))
    · refine HasCompactSupport.intro
        (IsCompact.image (f := fun y : I.Gf => x j * y⁻¹) hC (continuous_const.mul continuous_inv)) ?_
      intro z hz
      have h0 : f (z⁻¹ * x j) = 0 := by
        by_contra h
        apply hz
        exact ⟨z⁻¹ * x j, hsupp _ h, by group⟩
      rw [h0, mul_zero]
  have hF : ∀ z, ((Complex.normSq (∑ j, c j * f (z⁻¹ * x j)) : ℝ) : ℂ) =
      ∑ i, ∑ j, star (c i) * c j * (star (f (z⁻¹ * x i)) * f (z⁻¹ * x j)) := by
    intro z
    rw [Complex.normSq_eq_conj_mul_self, ← Complex.star_def, star_sum, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [star_mul']
    ring
  have key : ∫ z, ((Complex.normSq (∑ j, c j * f (z⁻¹ * x j)) : ℝ) : ℂ) ∂μ =
      ∑ i, ∑ j, star (c i) * c j * f ((x i)⁻¹ * x j) := by
    simp_rw [hF]
    rw [integral_finset_sum _ (fun i _ => integrable_finset_sum _ (fun j _ => (hint i j).const_mul _))]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    rw [integral_finset_sum _ (fun j _ => (hint i j).const_mul _)]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [integral_const_mul, hsub]
  rw [← key, integral_complex_ofReal]
  exact Complex.zero_le_real.2 (integral_nonneg fun z => Complex.normSq_nonneg _)

end Pos

theorem eT_pos (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : X.B.I.IsPosType (eT X S) := by
  exact posType_of X.B.I X.B.μf (isLocallyConstant_eT X S).continuous (PI.WAU.Cpt.isCompact_Kset X.B.I)
    (PI.WAU.kset_open X.B.I) (fun x hx => by_contra fun h => hx (eT_of_not_mem X S h)) (eT_idem hS S)
    (eT_inv hS S)

theorem etAlg_of (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : EtAlg X S :=
  ⟨eT_idem hS S, eT_inv hS S, eT_pos hS S⟩

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsEtAlg


[ARO_finite_events_01] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/AllRowsOf.lean:18-79

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

open Classical in

def egA (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd)) (x : X.B.I.Gf) : ℂ :=
  if h : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset ∧ ∃ γ : G0g D.nd, GoodAt X x γ then
    a.coeff (Classical.choose h.2) / ((X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg X.B.g)).toReal : ℂ)
  else 0

theorem goodIdempotent_eq : egA X D.ntypes.e0 = eg X := rfl

def tupOf (a : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) : Tup2 D :=
  haveI : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
  fun k v => Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a ⟨k, v⟩)

def ES (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd) :=
  haveI : ∀ k, NeZero (D.host.g k) := fun k => ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  haveI : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
  MonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff <| Finsupp.equivFunOnFinite.symm fun g : G0g D.nd =>
    ∑ᶠ a : (∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))),
      (open Classical in if tupOf a ∈ S then
        ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))).coeff (g kv) else 0)

theorem ES_good : ES (D := D) {t | D.ntypes.goodTuple t} = D.ntypes.e0 := rfl

def eT (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ :=
  X.B.I.convF X.B.μf X.B.e (egA X (ES S))

open Classical in

def ReT (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund :=
  if h : ∃ T, X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (eT X S) T then Classical.choose h else 0

def dimX (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : ℕ := X.B.I.Dcount X.B.σs X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (ReT X S)

end Events

section Props

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

def EJIdem (D : D7) (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ k : Fin 2, eJk D k (ψ₀ k) * eJk D k (ψ₀ k) = eJk D k (ψ₀ k)

def EtUniv : Prop := eT X Set.univ = X.B.e

def EtOne (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Fin 2) (S : Finset (Tup D k)),
    (eT X (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k))) 1).re =
      (X.B.e 1).re * ∑ t ∈ S, ∏ v, wtOf D k (eJk D k (ψ₀ k)) (t v)

def EtInt : Prop := ∀ S : Set (Tup2 D), Integrable (eT X S) X.B.μf

def RowsLab : Prop :=
  ∃ (lab : ∀ k, Fin D.what₂.Dall → D.nd.PlG k → (ZMod (D.host.g k))[X]) (inc : Fin D.what₂.Drows → Fin D.what₂.Dall),
    (∀ k σ v, IsLabel D k (lab k σ v)) ∧
    (∀ S : Set (Tup2 D), dimX X S = Nat.card {τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall // (fun k => lab k τ) ∈ S}) ∧
    Function.Injective inc ∧ (∀ k σ, lab k (inc σ) = D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) ∧
    ∀ τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall, D.ntypes.goodTuple (fun k => lab k τ) ↔ τ ∈ Set.range inc

end Props

variable {D : D7}

abbrev CV (D : D7) : Type := ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)

def Ea (a : CV D) : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd) :=
  haveI : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
  MonoidAlgebra.ofCoeff <| Finsupp.equivFunOnFinite.symm fun γ : G0g D.nd =>
    ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))).coeff (γ kv)

theorem Ea_coeff (a : CV D) (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    (Ea a).coeff γ = haveI : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
      ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))).coeff (γ kv) := by
  simp [Ea, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_ofCoeff]

open Classical in
theorem ES_eq (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : ES S = ∑ a ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a ∈ S), Ea a := by
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext
  ext γ
  rw [MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, Finset.sum_apply', Finset.sum_filter]
  unfold ES
  simp only [Finsupp.coe_equivFunOnFinite_symm]
  rw [finsum_eq_sum_of_fintype]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
  split_ifs
  · rw [Ea_coeff]
  · rfl

theorem monicOf_inj {g : ℕ} [Fact g.Prime] {N : ℕ} {a b : Fin N → ZMod g}
    (h : Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf a = Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf b) : a = b := by
  funext i
  rw [← Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.coeff_monicOf a i, ← Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.coeff_monicOf b i, h]

open Classical in
theorem Ea_mul (a b : CV D) : Ea a * Ea b = if a = b then Ea a else 0 := by
  have : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext
  ext γ
  rw [coeff_mul_fin]
  simp only [Ea_coeff]
  have key : ∑ δ : G0g D.nd, (∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k),
        (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))).coeff (δ kv)) *
      ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (b kv))).coeff ((δ⁻¹ * γ) kv) =
      ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), ((D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))) *
        (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (b kv)))).coeff (γ kv) := by
    simp only [coeff_mul_fin]
    rw [Fintype.prod_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun δ _ => ?_
    rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
    rfl
  rw [key]
  split_ifs with hab
  · subst hab
    rw [Ea_coeff]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
    rw [D.ntypes.eSer_idem]
  · obtain ⟨kv, hkv⟩ : ∃ kv, a kv ≠ b kv := by
      by_contra h
      push Not at h
      exact hab (funext h)
    have h0 : (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))) * (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (b kv))) = 0 :=
      D.ntypes.eSer_orth kv.1 _ _ fun h => hkv (monicOf_inj h)
    rw [Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ kv) (by rw [h0]; simp)]
    simp

open Classical in

theorem ES_idem (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : ES S * ES S = ES S := by
  rw [ES_eq, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a ha => ?_
  simp only [Ea_mul]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single a (fun b _ hb => if_neg (Ne.symm hb)) (fun h => absurd ha h), if_pos rfl]

theorem ES_central (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (y : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd)) : ES S * y = y * ES S :=
  central_of_class _ (ES_conj S) y

theorem ES_selfadj (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : conjA (ES S) = ES S :=
  selfadj_of_central_idem _ (ES_idem S) (ES_central S)

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsES

variable {D : D7}

instance instFactG' (k : Fin 2) : Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩

open Classical in

theorem ES_inter (S S' : Set (Tup2 D)) : ES S * ES S' = ES (S ∩ S') := by
  rw [ES_eq, ES_eq, ES_eq, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
  simp only [Ea_mul]
  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => Finset.sum_ite_eq _ _ _, ← Finset.sum_filter]
  congr 1
  ext a
  simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Set.mem_inter_iff]

theorem Ea_zero (a : CV D) (h : tupOf a ∉ labT (D := D)) : Ea a = 0 := by
  classical
  have : ∀ k, NeZero (D.host.g k) := fun k => ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  have : ∃ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), ¬ IsLabel D kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    apply h
    unfold labT
    refine Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨a, Finset.mem_univ _, rfl⟩, fun k v => hc ⟨k, v⟩⟩
  obtain ⟨kv, hkv⟩ := this
  have h0 : D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) = 0 := D.ntypes.eSer_zero _ _ hkv
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext; ext γ
  rw [Ea_coeff, Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ kv) (by rw [h0]; rfl)]
  rfl

open Classical in

theorem ES_split (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : ES S = ∑ t ∈ (labT (D := D)).filter (· ∈ S), ES {t} := by
  simp only [ES_eq]
  have e1 : ∑ a ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a ∈ S), Ea a =
      ∑ a ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a ∈ S ∧ tupOf a ∈ labT (D := D)), Ea a := by
    rw [Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_filter]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
    by_cases h1 : tupOf a ∈ S
    · by_cases h2 : tupOf a ∈ labT (D := D)
      · rw [if_pos h1, if_pos ⟨h1, h2⟩]
      · rw [if_pos h1, if_neg (fun h => h2 h.2), Ea_zero a h2]
    · rw [if_neg h1, if_neg (fun h => h1 h.1)]
  rw [e1, ← Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to (g := tupOf) (t := (labT (D := D)).filter (· ∈ S)) (fun a ha => by
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at ha ⊢
    exact ⟨ha.2, ha.1⟩)]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun t ht => ?_
  rw [Finset.filter_filter]
  congr 1
  ext a
  simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Set.mem_singleton_iff]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨-, h⟩; exact h
  · intro h; rw [Finset.mem_filter] at ht; exact ⟨⟨h ▸ ht.2, h ▸ ht.1⟩, h⟩

def wtOf (k : Fin 2) (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k))))
    (f : (ZMod (D.host.g k))[X]) : ℝ :=
  ((a * D.ntypes.eSer k f).coeff 1).re / (a.coeff 1).re

structure AllRows (D : D7) : Type 1 where
  B : Formalism.MainNotions.S.RowsAut
  n_eq : B.I.n = D.R.r
  f_eq : B.I.f = D.R.f
  labAll : ∀ k, Fin D.what₂.Dall → D.nd.PlG k → (ZMod (D.host.g k))[X]
  labAll_label : ∀ k σ v, IsLabel D k (labAll k σ v)
  inc : Fin D.what₂.Drows → Fin D.what₂.Dall
  eVec : ∀ k, D.nd.PlG k → MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k)))
  eVec_idem : ∀ k v, eVec k v * eVec k v = eVec k v
  eT : Set (Tup2 D) → B.I.Gf → ℂ
  ReT : Set (Tup2 D) → (B.I.L2 B.μf B.fund →L[ℂ] B.I.L2 B.μf B.fund)
  ReT_spec : ∀ S, B.I.IsRConvF B.μf B.fund B.R (eT S) (ReT S)
  eT_univ : eT Set.univ = B.e
  ReT_univ : ReT Set.univ = B.Re
  eT_one : ∀ (k : Fin 2) (S : Finset (Tup D k)),
    (eT (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k))) 1).re =
      (B.e 1).re * ∑ t ∈ S, ∏ v, wtOf D k (eVec k v) (t v)

  A.B.I.Dcount A.B.σs A.B.μf A.B.fund A.B.R (A.ReT S)

def DimEqTotalCount (A : AllRows D) : Prop := D.what₂.Dall = A.B.D

def DimEqGoodCount (A : AllRows D) : Prop := D.what₂.Drows = A.dimT {t | D.ntypes.goodTuple t}

def LabelCountGoodInjective (A : AllRows D) : Prop :=
  (∀ S : Set (Tup2 D), A.dimT S = Nat.card {τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall // (fun k => A.labAll k τ) ∈ S}) ∧
    Function.Injective A.inc ∧ (∀ k σ, A.labAll k (A.inc σ) = D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) ∧
    ∀ τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall, D.ntypes.goodTuple (fun k => A.labAll k τ) ↔ τ ∈ Set.range A.inc

def GramW (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)) {r : ℕ} (fr frd : Fin r → Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) : Prop :=
  ∀ i j, hhat I (frd i) (fr j) - (if i = j then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq I w

theorem exists_lifts (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) {r : ℕ}
    {fr : Fin r → Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hfr : ∀ j, fr j ∈ I.Lf) :
    ∃ c : Fin r → Fin I.n → I.E, ∀ j, c j ∈ I.L ∧ fr j - iv I (c j) ∈ Lq I w := by
  choose c hcL hc using fun j => dense I w hp hcard (hfr j)
  exact ⟨c, fun j => ⟨hcL j, hc j⟩⟩

theorem gram_lift {r : ℕ} {fr frd : Fin r → Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hfrd : ∀ i, frd i ∈ I.Lf)
    (hg : GramW w fr frd) {c : Fin r → Fin I.n → I.E} (hc : ∀ j, fr j - iv I (c j) ∈ Lq I w) :
    GramW w (fun j => iv I (c j)) frd := fun i j => by
  have := sub_mem (hg i j) (hhat_Lq_right (hfrd i) (hc j))
  convert this using 1
  rw [hhat_sub_right]
  ring

theorem isRedBasis_of_gram (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) (hm : m = I.n)
    {frd : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hfrd : ∀ i, frd i ∈ I.Lf) {c : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E}
    (hcL : ∀ j, c j ∈ I.L) (hg : GramW w (fun j => iv I (c j)) frd) : IsRedBasis0 I w c := by
  subst hm
  refine ⟨hcL, ?_⟩
  let b' : Fin I.n → I.L := fun j => ⟨c j, hcL j⟩
  let f := Submodule.mkQ (w.asIdeal • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 I.E) I.L))
  have hli : LinearIndependent (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) (f ∘ b') := by
    rw [Fintype.linearIndependent_iff]
    intro g hg0 i
    have hn : 0 < I.n := Fin.pos i
    choose α hα using fun j => Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective (g j)
    have h0 : (∑ j, α j • b' j : I.L) ∈ w.asIdeal • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 I.E) I.L) := by
      rw [← Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero, mk_sum' I w, ← hg0]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
      rw [← hα j]
      exact (Module.Quotient.mk_smul_mk _ _ _ _).symm
    rw [← mem_iff] at h0
    have h1 : iv I (∑ j, ((α j : 𝓞 I.E) : I.E) • c j) ∈ Lq I w := by
      refine iv_mem_w I w ?_
      convert h0 using 1
      simp [b']
    have hiv : iv I (∑ j, ((α j : 𝓞 I.E) : I.E) • c j) = ∑ j, iE I (α j) • iv I (c j) := by
      funext k
      simp only [iv, iE, Finset.sum_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, map_sum, map_mul]
    have h2 := hhat_Lq_right (hfrd i) h1
    rw [hiv, hhat_sum_right] at h2
    have h3 : ∑ j, iE I (α j) * (hhat I (frd i) (iv I (c j)) - (if i = j then 1 else 0)) ∈ Wq I w :=
      sum_mem fun j _ => Wq_mul (hg i j) (iE_int I (α j))
    have h4 := sub_mem h2 h3
    have e : ∑ j, iE I (α j) * hhat I (frd i) (iv I (c j)) -
        ∑ j, iE I (α j) * (hhat I (frd i) (iv I (c j)) - (if i = j then 1 else 0)) = iE I (α i) := by
      rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
      simp only [mul_sub, sub_sub_cancel, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero]
      rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq]
      simp
    rw [e] at h4
    have h5 : α i ∈ w.asIdeal := int_of_Wq hp hcard hn h4
    rw [← hα i]
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.2 h5
  intro y hy
  by_cases hn : I.n = 0
  · refine ⟨fun _ => 0, ?_⟩
    have : y = 0 := by
      funext k
      exact absurd k.2 (by omega)
    rw [this]
    simp
  have : Nonempty (Fin I.n) := ⟨⟨0, Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hn⟩⟩
  have hcE : Fintype.card (Fin I.n) = Module.finrank (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) (RedQ I w) := by
    rw [Fintype.card_fin, finrank_eq I w]
  have hspan := hli.span_eq_top_of_card_eq_finrank hcE
  have hmem : f ⟨y, hy⟩ ∈ Submodule.span (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) (Set.range (f ∘ b')) := by
    rw [hspan]; trivial
  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun _).1 hmem
  choose k hk using fun j => int_surj I w hp hcard (a j)
  refine ⟨k, ?_⟩
  have h1 : ((⟨y, hy⟩ : I.L) - ∑ j, k j • b' j) ∈ (w.asIdeal • (⊤ : Submodule (𝓞 I.E) I.L)) := by
    rw [← Submodule.Quotient.mk_eq_zero, Submodule.Quotient.mk_sub, sub_eq_zero, mk_sum' I w]
    change f ⟨y, hy⟩ = _
    rw [← ha]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [← hk j, Submodule.Quotient.mk_smul, Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul]
    rfl
  rw [← mem_iff] at h1
  convert h1 using 1


[ARO_level_shape_02] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/KppFrameW.lean:242-284

theorem redMatB_rep {b : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} {x : I.Gf} {M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)}
    (h : RedMatB I w p b x M) (i : Fin m) :
    RepV w p (fun j => iv I (b j)) (I.fmat x *ᵥ iv I (b i)) (fun j => M j i) := h i

theorem conj_basis (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) (hm : m = I.n)
    {b c : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hb : IsRedBasis0 I w b) (hc : IsRedBasis0 I w c) :
    ∃ P Q : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p), P * Q = 1 ∧ Q * P = 1 ∧
      ∀ x ∈ I.Kset, ∀ M N : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p), RedMatB I w p b x M → RedMatB I w p c x N →
        M * P = P * N := by
  have : NeZero p := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  choose Pc hPc using fun j => rep_exists hp hcard hb (DictLat.iv_mem I (hc.1 j))
  choose Qc hQc using fun j => rep_exists hp hcard hc (DictLat.iv_mem I (hb.1 j))
  let P : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p) := Matrix.of fun k j => Pc j k
  let Q : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p) := Matrix.of fun k j => Qc j k
  have hP : ∀ j, RepV w p (fun k => iv I (b k)) (iv I (c j)) (fun k => P k j) := hPc
  have hQ : ∀ j, RepV w p (fun k => iv I (c k)) (iv I (b j)) (fun k => Q k j) := hQc
  have hPQ : P * Q = 1 := by
    ext i j
    have h1 := rep_comp hp hcard hb.1 (hQ j) hP
    have h2 := rep_self (w := w) hp (fun k => iv I (b k)) j
    have := rep_unique hp hcard hm hb h1 h2
    have := congrFun this i
    simpa [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Matrix.mul_apply] using this
  refine ⟨P, Q, hPQ, (Matrix.mul_eq_one_comm_of_card_eq (Fin m) (Fin m) _ rfl).mp hPQ, ?_⟩
  intro x hx M N hM hN
  ext i j
  have h1 := rep_comp hp hcard hb.1 (redMatB_rep hN j) hP
  have h2 := rep_comp hp hcard hb.1 (rep_mulVec hx (hP j)) (fun k => redMatB_rep hM k)
  have := rep_unique hp hcard hm hb h2 h1
  have := congrFun this i
  simpa [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct, Matrix.mul_apply] using this

theorem entry [NeZero p] {fr frd : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hfrd : ∀ i, frd i ∈ I.Lf)
    {c : Fin m → Fin I.n → I.E} (hcL : ∀ j, c j ∈ I.L) (hc : ∀ j, fr j - iv I (c j) ∈ Lq I w)
    (hg : GramW w (fun j => iv I (c j)) frd) {x : I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ I.Kset) {N : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) (ZMod p)}
    (hN : RedMatB I w p c x N) (i j : Fin m) :
    CongW (w := w) p (hhat I (frd i) (I.fmat x *ᵥ fr j)) (N i j) := by
  have h1 := coef hfrd (fun k => DictLat.iv_mem I (hcL k)) hg (redMatB_rep hN j) i
  refine congW_of_sub h1 ?_
  rw [← hhat_sub_right, ← Matrix.mulVec_sub]
  exact hhat_Lq_right (hfrd i) (stab_w I w hx (hc j))

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppFrameW


[ARO_level_shape_03] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/KppLoc.lean:1-214

import Mathlib
