open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf PIW.AdelClassFin Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf
  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKerA Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppRedW Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppFrameW Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppIdx

section Gen

variable {I : AutInst} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)} {g : ℕ}

theorem zmod_Wq (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {c : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (h : ZMod' I g c) : c ∈ Wq I w := by
  obtain ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩ := h
  have : (g : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) ∈ Wq I w := by
    have := iE_mem (I := I) hg
    simpa [iE] using this
  exact Wq_mul' this ht

theorem zmod_sg {c : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (h : ZMod' I g c) : ZMod' I g (sg I c) := by
  obtain ⟨t, ht, rfl⟩ := h
  exact ⟨sg I t, DictLat.sigR_int I ht, by rw [map_mul, sg_natCast]⟩

theorem mL_le (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) : mL I g ≤ Lq I w := by
  unfold mL
  rw [Submodule.span_le]
  rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
  have := smul_mem I w hg hx
  simpa using this

theorem hhat_neg_left (u v : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) : hhat I (-u) v = -hhat I u v := by
  have := hhat_sub_left I 0 u v
  rwa [zero_sub, DictLat.hhat_zero_left, zero_sub] at this

theorem hhat_neg_right (u v : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) : hhat I u (-v) = -hhat I u v := by
  have := hhat_sub_right I u 0 v
  rwa [zero_sub, DictLat.hhat_zero_right, zero_sub] at this

theorem pair_right (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {u : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hu : u ∈ I.Lf)
    {S : Set (VA I)} (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, hhat I u s ∈ Wq I w) {v : VA I} (hv : InMod I g S v) : hhat I u v ∈ Wq I w := by
  obtain ⟨y, hy, z, hz, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 hv
  clear hv
  rw [DictLat.hhat_add_right]
  refine add_mem ?_ (hhat_Lq_right hu (mL_le hg hz))
  induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem s hs => exact hS s hs
  | zero => rw [DictLat.hhat_zero_right]; exact zero_mem _
  | add a b _ _ ha hb => rw [DictLat.hhat_add_right]; exact add_mem ha hb
  | smul r a _ ha =>
    show hhat I u ((r : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • a) ∈ Wq I w
    rw [DictLat.hhat_smul_right]; exact Wq_mul ha r.2

theorem pair_left (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {v : Fin I.n → I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (hv : v ∈ I.Lf)
    {S : Set (VA I)} (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, hhat I s v ∈ Wq I w) {u : VA I} (hu : InMod I g S u) : hhat I u v ∈ Wq I w := by
  obtain ⟨y, hy, z, hz, rfl⟩ := Submodule.mem_sup.1 hu
  clear hu
  rw [DictLat.hhat_add_left]
  refine add_mem ?_ ?_
  · induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem s hs => exact hS s hs
    | zero => rw [DictLat.hhat_zero_left]; exact zero_mem _
    | add a b _ _ ha hb => rw [DictLat.hhat_add_left]; exact add_mem ha hb
    | smul r a _ ha =>
      show hhat I ((r : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • a) v ∈ Wq I w
      rw [DictLat.hhat_smul_left]; exact Wq_mul ha (DictLat.sigR_int I r.2)
  · have hgW : (g : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) ∈ Wq I w := by
      have := iE_mem (I := I) hg
      simpa [iE] using this
    unfold mL at hz
    induction hz using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem s hs =>
      obtain ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩ := hs
      rw [DictLat.hhat_smul_left, sg_natCast]
      exact Wq_mul' hgW (dual_hat_integral I x hx v hv)
    | zero => rw [DictLat.hhat_zero_left]; exact zero_mem _
    | add a b _ _ ha hb => rw [DictLat.hhat_add_left]; exact add_mem ha hb
    | smul r a _ ha =>
      show hhat I ((r : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • a) v ∈ Wq I w
      rw [DictLat.hhat_smul_left]; exact Wq_mul ha (DictLat.sigR_int I r.2)

theorem dir_col (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {x : I.Gf} {u v : VA I} (hu : u ∈ I.Lf) {S : Set (VA I)}
    (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, hhat I u s ∈ Wq I w) (hx : InMod I g S ((I.fmat x - 1) *ᵥ v)) :
    hhat I u (I.fmat x *ᵥ v) - hhat I u v ∈ Wq I w := by
  have := pair_right hg hu hS hx
  rwa [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec, hhat_sub_right] at this

theorem dual_col (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {x : I.Gf} {u v : VA I} (hv : v ∈ I.Lf) {S : Set (VA I)}
    (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, hhat I s v ∈ Wq I w) (hx : InMod I g S ((I.fmat x⁻¹ - 1) *ᵥ u)) :
    hhat I u (I.fmat x *ᵥ v) - hhat I u v ∈ Wq I w := by
  have h1 : hhat I u (I.fmat x *ᵥ v) = hhat I (I.fmat x⁻¹ *ᵥ u) v := by
    have := hhat_fmat I x (I.fmat x⁻¹ *ᵥ u) v
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← fmat_mul, mul_inv_cancel, fmat_one, Matrix.one_mulVec] at this
    exact this
  have := pair_left hg hv hS hx
  rwa [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec, hhat_sub_left, ← h1] at this

variable {p : ℕ} {r : ℕ}

theorem cong_dir (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {fr frd : Fin r → VA I} (hfrd : ∀ i, frd i ∈ I.Lf)
    (hgr : GramW w fr frd) {x : I.Gf} (i j : Fin r) {S : Set (VA I)}
    (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, ∃ j', s = fr j' ∧ j' ≠ i) (hx : InMod I g S ((I.fmat x - 1) *ᵥ fr j)) :
    CongW (w := w) p (hhat I (frd i) (I.fmat x *ᵥ fr j)) (if i = j then 1 else 0) := by
  have h1 := dir_col hg (hfrd i) (fun s hs => by
    obtain ⟨j', rfl, hj'⟩ := hS s hs
    have := hgr i j'
    rwa [if_neg (Ne.symm hj'), sub_zero] at this) hx
  refine congW_of_sub ?_ h1
  refine congW_of_sub (t := ((if i = j then 1 else 0 : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F)) ?_ ?_
  · have := congW_intCast (I := I) (w := w) (p := p) (if i = j then 1 else 0)
    convert this using 2
    split_ifs <;> simp
  · have := hgr i j
    convert this using 2
    split_ifs <;> simp

theorem cong_dual (hg : (g : 𝓞 I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {fr frd : Fin r → VA I} (hfr : ∀ j, fr j ∈ I.Lf)
    (hgr : GramW w fr frd) {x : I.Gf} (i j : Fin r) {S : Set (VA I)}
    (hS : ∀ s ∈ S, ∃ i', s = frd i' ∧ i' ≠ j) (hx : InMod I g S ((I.fmat x⁻¹ - 1) *ᵥ frd i)) :
    CongW (w := w) p (hhat I (frd i) (I.fmat x *ᵥ fr j)) (if i = j then 1 else 0) := by
  have h1 := dual_col hg (hfr j) (fun s hs => by
    obtain ⟨i', rfl, hi'⟩ := hS s hs
    have := hgr i' j
    rwa [if_neg hi', sub_zero] at this) hx
  refine congW_of_sub ?_ h1
  refine congW_of_sub (t := ((if i = j then 1 else 0 : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F)) ?_ ?_
  · have := congW_intCast (I := I) (w := w) (p := p) (if i = j then 1 else 0)
    convert this using 2
    split_ifs <;> simp
  · have := hgr i j
    convert this using 2
    split_ifs <;> simp

theorem inMod_neg {S : Set (VA I)} {v : VA I} (h : InMod I g S v) : InMod I g S (-v) := neg_mem h

end Gen

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem hrD (D : D7) : D.R.r = D.R.k + 2 * D.R.rS + 1 := by
  unfold Ranks.r Ranks.rS
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := D.R.m_even
  have := D.R.two_le_m
  omega

def fr (ob : Bool) (j : Fin D.R.r) : VA X.B.I :=
  if h1 : j.1 < D.R.rS then X.e0 ⟨j.1, h1⟩
  else if h2 : j.1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k then
    (if ob then X.bw ⟨j.1 - D.R.rS, by omega⟩ else X.bwd ⟨D.R.k - 1 - (j.1 - D.R.rS), by omega⟩)
  else if j.1 = D.R.rS + D.R.k then X.z0
  else if h4 : j.1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1) < D.R.rS then X.f0 ⟨D.R.rS - 1 - (j.1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1)), by omega⟩
  else 0

def frd (ob : Bool) (j : Fin D.R.r) : VA X.B.I :=
  if h1 : j.1 < D.R.rS then X.f0 ⟨j.1, h1⟩
  else if h2 : j.1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k then
    (if ob then X.bwd ⟨j.1 - D.R.rS, by omega⟩ else X.bw ⟨D.R.k - 1 - (j.1 - D.R.rS), by omega⟩)
  else if j.1 = D.R.rS + D.R.k then -X.z0
  else if h4 : j.1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1) < D.R.rS then X.e0 ⟨D.R.rS - 1 - (j.1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1)), by omega⟩
  else 0

def rv {n : ℕ} (a : Fin n) : Fin n := ⟨n - 1 - a.1, by omega⟩

@[simp] theorem rv_val {n : ℕ} (a : Fin n) : (rv a).1 = n - 1 - a.1 := rfl

theorem rv_rv {n : ℕ} (a : Fin n) : rv (rv a) = a := Fin.ext (by simp <;> omega)

theorem frame_isotropic (ob : Bool) (a : Fin D.R.rS) : fr X ob (xI (hrD D) a) = X.e0 a := by
  unfold fr; rw [dif_pos (show (xI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS from a.2)]; rfl

theorem fr_w (ob : Bool) (i : Fin D.R.k) : fr X ob (wI (hrD D) i) = if ob then X.bw i else X.bwd (rv i) := by
  unfold fr
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (wI (hrD D) i).1 < D.R.rS by simp), dif_pos (show (wI (hrD D) i).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp)]
  congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> ext <;> simp

theorem frame_anisotropic (ob : Bool) : fr X ob (zI (hrD D)) = X.z0 := by
  unfold fr
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (zI (hrD D)).1 < D.R.rS by simp <;> omega),
    dif_neg (show ¬ (zI (hrD D)).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp), if_pos (by simp)]

theorem fr_y (ob : Bool) (a : Fin D.R.rS) : fr X ob (yI (hrD D) a) = X.f0 (rv a) := by
  unfold fr
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (yI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS by simp <;> omega),
    dif_neg (show ¬ (yI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp <;> omega), if_neg (by simp <;> omega),
    dif_pos (show (yI (hrD D) a).1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1) < D.R.rS by simp)]
  congr 1; ext; simp

theorem dual_frame_isotropic (ob : Bool) (a : Fin D.R.rS) : frd X ob (xI (hrD D) a) = X.f0 a := by
  unfold frd; rw [dif_pos (show (xI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS from a.2)]; rfl

theorem frd_w (ob : Bool) (i : Fin D.R.k) : frd X ob (wI (hrD D) i) = if ob then X.bwd i else X.bw (rv i) := by
  unfold frd
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (wI (hrD D) i).1 < D.R.rS by simp), dif_pos (show (wI (hrD D) i).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp)]
  congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> ext <;> simp

theorem dual_frame_anisotropic (ob : Bool) : frd X ob (zI (hrD D)) = -X.z0 := by
  unfold frd
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (zI (hrD D)).1 < D.R.rS by simp <;> omega),
    dif_neg (show ¬ (zI (hrD D)).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp), if_pos (by simp)]

theorem frd_y (ob : Bool) (a : Fin D.R.rS) : frd X ob (yI (hrD D) a) = X.e0 (rv a) := by
  unfold frd
  rw [dif_neg (show ¬ (yI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS by simp <;> omega),
    dif_neg (show ¬ (yI (hrD D) a).1 < D.R.rS + D.R.k by simp <;> omega), if_neg (by simp <;> omega),
    dif_pos (show (yI (hrD D) a).1 - (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1) < D.R.rS by simp)]
  congr 1; ext; simp

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppLoc


[ARO_level_shape_04] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/KppLocG.lean:1-156
Range SHA256 e243182826a416248e22bd39f23e49b05fefea4bdd2db1c04dbcb77e6e3a0cd1
import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.KppLoc

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Matrix
open scoped TensorProduct

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppLoc

open Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.S Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf PIW.AdelClassFin Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf
  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKerA Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppRedW Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppFrameW Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppIdx

theorem ne_val {r : ℕ} {i j : Fin r} (h : i.1 ≠ j.1) : i ≠ j := fun e => h (congrArg Fin.val e)

theorem rv_inj {n : ℕ} {a b : Fin n} : rv a = rv b ↔ a = b :=
  ⟨fun h => by rw [← rv_rv a, h, rv_rv], fun h => h ▸ rfl⟩

theorem gram_of {I : AutInst} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)} {u v : VA I} {P Q : Prop} [Decidable P] [Decidable Q]
    (h : hhat I u v - (if P then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq I w) (hPQ : P ↔ Q) :
    hhat I u v - (if Q then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq I w := by
  have e : (if P then (1 : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) else 0) = if Q then 1 else 0 := by
    by_cases hp : P
    · rw [if_pos hp, if_pos (hPQ.1 hp)]
    · rw [if_neg hp, if_neg (fun hq => hp (hPQ.2 hq))]
  rwa [e] at h

theorem gram_ne {I : AutInst} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)} {u v : VA I} {Q : Prop} [Decidable Q]
    (h : hhat I u v ∈ Wq I w) (hQ : ¬ Q) : hhat I u v - (if Q then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq I w := by
  rwa [if_neg hQ, sub_zero]

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem sg_hA (u v : VA X.B.I) : sg X.B.I (hA X.B.I u v) = hhat X.B.I v u := hhat_conj X.B.I u v

theorem fr_wt (i : Fin D.R.k) : fr X true (wI (hrD D) i) = X.bw i := by rw [fr_w]; rfl
theorem fr_wf (i : Fin D.R.k) : fr X false (wI (hrD D) i) = X.bwd (rv i) := by rw [fr_w]; rfl
theorem frd_wt (i : Fin D.R.k) : frd X true (wI (hrD D) i) = X.bwd i := by rw [frd_w]; rfl
theorem frd_wf (i : Fin D.R.k) : frd X false (wI (hrD D) i) = X.bw (rv i) := by rw [frd_w]; rfl

variable {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)}

theorem wq (hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {c : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F}
    (h : ZMod' X.B.I X.B.g c) : c ∈ Wq X.B.I w := zmod_Wq hg h

theorem wqs (hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal) {c : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F}
    (h : ZMod' X.B.I X.B.g c) : sg X.B.I c ∈ Wq X.B.I w := zmod_Wq hg (zmod_sg h)

theorem xI_inj {a b : Fin D.R.rS} : xI (hrD D) a = xI (hrD D) b ↔ a = b :=
  ⟨fun h => Fin.ext (by have := congrArg Fin.val h; simpa using this), fun h => h ▸ rfl⟩
theorem wI_inj {a b : Fin D.R.k} : wI (hrD D) a = wI (hrD D) b ↔ a = b :=
  ⟨fun h => Fin.ext (by have := congrArg Fin.val h; simpa using this), fun h => h ▸ rfl⟩
theorem yI_inj {a b : Fin D.R.rS} : yI (hrD D) a = yI (hrD D) b ↔ a = b :=
  ⟨fun h => Fin.ext (by have := congrArg Fin.val h; simpa using this), fun h => h ▸ rfl⟩

theorem gram (ob : Bool) (hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal)
    (hew : X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) (hsew : sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) :
    GramW w (fr X ob) (frd X ob) := by
  intro i j
  have sg_ite : ∀ (P : Prop) [Decidable P],
      sg X.B.I (if P then (1 : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) else 0) = if P then 1 else 0 := by
    intro P _; split_ifs <;> simp
  rcases kind_cases (hrD D) i with ⟨a, rfl⟩ | ⟨a, rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨a, rfl⟩ <;>
  rcases kind_cases (hrD D) j with ⟨b, rfl⟩ | ⟨b, rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨b, rfl⟩
  ·
    rw [dual_frame_isotropic, frame_isotropic]
    have h := wqs X hg ((X.gramIntegral b a).2.2)
    rw [map_sub, sg_hA, sg_ite] at h
    exact gram_of h (by rw [xI_inj]; exact eq_comm)
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [dual_frame_isotropic, fr_wf]; exact wq X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv b)).2.2 a).2.2.2
    · rw [dual_frame_isotropic, fr_wt]; exact wq X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral b).2.2 a).2.1
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [dual_frame_isotropic, frame_anisotropic]; exact wq X hg (X.formsAtZero.2 a).2
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [dual_frame_isotropic, fr_y]; exact wq X hg (X.gramIntegral a (rv b)).2.1
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [frd_wf, frame_isotropic]; have := wqs X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv a)).2.2 b).1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
    · rw [frd_wt, frame_isotropic]; have := wqs X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral a).2.2 b).2.2.1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
  ·
    cases ob
    · rw [frd_wf, fr_wf]
      simp only [Bool.false_eq_true, if_false] at hsew
      have h := wqs X hg (X.bw_dual (rv b) (rv a))
      rw [map_sub, sg_hA] at h
      by_cases hab : a = b
      · subst hab
        rw [if_pos rfl] at h ⊢
        have := add_mem h hsew
        convert this using 1; ring
      · rw [if_neg (fun h' => hab (rv_inj.1 h'.symm)), map_zero, sub_zero] at h
        exact gram_ne h (fun h' => hab (wI_inj.1 h'))
    · rw [frd_wt, fr_wt]
      simp only [if_true] at hew
      have h := wq X hg (X.bw_dual a b)
      by_cases hab : a = b
      · subst hab
        rw [if_pos rfl] at h ⊢
        change hhat X.B.I (X.bwd a) (X.bw a) - X.ew ∈ _ at h
        have := add_mem h hew
        convert this using 1; ring
      · rw [if_neg hab, sub_zero] at h
        exact gram_ne h (fun h' => hab (wI_inj.1 h'))
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [frd_wf, frame_anisotropic]; have := wqs X hg (X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv a)).1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
    · rw [frd_wt, frame_anisotropic]; have := wqs X hg (X.weightVectorsIntegral a).2.1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [frd_wf, fr_y]; have := wqs X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv a)).2.2 (rv b)).2.1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
    · rw [frd_wt, fr_y]; have := wqs X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral a).2.2 (rv b)).2.2.2; rwa [sg_hA] at this
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, frame_isotropic, hhat_neg_left]
    refine neg_mem ?_
    have := wqs X hg (X.formsAtZero.2 b).1; rwa [sg_hA] at this
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, fr_wf, hhat_neg_left]; exact neg_mem (wq X hg (X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv b)).2.1)
    · rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, fr_wt, hhat_neg_left]; exact neg_mem (wq X hg (X.weightVectorsIntegral b).1)
  ·
    rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, frame_anisotropic, hhat_neg_left, if_pos rfl]
    have := neg_mem (wq X hg X.formsAtZero.1)
    convert this using 1
    show -hhat X.B.I X.z0 X.z0 - 1 = -(hA X.B.I X.z0 X.z0 + 1)
    rw [show hA X.B.I X.z0 X.z0 = hhat X.B.I X.z0 X.z0 from rfl]; ring
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, fr_y, hhat_neg_left]
    refine neg_mem ?_
    have := wqs X hg (X.formsAtZero.2 (rv b)).2; rwa [sg_hA] at this
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [frd_y, frame_isotropic]; exact wq X hg (X.gramIntegral (rv a) b).1
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    · rw [frd_y, fr_wf]; exact wq X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral (rv b)).2.2 (rv a)).2.2.1
    · rw [frd_y, fr_wt]; exact wq X hg ((X.weightVectorsIntegral b).2.2 (rv a)).1
  ·
    refine gram_ne ?_ (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rw [frd_y, frame_anisotropic]; exact wq X hg (X.formsAtZero.2 (rv a)).1
  ·
    rw [frd_y, fr_y]
    exact gram_of (wq X hg (X.gramIntegral (rv a) (rv b)).2.2) (by rw [rv_inj, yI_inj])

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppLoc

  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsKpp

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem fr_L (ob : Bool) (j : Fin D.R.r) : fr X ob j ∈ X.B.I.Lf := by
  rcases kind_cases (hrD D) j with ⟨a, rfl⟩ | ⟨a, rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨a, rfl⟩
  · rw [frame_isotropic]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.1 a).1
  · cases ob
    · rw [fr_wf]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).2
    · rw [fr_wt]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).1
  · rw [frame_anisotropic]; exact X.vectorsInLattice.2.1
  · rw [fr_y]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.1 _).2

theorem frd_L (ob : Bool) (j : Fin D.R.r) : frd X ob j ∈ X.B.I.Lf := by
  rcases kind_cases (hrD D) j with ⟨a, rfl⟩ | ⟨a, rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨a, rfl⟩
  · rw [dual_frame_isotropic]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.1 a).2
  · cases ob
    · rw [frd_wf]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).1
    · rw [frd_wt]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).2
  · rw [dual_frame_anisotropic]; exact neg_mem X.vectorsInLattice.2.1
  · rw [frd_y]; exact (X.vectorsInLattice.1 _).1

variable {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)} {p : ℕ}

theorem col (ob : Bool) (hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal)
    (hew : X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) (hsew : sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I w)
    {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ X.K2) (i j : Fin D.R.r)
    (hij : j.1 ≤ i.1 ∨ ((∃ l, i = wI (hrD D) l) ∧ j = zI (hrD D))) :
    CongW (w := w) p (hhat X.B.I (frd X ob i) (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ fr X ob j)) (if i = j then 1 else 0) := by
  have hc : K2cong X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw x := hx.2
  have hci : K2cong X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw x⁻¹ := ((Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.level_subgroup_character_unitary X).2.2.1 x hx).2
  have hgr := gram X ob hg hew hsew
  rcases kind_cases (hrD D) j with ⟨b, rfl⟩ | ⟨l, rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨b, rfl⟩
  ·
    have hpos : b.1 ≤ i.1 := by
      rcases hij with h | ⟨-, h⟩
      · simpa using h
      · exact absurd h (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    refine cong_dir hg (frd_L X ob) hgr i (xI (hrD D) b) (S := X.e0 '' {c | c.1 < b.1}) ?_ ?_
    · rintro s ⟨c, hc', rfl⟩
      exact ⟨xI (hrD D) c, (frame_isotropic X ob c).symm, ne_val (by simp at hc' ⊢; omega)⟩
    · rw [frame_isotropic]; exact hc.1 b
  ·
    have hpos : D.R.rS + l.1 ≤ i.1 := by
      rcases hij with h | ⟨-, h⟩
      · simpa using h
      · exact absurd h (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    cases ob
    ·
      rcases kind_cases (hrD D) i with ⟨a, rfl⟩ | ⟨l', rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨a, rfl⟩
      · exfalso; simp at hpos; omega
      · simp at hpos
        refine cong_dual hg (fr_L X false) hgr (wI (hrD D) l') (wI (hrD D) l)
          (S := Set.range X.e0 ∪ X.bw '' {c | c.1 < (rv l').1}) ?_ ?_
        · rintro s (⟨c, rfl⟩ | ⟨c, hc', rfl⟩)
          · exact ⟨yI (hrD D) (rv c), by rw [frd_y, rv_rv], ne_val (by simp <;> omega)⟩
          · exact ⟨wI (hrD D) (rv c), by rw [frd_wf, rv_rv], ne_val (by simp at hc' ⊢; omega)⟩
        · rw [frd_wf]; exact hci.2.2 (rv l')
      · refine cong_dual hg (fr_L X false) hgr (zI (hrD D)) (wI (hrD D) l) (S := Set.range X.e0) ?_ ?_
        · rintro s ⟨c, rfl⟩
          exact ⟨yI (hrD D) (rv c), by rw [frd_y, rv_rv], ne_val (by simp <;> omega)⟩
        · rw [dual_frame_anisotropic, Matrix.mulVec_neg]; exact inMod_neg hci.2.1
      · refine cong_dual hg (fr_L X false) hgr (yI (hrD D) a) (wI (hrD D) l)
          (S := X.e0 '' {c | c.1 < (rv a).1}) ?_ ?_
        · rintro s ⟨c, -, rfl⟩
          exact ⟨yI (hrD D) (rv c), by rw [frd_y, rv_rv], ne_val (by simp <;> omega)⟩
        · rw [frd_y]; exact hci.1 (rv a)
    ·
      refine cong_dir hg (frd_L X true) hgr i (wI (hrD D) l) (S := Set.range X.e0 ∪ X.bw '' {c | c.1 < l.1}) ?_ ?_
      · rintro s (⟨c, rfl⟩ | ⟨c, hc', rfl⟩)
        · exact ⟨xI (hrD D) c, (frame_isotropic X true c).symm, ne_val (by simp; omega)⟩
        · exact ⟨wI (hrD D) c, (fr_wt X c).symm, ne_val (by simp at hc' ⊢; omega)⟩
      · rw [fr_wt]; exact hc.2.2 l
  ·
    have hpos : D.R.rS ≤ i.1 := by

    · rintro s ⟨c, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨xI (hrD D) c, (frame_isotropic X ob c).symm, ne_val (by simp; omega)⟩
    · rw [frame_anisotropic]; exact hc.2.1
  ·
    have hpos : D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 + b.1 ≤ i.1 := by
      rcases hij with h | ⟨-, h⟩
      · simpa using h
      · exact absurd h (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))
    rcases kind_cases (hrD D) i with ⟨a, rfl⟩ | ⟨l', rfl⟩ | rfl | ⟨a, rfl⟩
    · exfalso; simp at hpos; omega
    · exfalso; simp at hpos; omega
    · exfalso; simp at hpos; omega
    · simp at hpos
      refine cong_dual hg (fr_L X ob) hgr (yI (hrD D) a) (yI (hrD D) b)
        (S := X.e0 '' {c | c.1 < (rv a).1}) ?_ ?_
      · rintro s ⟨c, hc', rfl⟩
        exact ⟨yI (hrD D) (rv c), by rw [frd_y, rv_rv], ne_val (by simp at hc' ⊢; omega)⟩
      · rw [frd_y]; exact hci.1 (rv a)

theorem shape (ob : Bool) (hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ w.asIdeal)
    (hew : X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) (hsew : sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I w)
    (hp : p.Prime) (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 X.B.I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) (hn : 0 < X.B.I.n)
    {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ X.K2) {N : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)}
    (hN : ∀ i j, CongW (w := w) p (hhat X.B.I (frd X ob i) (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ fr X ob j)) (N i j)) :
    JPred D.R.k D.R.rS N := by
  refine jPred_of (hrD D) (fun a b hba => ?_) (fun a => ?_) (fun l => ?_)
  · have h := col (p := p) X ob hg hew hsew hx a b (Or.inl (le_of_lt hba))
    rw [if_neg (fun h' => (ne_of_gt hba) h')] at h
    exact congW_unique hp hcard hn (hN a b) h
  · have h := col (p := p) X ob hg hew hsew hx a a (Or.inl le_rfl)
    rw [if_pos rfl] at h
    exact congW_unique hp hcard hn (hN a a) h
  · have h := col (p := p) X ob hg hew hsew hx (wI (hrD D) l) (zI (hrD D)) (Or.inr ⟨⟨l, rfl⟩, rfl⟩)
    rw [if_neg (ne_val (by simp <;> omega))] at h
    exact congW_unique hp hcard hn (hN _ _) h

[ARO_level_shape_06] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/KppIdx.lean:1-106
Range SHA256 e32d210b49dad61c280a48eb69476556cf29f806902cd349302fd39331de9d10
import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.AllRowsKpp

noncomputable section

open Matrix Finset

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppIdx

open Formalism.MainNotions.ArithmeticCylinders Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsKpp

variable {s kk r : ℕ}

def xI (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (a : Fin s) : Fin r := ⟨a.1, by omega⟩
def wI (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (i : Fin kk) : Fin r := ⟨s + i.1, by omega⟩
def zI (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) : Fin r := ⟨s + kk, by omega⟩
def yI (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (a : Fin s) : Fin r := ⟨s + kk + 1 + a.1, by omega⟩

theorem kind_cases (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (j : Fin r) :
    (∃ a, j = xI hr a) ∨ (∃ i, j = wI hr i) ∨ j = zI hr ∨ ∃ a, j = yI hr a := by
  rcases lt_or_ge j.1 s with h | h
  · exact Or.inl ⟨⟨j.1, h⟩, Fin.ext rfl⟩
  rcases lt_or_ge j.1 (s + kk) with h' | h'
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl ⟨⟨j.1 - s, by omega⟩, Fin.ext (by show j.1 = s + (j.1 - s); omega)⟩)
  rcases eq_or_lt_of_le h' with h'' | h''
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inl (Fin.ext (by show j.1 = s + kk; omega))))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨⟨j.1 - (s + kk + 1), by omega⟩,
      Fin.ext (by show j.1 = s + kk + 1 + (j.1 - (s + kk + 1)); omega)⟩))

@[simp] theorem xI_val (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (a : Fin s) : (xI hr a).1 = a.1 := rfl
@[simp] theorem wI_val (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (i : Fin kk) : (wI hr i).1 = s + i.1 := rfl
@[simp] theorem zI_val (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) : (zI hr).1 = s + kk := rfl
@[simp] theorem yI_val (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) (a : Fin s) : (yI hr a).1 = s + kk + 1 + a.1 := rfl

variable {g : ℕ}

theorem jPred_of (hr : r = kk + 2 * s + 1) {N : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g)}
    (hlow : ∀ a b : Fin r, b < a → N a b = 0) (hdiag : ∀ a, N a a = 1) (hwz : ∀ i, N (wI hr i) (zI hr) = 0) :
    JPred kk s N := by
  refine ⟨fun a b => ⟨hlow a b, fun h => h ▸ hdiag a⟩, fun a ha1 ha2 => ?_⟩
  unfold entN
  rw [dif_pos ⟨by omega, by omega⟩]
  have := hwz ⟨a - s, by omega⟩
  convert this using 2 <;> ext <;> simp <;> omega

theorem low_of {N : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g)} (hJ : JPred kk s N) (a b : Fin r) (h : b < a) : N a b = 0 :=
  (hJ.1 a b).1 h

theorem diag_of {N : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g)} (hJ : JPred kk s N) (a : Fin r) : N a a = 1 :=
  (hJ.1 a a).2 rfl

theorem inv_super {N N' : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g)} (hJ : JPred kk s N) (hJ' : JPred kk s N')
    (h1 : N * N' = 1) (i : ℕ) (hi : i + 1 < r) : entN N' i (i + 1) = -entN N i (i + 1) := by
  have e := congrFun (congrFun h1 ⟨i, by omega⟩) ⟨i + 1, hi⟩
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.one_apply_ne (by intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this)] at e
  have hne : (⟨i, by omega⟩ : Fin r) ≠ ⟨i + 1, hi⟩ := by intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_add (⟨i, by omega⟩ : Fin r) ⟨i + 1, hi⟩ hne (fun c _ hc => ?_) (by simp) (by simp)] at e
  · rw [diag_of hJ, diag_of hJ', one_mul, mul_one] at e
    unfold entN
    rw [dif_pos ⟨by omega, hi⟩, dif_pos ⟨by omega, hi⟩]
    linear_combination e
  · rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne (show c.1 ≠ i from fun h => hc.1 (Fin.ext h)) with h | h
    · rw [low_of hJ _ _ (show c < ⟨i, by omega⟩ from h), zero_mul]
    · have : i + 1 < c.1 := by
        rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne (show c.1 ≠ i + 1 from fun h => hc.2 (Fin.ext h)) with h' | h'
        · omega
        · exact h'
      rw [low_of hJ' _ _ (show (⟨i + 1, hi⟩ : Fin r) < c from this), mul_zero]

def dN (s kk : ℕ) (ob : Bool) (j : ℕ) : ZMod g :=
  if s + kk + 1 ≤ j then (-1) ^ (j - (s + kk + 1))
  else if ob = false ∧ s ≤ j ∧ j < s + kk then (-1) ^ (j - s) else 1

theorem dN_sq (ob : Bool) (j : ℕ) : dN (g := g) s kk ob j * dN s kk ob j = 1 := by
  unfold dN
  split_ifs <;> simp [← pow_add, ← two_mul, pow_mul]

theorem index_diag_isotropic (ob : Bool) {j : ℕ} (h : j < s) : dN (g := g) s kk ob j = 1 := by
  unfold dN; rw [if_neg (by omega), if_neg (fun h' => absurd h'.2.1 (by omega))]

theorem dN_wt {j : ℕ} (h1 : s ≤ j) (h2 : j < s + kk) : dN (g := g) s kk true j = 1 := by
  unfold dN; rw [if_neg (by omega), if_neg (fun h' => absurd h'.1 (by simp))]

theorem dN_wf {j : ℕ} (h1 : s ≤ j) (h2 : j < s + kk) : dN (g := g) s kk false j = (-1) ^ (j - s) := by
  unfold dN; rw [if_neg (by omega), if_pos ⟨rfl, h1, h2⟩]

theorem dN_add_eq_one (ob : Bool) : dN (g := g) s kk ob (s + kk) = 1 := by
  unfold dN; rw [if_neg (by omega), if_neg (fun h' => absurd h'.2.2 (by omega))]

theorem dN_y (ob : Bool) {j : ℕ} (h : s + kk + 1 ≤ j) : dN (g := g) s kk ob j = (-1) ^ (j - (s + kk + 1)) := by
  unfold dN; rw [if_pos h]

theorem neg_one_sq (i : ℕ) : ((-1 : ZMod g) ^ i) * (-1) ^ i = 1 := by
  rw [← pow_add, ← two_mul, pow_mul]; simp

def dM (s kk : ℕ) (ob : Bool) : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g) := Matrix.diagonal fun j => dN s kk ob j.1

theorem dM_sq (ob : Bool) : dM (r := r) (g := g) s kk ob * dM s kk ob = 1 := by
  rw [dM, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, ← Matrix.diagonal_one]
  congr 1
  funext j
  exact dN_sq ob j.1

theorem entN_conj (ob : Bool) (N : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod g)) (a b : ℕ) :
    entN (dM s kk ob * N * dM s kk ob) a b = dN s kk ob a * entN N a b * dN s kk ob b := by
  unfold entN

  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppFrameW Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppIdx Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppLoc Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppChar Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRho
  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRhoX Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsKpp

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem n_pos : 0 < X.B.I.n := by
  rw [X.n_eq, hrD D]
  omega

theorem Wq_of_smul {I : AutInst} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)} {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) (hn : 0 < I.n) {t : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} (ht : t ∈ IntAf I.F I.E)
    (h : ∀ y ∈ I.Lf, t • y ∈ Lq I w) : t ∈ Wq I w := by
  obtain ⟨m, c, -, hc⟩ := congW_exists hp hcard ht
  have hcy : ∀ y ∈ I.Lf, ((c : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • y ∈ Lq I w := by
    intro y hy
    have h1 := (Lq I w).sub_mem (h y hy) (Wq_smul hc hy)
    rwa [sub_smul, sub_sub_cancel] at h1
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf.exists_redBasis I w hp hcard
  set i0 : Fin I.n := ⟨0, hn⟩
  have hv : iv I (∑ j, (((Pi.single i0 c : Fin I.n → ℤ) j : ℤ) : I.E) • b j) ∈ lamAf (F := I.F) (wLat0 I w) := by
    rw [iv_sum_int]
    have hs : ∑ j, (((Pi.single i0 c : Fin I.n → ℤ) j : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b j) =
        ((c : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • iv I (b i0) := by
      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i0]
      · simp
      · intro j _ hj
        simp [hj]
      · intro h0
        exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h0
    rw [hs]
    exact hcy _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨⟨b i0, hb.1 i0⟩, rfl⟩)
  have hdvd : (p : ℤ) ∣ c := by
    have := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf.redBasis_indep I w hp hcard hb (Pi.single i0 c : Fin I.n → ℤ)
      (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RedBasicsPf.inter I w hv) i0
    simpa using this
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := hdvd
  have hcW : ((p * k : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) ∈ Wq I w := by
    push_cast
    exact Wq_mul' (natCast_Wq hp hcard) (intCast_int k)
  have := (Wq I w).add_mem hc hcW
  simpa using this

private theorem congW_sub_mem_of_prime_residue_card {I : AutInst} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 I.E)} {p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime)
    (hcard : Nat.card (𝓞 I.E ⧸ w.asIdeal) = p) {t : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F} {c : ℕ}
    (h : CongW (w := w) p t (c : ZMod p)) : t - (c : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) ∈ Wq I w :=
  Wq_of_congW_zero hp hcard (by simpa using congW_sub h (congW_natCast (w := w) (p := p) c))

theorem ew_res (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) :
    ∃ ob : Bool, X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) ∧
      sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) := by
  have hp := D.host.g_prime kv.1
  have hcard := card_wK X kv
  have hn := n_pos X
  have : Fact (D.host.g kv.1).Prime := ⟨hp⟩
  have hsI : sg X.B.I X.ew ∈ IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E := by
    obtain ⟨m0, c, -, hc⟩ := congW_exists (w := wc X.B.I (wK X kv)) hp (card_wKc X kv) X.ew_int
    have h1 := sg_Wq hc
    rw [wc_wc, map_sub, sg_intCast] at h1
    have h2 := (IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E).add_mem (Wq_int h1) (intCast_int c)
    simpa using h2
  have hA : 1 - X.ew - sg X.B.I X.ew ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) := by
    refine Wq_of_smul hp hcard hn ?_ ?_
    · exact (IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E).sub_mem
        ((IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E).sub_mem (IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E).one_mem X.ew_int) hsI
    · intro y hy
      exact mL_le (level_mem_wB' X kv) (X.ew_full y hy)
  have key : ∀ ob : Bool, sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) =
      -(1 - X.ew - sg X.B.I X.ew) - (X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0)) := by
    intro ob
    cases ob <;> simp only [Bool.false_eq_true, ↓reduceIte] <;> ring
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := congW_exists (w := wK X kv) hp hcard X.ew_int
  have hmm : m * m = m := by
    have h1 := congW_mul X.ew_int hm hm
    rw [X.ew_idem] at h1
    exact congW_unique hp hcard hn h1 hm
  have h01 : m = 0 ∨ m = 1 := by
    have h1 : m * (m - 1) = 0 := by rw [mul_sub, mul_one, hmm, sub_self]
    rcases mul_eq_zero.mp h1 with h | h
    · exact Or.inl h
    · exact Or.inr (sub_eq_zero.mp h)
  have hfin : ∀ ob : Bool, X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) →
      ∃ ob : Bool, X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) ∧
        sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I (wK X kv) := by
    intro ob hob
    refine ⟨ob, hob, ?_⟩
    rw [key ob]
    exact (Wq X.B.I (wK X kv)).sub_mem ((Wq X.B.I (wK X kv)).neg_mem hA) hob
  rcases h01 with h0 | h1
  · refine hfin false ?_
    have := congW_sub_mem_of_prime_residue_card hp hcard (c := 0) (by simpa [h0] using hm)
    simpa using this
  · refine hfin true ?_
    have := congW_sub_mem_of_prime_residue_card hp hcard (c := 1) (by simpa [h1] using hm)
    simpa using this

private theorem exists_conj_rhoX_JPred_of_mem_K2 (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) :
    ∃ Pm : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)), ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      JPred D.R.k D.R.rS ((Pm⁻¹ * rhoX X x kv * Pm : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
        Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) ∧
      ∃ t1 t2 : ZMod (D.host.g kv.1), CongW (w := wK X kv) (D.host.g kv.1) (argC X x) t1 ∧
        CongW (w := wc X.B.I (wK X kv)) (D.host.g kv.1) (argC X x) t2 ∧
        t1 + t2 = phiJ D.R.k D.R.rS ((Pm⁻¹ * rhoX X x kv * Pm : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
          Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) := by
  have hp := D.host.g_prime kv.1
  have hcard := card_wK X kv
  have hn := n_pos X
  have hg := level_mem_wB' X kv
  have : NeZero (D.host.g kv.1) := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨ob, hew, hsew⟩ := ew_res X kv
  have hgr := gram X ob hg hew hsew
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := exists_lifts hp hcard (fr_L X ob)
  have hcL : ∀ j, c j ∈ X.B.I.L := fun j => (hc j).1
  have hcq : ∀ j, fr X ob j - iv X.B.I (c j) ∈ Lq X.B.I (wK X kv) := fun j => (hc j).2
  have hgc := gram_lift (frd_L X ob) hgr hcq
  have hbc : IsRedBasis0 X.B.I (wK X kv) c :=
    isRedBasis_of_gram hp hcard X.n_eq.symm (frd_L X ob) hcL hgc
  obtain ⟨P0, Q0, hPQ, hQP, hconj⟩ := conj_basis hp hcard X.n_eq.symm (redBX X kv).hb hbc
  have hdd : (dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) *
      dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob = 1 := dM_sq ob
  have hPm : ∃ Pm : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)),
      (Pm : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) = P0 * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob ∧
      ((Pm⁻¹ : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) =
        dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * Q0 := by
    refine ⟨⟨P0 * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob, dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * Q0, ?_, ?_⟩, rfl, rfl⟩
    · rw [mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc (dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob), hdd, one_mul, hPQ]
    · rw [mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc Q0, hQP, one_mul, hdd]
  obtain ⟨Pm, hPm1, hPm2⟩ := hPm
  refine ⟨Pm, fun x hx => ?_⟩
  have hxK : (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kset := hx.1
  have hxi : (x : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ ∈ X.K2 := (Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.level_subgroup_is_group X).2.2.1 _ hx
  have hxiK : (x : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ ∈ X.B.I.Kset := hxi.1
  have hM : RedMatB X.B.I (wK X kv) (D.host.g kv.1) (redBX X kv).b (x : X.B.I.Gf)
      ((rhoX X x kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
        Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) := by
    rw [rhoX_apply]
    exact rhoB_spec (redBX X kv) x
  obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := redMatB_exists X.B.I (wK X kv) hp hcard hbc hxK
  obtain ⟨N', hN'⟩ := redMatB_exists X.B.I (wK X kv) hp hcard hbc hxiK
  have hMN := hconj _ hxK _ N hM hN
  have hval : ((Pm⁻¹ * rhoX X x kv * Pm : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
      Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) =
      dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * N * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob := by
    rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, hPm1, hPm2]
    calc dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * Q0 * ((rhoX X x kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
            Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) * (P0 * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob)
        = dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * Q0 * (((rhoX X x kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
            Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) * P0) * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob := by
          simp only [mul_assoc]
      _ = dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * (Q0 * P0) * N * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob := by
          rw [hMN]
          simp only [mul_assoc]
      _ = dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * N * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob := by
          rw [hQP, mul_one]
  have hW := entry (frd_L X ob) hcL hcq hgc hxK hN
  have hW' := entry (frd_L X ob) hcL hcq hgc hxiK hN'
  have hJ := shape X ob hg hew hsew hp hcard hn hx hW
  have hJ' := shape X ob hg hew hsew hp hcard hn hxi hW'
  have hmul : N * N' = 1 := by
    have h1 := redMatB_mul X.B.I (wK X kv) hp hcard hcL hxK hN hN'
    rw [mul_inv_cancel] at h1
    exact redMatB_unique X.B.I (wK X kv) hp hcard X.n_eq.symm hbc h1 (redMatB_one X.B.I (wK X kv) hp c)
  have hinv := inv_super hJ hJ' hmul
  rw [hval]
  exact ⟨jPred_conj (hrD D) ob hJ, tW (D := D) ob N, tWc (D := D) ob N', congC X ob hew hxK hW,
    congCc X ob hsew hxK hW', sum_eq ob hinv⟩

theorem kppFwd_holds : KppFwd X := by
  have hs : D.R.m / 2 - 1 = D.R.rS := by
    have h1 := r_eq D
    have h2 := hrD D
    omega
  choose Pm hPm using exists_conj_rhoX_JPred_of_mem_K2 X
  choose ε hε1 hεP using exists_eps X
  have hε : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k),
      ε kv ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)} :=
    fun kv => eps_other X kv (hεP kv) (1 - kv.1) (other_ne kv.1)
  unfold KppFwd
  refine ⟨Pm, fun kv => psiK X (ε kv) (hε kv), fun kv => psiK_ne X (hε1 kv) (hεP kv) (hε kv), ?_⟩
  intro x hx
  rw [hs]
  choose t1 t2 h1 h2 h12 using fun kv => (hPm kv x hx).2
  refine ⟨fun kv => (hPm kv x hx).1, ?_⟩
  rw [quadratic_character_eq_additive X (x : X.B.I.Gf), chi_split X ε hε1 hεP hε (n_pos X) (argC_int X hx.1) t1 t2 h1 h2]
  exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => by rw [h12 kv]

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppFwdPf

variable {D : D7}

abbrev cjM (P γ : G0g D.nd) (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) :=
  (((P kv)⁻¹ * γ kv * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)))

def KppShape (X : HidaSetting D) : Prop :=
  ∃ (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ), (∀ kv, ψ kv ≠ 1) ∧
    ∀ x : KSub X.B.I,
      ((x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 ↔ ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv)) ∧
      ((x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 → X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv)))

def KppFwd (X : HidaSetting D) : Prop :=
  ∃ (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ), (∀ kv, ψ kv ≠ 1) ∧
    ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      (∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv)) ∧
      X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))

theorem blk_mono (rS k : ℕ) {i j : ℕ} (h : i ≤ j) : Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk rS k i ≤ Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk rS k j := by
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk
  split_ifs <;> omega

theorem jPred_iff (kk s : ℕ) {N g : ℕ} (hN : N = kk + 2 * s + 1) (M : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :
    JPred kk s M ↔ Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.IsRowLev kk s M := by
  subst hN
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hU, hz⟩
    refine ⟨fun i j hij => ?_, fun i => (hU i i).2 rfl, fun i j _ hji => (hU i j).1 hji,
      fun i j hj hij hbi => ?_⟩
    · have hji : j < i := by
        by_contra h
        push_neg at h
        have := blk_mono s kk (Fin.le_def.1 h)
        omega
      exact (hU i j).1 hji
    · have hne : i.val ≠ s + kk := fun h => hij (Fin.ext (h.trans hj.symm))
      have hi1 : s ≤ i.val ∧ i.val < s + kk := by
        unfold Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk at hbi
        split_ifs at hbi with h1 h2 <;> omega
      have h5 := hz i.val hi1.1 hi1.2
      unfold entN at h5
      rw [dif_pos ⟨i.isLt, by omega⟩] at h5
      have hj' : j = ⟨s + kk, by omega⟩ := Fin.ext hj
      rw [hj']
      exact h5
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4⟩
    refine ⟨fun a b => ⟨fun hba => ?_, fun hab => by subst hab; exact h2 a⟩, fun a ha1 ha2 => ?_⟩
    · have hle := blk_mono s kk (Fin.le_def.1 hba.le)
      rcases lt_or_eq_of_le hle with h | h
      · exact h1 a b h
      · exact h3 a b h.symm hba
    · unfold entN
      rw [dif_pos ⟨by omega, by omega⟩]
      refine h4 _ _ rfl ?_ ?_
      · intro h
        have := congrArg Fin.val h
        simp at this
        omega
      · show Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk s kk a = s
        unfold Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.blk
        split_ifs <;> omega

theorem r_eq (D : D7) : D.R.r = D.R.k + 2 * (D.R.m / 2 - 1) + 1 := by
  have h1 := D.R.m_even
  have h2 := D.R.two_le_m
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := h1
  show D.R.k + D.R.m - 1 = _
  omega

def JJ (P : G0g D.nd) : Finset (G0g D.nd) :=
  Finset.univ.filter fun γ => ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P γ kv)

def cJ (D : D7) (g : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  Nat.card {A : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod g) // Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.IsRowLev D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1)
    (A : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod g))}

theorem levelSubgroup_relIndex_eq (hS : Function.Surjective (rhoX X)) :
    (KgSub X).relIndex (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) = cJ D (D.host.g 0) ^ D.R.f * cJ D (D.host.g 1) ^ D.R.f := by
  have : X.B.μf.IsHaarMeasure := X.B.haar
  have hopen : IsOpen ((KgSub X : Set X.B.I.Gf)) := X.B.I.topf_open X.B.g (g_pos X)
  have hc1 := idx1 hS
  have hpos1 : (KgSub X).relIndex (KSub X.B.I) ≠ 0 := by rw [hc1]; exact Fintype.card_ne_zero
  have hmul := Subgroup.relIndex_mul_relIndex (KgSub X) (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) (KSub X.B.I) (le2 X) (le3 X)
  have hpos2 : (KgSub X).relIndex (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0; rw [h0, zero_mul] at hmul; exact hpos1 hmul.symm
  have hμ1 := Glob_measure_eq_relIndex_mul X.B.μf (KSub X.B.I) (KgSub X) (le1 X) hopen hpos1
  have hμ2 := Glob_measure_eq_relIndex_mul X.B.μf (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) (KgSub X) (le2 X) hopen hpos2
  have hD := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.DstarIdxPf.dstarIdx_holds X
  unfold Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.DstarIdx at hD
  have e1 : (X.B.μf X.B.I.Kset).toReal = (Fintype.card (G0g D.nd) : ℝ) * V X := by
    have : X.B.μf X.B.I.Kset = X.B.μf (KSub X.B.I : Set X.B.I.Gf) := rfl
    rw [this, hμ1, ENNReal.toReal_mul, hc1, ENNReal.toReal_natCast]
    rfl
  have e2 : X.volK2 = ((KgSub X).relIndex (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) : ℝ) * V X := by
    unfold HidaSetting.volK2
    have : X.B.μf X.K2 = X.B.μf (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X : Set X.B.I.Gf) := rfl
    rw [this, hμ2, ENNReal.toReal_mul, ENNReal.toReal_natCast]
    rfl
  rw [e1, e2] at hD
  unfold Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.idxSig Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.idxRowLoc Formalism.MainNotions.StandardLattice.idxLoc at hD
  rw [natCard_codeWordStages_finiteGroup_eq] at hD
  have hV := V_pos X
  have hJ0 := cJ_pos D 0
  have hJ1 := cJ_pos D 1
  have hG0 : 0 < Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) := Nat.card_pos
  have hG1 : 0 < Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) := Nat.card_pos
  have hD' : (((Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) ^ D.R.f *
      Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) ^ D.R.f : ℕ) : ℝ) * V X) /
      (((KgSub X).relIndex (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) : ℝ) * V X) =
      ((Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) : ℝ) / (cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)) ^ D.R.f *
      ((Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) : ℝ) / (cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)) ^ D.R.f := hD
  have hc2 : (0 : ℝ) < ((KgSub X).relIndex (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X) : ℝ) := by exact_mod_cast Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hpos2
  rw [mul_div_mul_right _ _ hV.ne', div_pow, div_pow, div_mul_div_comm,
    div_eq_div_iff hc2.ne' (by positivity)] at hD'
  push_cast at hD'
  have hne : ((Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 0))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f *
      (Nat.card (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g 1))) : ℝ) ^ D.R.f) ≠ 0 := by positivity
  have key := mul_left_cancel₀ hne hD'
  exact_mod_cast key.symm

theorem kppShape_of_fwd (hS : Function.Surjective (rhoX X)) (hF : KppFwd X) : KppShape X := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨P, ψ, hψ, hF⟩ := hF
  refine ⟨P, ψ, hψ, fun x => ⟨⟨fun hx => (hF x hx).1, fun hJ => ?_⟩, fun hx => (hF x hx).2⟩⟩
  set R : Finset (G0g D.nd) := Finset.univ.filter fun γ => γ ∈ (rho2 X).range with hR
  have hRsub : R ⊆ JJ P := by
    intro γ hγ
    rw [hR, Finset.mem_filter] at hγ
    obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := hγ.2
    unfold JJ
    rw [Finset.mem_filter]
    exact ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, (hF (Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y) y.2).1⟩
  have hRcard : R.card = (JJ P).card := by
    rw [card_JJ, ← levelSubgroup_relIndex_eq hS, idx2, hR, Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, ← Fintype.card_subtype]
  have hEq : R = JJ P := Finset.eq_of_subset_of_card_le hRsub hRcard.ge
  have hmem : rhoX X x ∈ R := by
    rw [hEq]; unfold JJ; rw [Finset.mem_filter]; exact ⟨Finset.mem_univ _, hJ⟩
  rw [hR, Finset.mem_filter] at hmem
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hmem.2
  have h1 : rhoX X ((Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y)⁻¹ * x) = 1 := by
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, ← hy]
    show (rhoX X (Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y))⁻¹ * rhoX X (Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y) = 1
    exact inv_mul_cancel _
  have h2 := (rhoX_eq_one_iff X _).1 h1
  have h3 : ((Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y)⁻¹ * x : KSub X.B.I).1 ∈ concreteLevelAdelicSubset X := le2 X h2
  have h4 : (y : X.B.I.Gf) * ((Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y)⁻¹ * x : KSub X.B.I).1 ∈ concreteLevelAdelicSubset X := (concreteLevelAdelicSubset X).mul_mem y.2 h3
  have e : (y : X.B.I.Gf) * ((Subgroup.inclusion (le3 X) y)⁻¹ * x : KSub X.B.I).1 = x := by
    show (y : X.B.I.Gf) * ((y : X.B.I.Gf)⁻¹ * (x : X.B.I.Gf)) = x
    group
  rw [e] at h4
  exact h4

end Count

theorem over_gk (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) (k : Fin 2) (hP : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal) :
    ∃ v : D.nd.PlG k, P = wK X ⟨k, v⟩ ∨ P = wc X.B.I (wK X ⟨k, v⟩) := by
  have hwH : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ (toH X P).asIdeal := by
    show (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).symm ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 D.host.E) ∈ P.asIdeal
    rw [map_natCast]; exact hP
  obtain ⟨v, hv | hv⟩ := cover X k (toH X P) hwH
  · exact ⟨v, Or.inl (by rw [← wB_toH X P, hv])⟩
  · exact ⟨v, Or.inr (by rw [← wB_toH X P, hv, wB_conjH X])⟩

theorem over_g (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) (hP : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal) :
    ∃ kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k, P = wK X kv ∨ P = wc X.B.I (wK X kv) := by
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := gk_of_g X P.isPrime hP
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := over_gk X P k hk
  exact ⟨⟨k, v⟩, hv⟩

theorem wB_inj {w w' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 D.host.E)} (h : wB X w = wB X w') : w = w' := by
  refine HeightOneSpectrum.ext ?_
  ext a
  obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := (RingOfIntegers.mapRingEquiv X.ιE).surjective a
  rw [← mem_wB, ← mem_wB, h]

theorem wK_ne {kv kv' : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k} (h : kv ≠ kv') : wK X kv ≠ wK X kv' := by
  obtain ⟨k, v⟩ := kv
  obtain ⟨k', v'⟩ := kv'
  intro he
  by_cases hk : k = k'
  · subst hk
    have hv : v ≠ v' := fun hv => h (by rw [hv])
    exact hv (D.naut.plGw_inj k (wB_inj X he))
  · have h0 := gk_mem X ⟨k, v⟩
    have h1 := gk_mem X ⟨k', v'⟩
    rw [← he] at h1
    exact not_both' X _ (wK X ⟨k, v⟩).isPrime.ne_top hk h0 h1

theorem wK_ne_wc (kv kv' : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : wK X kv ≠ wc X.B.I (wK X kv') := by
  obtain ⟨k, v⟩ := kv
  obtain ⟨k', v'⟩ := kv'
  intro he
  by_cases hk : k = k'
  · subst hk
    rw [show wc X.B.I (wK X ⟨k, v'⟩) = wB X (conjH (D.naut.plGw k v')) from (wB_conjH X _).symm] at he
    have he' := wB_inj X he
    apply D.naut.plGw_nconj k v' v
    have := isConjPl_conjH X (D.naut.plGw k v')
    rw [← he'] at this
    exact this
  · have h0 := gk_mem X ⟨k, v⟩
    have h1 := gk_mem_c X ⟨k', v'⟩
    rw [← he] at h1
    exact not_both' X _ (wK X ⟨k, v⟩).isPrime.ne_top hk h0 h1

theorem mem_g_of_all {a : 𝓞 X.B.I.E}
    (ha : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → a ∈ P.asIdeal) :
    ∃ c : 𝓞 X.B.I.E, a = ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * c := by
  have hk : ∀ k : Fin 2, a ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)} := by
    intro k
    refine mem_span_of_all (D.host.g_prime k) (unram X k) fun P hPm hgP => ?_
    let P' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E) := ⟨P, hPm.isPrime, fun hb => by
      rw [hb] at hgP
      exact (by exact_mod_cast (D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero : ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ≠ 0)
        ((Submodule.mem_bot _).1 hgP)⟩
    exact ha P' (g_mem_of_gk X hgP)
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 (hk 0)
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 (hk 1)
  have hcop : IsCoprime ((D.host.g 0 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ((D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) := by
    have h' : IsCoprime ((D.host.g 0 : ℕ) : ℤ) ((D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : ℤ) :=
      Nat.isCoprime_iff_coprime.2 ((Nat.coprime_primes (D.host.g_prime 0) (D.host.g_prime 1)).2 D.host.g_ne)
    simpa using h'.map (Int.castRingHom (𝓞 X.B.I.E))
  obtain ⟨α, β, hαβ⟩ := hcop
  refine ⟨α * v + β * u, ?_⟩
  rw [g_eq_cast]
  calc a = (α * ((D.host.g 0 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) + β * ((D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) * a := by rw [hαβ, one_mul]
    _ = α * ((D.host.g 0 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * (v * ((D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) +
        β * ((D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * (u * ((D.host.g 0 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) := by
          rw [hv, hu]; ring
    _ = _ := by ring

theorem exists_eps (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) :
    ∃ ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E, ε - 1 ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal ∧
      ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → P ≠ wK X kv → ε ∈ P.asIdeal := by
  classical
  let S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) :=
    Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.plOver X.B.I.E (D.host.g 0) ∪ Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.plOver X.B.I.E (D.host.g 1)
  have hS : S.Finite := (Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.finite_plOver X.B.I.E (D.host.g_prime 0)).union
    (Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.finite_plOver X.B.I.E (D.host.g_prime 1))
  have : Finite S := hS.to_subtype
  have hcop : Pairwise (Function.onFun IsCoprime fun P : S => (P.1).asIdeal) := by
    intro P Q hPQ
    rw [Function.onFun, Ideal.isCoprime_iff_sup_eq]
    refine Ideal.IsMaximal.coprime_of_ne (P.1).isMaximal (Q.1).isMaximal fun h => hPQ ?_
    exact Subtype.ext (HeightOneSpectrum.ext h)
  obtain ⟨ε, hε⟩ := Ideal.exists_forall_sub_mem_ideal hcop fun P => if P.1 = wK X kv then 1 else 0
  have hmem : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → P ∈ S := by
    intro P hP
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := gk_of_g X P.isPrime hP
    fin_cases k
    · exact Or.inl hk
    · exact Or.inr hk
  refine ⟨ε, ?_, fun P hP hne => ?_⟩
  · have := hε ⟨wK X kv, hmem _ (g_mem_of_gk X (gk_mem X kv))⟩
    simpa using this
  · have := hε ⟨P, hmem P hP⟩
    simp only [if_neg hne, sub_zero] at this
    exact this

theorem eps_other (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) {ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E}
    (hε : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → P ≠ wK X kv → ε ∈ P.asIdeal)
    (k' : Fin 2) (hk : k' ≠ kv.1) : ε ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g k' : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)} := by
  refine mem_span_of_all (D.host.g_prime k') (unram X k') fun P hPm hgP => ?_
  let P' : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E) := ⟨P, hPm.isPrime, fun hb => by
    rw [hb] at hgP
    exact (by exact_mod_cast (D.host.g_prime k').ne_zero : ((D.host.g k' : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ≠ 0)
      ((Submodule.mem_bot _).1 hgP)⟩
  refine hε P' (g_mem_of_gk X hgP) fun he => ?_
  have h0 := gk_mem X kv
  have h1 : ((D.host.g k' : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by rw [← he]; exact hgP
  exact not_both' X _ (wK X kv).isPrime.ne_top hk h1 h0

theorem divN_add (a b : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) :
    divN X.B.I X.B.g (a + b) = divN X.B.I X.B.g a + divN X.B.I X.B.g b := by
  unfold divN; ring

theorem divN_g (c : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) :
    divN X.B.I X.B.g ((X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) * c) = c := by
  unfold divN

    inv_mul_cancel₀ hg, map_one, one_mul]

  rw [divN_add, X.psiEf_add, divN_g, X.psiEf_int d hd, mul_one]

theorem divN_incl (y : X.B.I.E) : divN X.B.I X.B.g (Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) y) =

  rw [map_inv₀, map_natCast]
  rfl

theorem psiEinf_star (y : X.B.I.E) : psiEinf X.B.I (star y) = psiEinf X.B.I y := by
  unfold psiEinf
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => ?_
  rw [X.B.I.cm.cmUnram.2.1 (X.B.I.φ v) y]
  congr 2
  simp only [starRingEnd_apply, star_star]
  ring

theorem psi_incl_star (y : X.B.I.E) :
    X.psiEf (Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) (star y)) =
      X.psiEf (Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) y) := by
  have h1 := X.psiEf_rat (star y)
  have h2 := X.psiEf_rat y
  rw [psiEinf_star] at h1
  have hne : psiEinf X.B.I y ≠ 0 := by intro h; rw [h, mul_zero] at h2; exact zero_ne_one h2
  exact mul_right_cancel₀ hne (h1.trans h2.symm)

theorem psi_sig (ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) (m : ℕ) :
    X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I (sigO X.B.I ε) * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) =
      X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) := by
  have e : ∀ a : 𝓞 X.B.I.E, iE X.B.I a * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) =
      Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) ((a : X.B.I.E) * m) := by
    intro a; rw [map_mul, map_natCast]
  rw [e, e, divN_incl, divN_incl, sigO_coe]
  have : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E)⁻¹ * (star (ε : X.B.I.E) * m) = star (((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E)⁻¹ * ((ε : X.B.I.E) * m)) := by
    rw [star_mul, star_mul, star_inv₀, star_natCast, star_natCast]; ring
  rw [this, psi_incl_star]

variable {kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k}

theorem g_split_mul {k k' : Fin 2} (hk : k' ≠ k) :
    ((D.host.g k : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * ((D.host.g k' : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) = ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) := by
  rw [g_eq_cast]
  fin_cases k <;> fin_cases k'
  · exact absurd rfl hk
  · rfl
  · exact mul_comm _ _
  · exact absurd rfl hk

theorem other_ne (k : Fin 2) : (1 - k) ≠ k := by fin_cases k <;> decide

theorem psi_period {ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E} (hε : ε ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)}) (m q : ℕ) :
    X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * ((m + D.host.g kv.1 * q : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) =
      X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) := by
  obtain ⟨δ, hδ⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 hε
  have e : iE X.B.I ε * ((m + D.host.g kv.1 * q : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) =
      iE X.B.I ε * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) + (X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) * (iE X.B.I δ * q) := by
    have hg : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) =
        iE X.B.I (((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * ((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) := by
      rw [g_split_mul X (other_ne kv.1)]; simp [iE]
    rw [hg, ← hδ]
    simp only [iE, map_mul, map_natCast, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_mul]
    ring
  rw [e, psi_modg X _ (mul_mem (iE_int X.B.I δ) (natCast_mem _ q))]

def psiK (ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) (hε : ε ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)}) :
    AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ where
  toFun t := X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * ((t.val : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F)))
  map_zero_eq_one' := by
    simp only [ZMod.val_zero, Nat.cast_zero, mul_zero]
    unfold divN; rw [mul_zero]; exact X.psiEf_int 0 (zero_mem _)
  map_add_eq_mul' a b := by
    have : NeZero (D.host.g kv.1) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime kv.1).ne_zero⟩
    have hval : a.val + b.val = (a + b).val + D.host.g kv.1 * ((a.val + b.val) / D.host.g kv.1) := by
      rw [ZMod.val_add]; exact (Nat.mod_add_div _ _).symm
    rw [← psi_period X hε (a + b).val ((a.val + b.val) / D.host.g kv.1), ← hval, Nat.cast_add, mul_add, divN_add,
      X.psiEf_add]

theorem psiK_apply (ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) (hε : ε ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)})
    (t : ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) :
    psiK X ε hε t = X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * ((t.val : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) := rfl

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppChar

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D) {kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k}

theorem level_mem_wB' (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal :=
  g_mem_of_gk X (gk_mem X kv)

theorem psiK_ne {ε : 𝓞 X.B.I.E} (hε1 : ε - 1 ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal)
    (hεP : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → P ≠ wK X kv → ε ∈ P.asIdeal)
    (hε : ε ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)}) : psiK X ε hε ≠ 1 := by
  intro h1
  have hp := D.host.g_prime kv.1
  have : NeZero (D.host.g kv.1) := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩

  have hm : ∀ m : ℕ, X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (iE X.B.I ε * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F))) = 1 := by
    intro m
    have h2 := congrArg (fun ψ : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ => ψ (m : ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) h1
    simp only [psiK_apply, AddChar.one_apply, ZMod.val_natCast] at h2
    rw [← psi_period X hε (m % D.host.g kv.1) (m / D.host.g kv.1), Nat.mod_add_div] at h2
    exact h2

  have hb : ∀ b : 𝓞 X.B.I.F, X.psiEf (Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F)
      (((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E)⁻¹ * ((ε : X.B.I.E) * ((algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) b : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) : X.B.I.E)))) = 1 := by
    intro b
    set bE : 𝓞 X.B.I.E := algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) b
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := int_surj X.B.I (wK X kv) hp (card_wK X kv) (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ bE)
    set m : ℕ := ((c : ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)).val : ℕ)
    have hbm : bE - (m : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
      have h1' : bE - (c : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
        rw [← Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem, map_sub, map_intCast, hc, sub_self]
      have h2' : ((c : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) - (m : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
        have : ((c - (m : ℤ) : ℤ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
          rw [int_mem_iff X.B.I (wK X kv) hp (card_wK X kv), ← ZMod.intCast_eq_intCast_iff_dvd_sub]
          simp [m]
        simpa using this
      have := add_mem h1' h2'
      simpa using this
    have hall : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal →
        ε * (bE - (m : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) ∈ P.asIdeal := by
      intro P hP
      by_cases hPw : P = wK X kv
      · rw [hPw]; exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hbm
      · exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (hεP P hP hPw)
    obtain ⟨c', hc'⟩ := mem_g_of_all X hall
    have e : iE X.B.I (ε * bE) = iE X.B.I ε * (m : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) +
        (X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) * iE X.B.I c' := by
      have : ε * bE = ε * (m : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) + ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) * c' := by rw [← hc']; ring
      rw [this]; simp [iE]
    have h3 := hm m
    rw [← psi_modg X _ (iE_int X.B.I c'), ← e] at h3
    have e2 : iE X.B.I (ε * bE) = Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) ((ε : X.B.I.E) * (bE : X.B.I.E)) := by
      simp [iE]
    rw [e2, divN_incl] at h3
    exact h3

  set τ := PI.ScPsiExists.UnitaryGroupMassFormula.tau X.B.I.cm.fixed
  set y : X.B.I.F := (((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.F))⁻¹ * τ (ε : X.B.I.E)
  have htr : ∀ z : 𝓞 X.B.I.F, ∃ n : ℤ, Algebra.trace ℚ X.B.I.F (y * algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F z) = n := by
    intro z
    have h4 := X.psiEf_rat (((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E)⁻¹ * ((ε : X.B.I.E) *
      ((algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) z : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) : X.B.I.E)))
    rw [hb z, one_mul] at h4
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := PsiCondAllPf.trace_of_psi X.B.I _ h4
    refine ⟨n, ?_⟩
    rw [← hn]
    congr 1
    have hz : ((algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) z : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) : X.B.I.E) =
        algebraMap X.B.I.F X.B.I.E (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F z) := rfl
    have e3 : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.E)⁻¹ * ((ε : X.B.I.E) * algebraMap X.B.I.F X.B.I.E (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F z)) =
        (((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.F)⁻¹ * algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F z) • (ε : X.B.I.E) := by
      rw [Algebra.smul_def, map_mul, map_inv₀, map_natCast]; ring
    rw [hz, e3, LinearMap.map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
    ring

  let v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.F) :=
    { asIdeal := (wK X kv).asIdeal.comap (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E))
      isPrime := Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _
      ne_bot := by
        intro hbot
        have h5 : ((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.F) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal.comap (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E)) := by
          rw [Ideal.mem_comap, map_natCast]; exact gk_mem X kv
        rw [hbot] at h5
        exact (by exact_mod_cast hp.ne_zero : ((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.F) ≠ 0) ((Submodule.mem_bot _).1 h5) }
  have hv : ((D.host.g kv.1 : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.F) ∈ v.asIdeal := by
    show _ ∈ Ideal.comap _ _
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, map_natCast]; exact gk_mem X kv
  have hun : Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt ℤ v.asIdeal := by
    letI : Algebra X.B.I.F D.host.E := (X.ιE.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap X.B.I.F X.B.I.E)).toAlgebra
    exact PsiCondAllPf.unram_of (D.host.g kv.1) hp v hv
      (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.TypedLoc.tower_ef X.B.I.F D.host.E hp (D.nd.g_split kv.1) v hv).1
  obtain ⟨d, hd, hdv⟩ := PsiCondAllPf.exists_diff_not_mem v hun
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := PsiCondAllPf.mul_diff_int y htr d hd
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := PI.ScPsiExists.UnitaryGroupMassFormula.tau_int X.B.I.cm.fixed ε

  have hgc : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.F) * c = t * d := by
    apply FaithfulSMul.algebraMap_injective (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F
    have hg0 : ((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.F) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (g_pos X).ne'
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, hc, ht, map_natCast]
    show (X.B.g : X.B.I.F) * ((((X.B.g : ℕ) : X.B.I.F))⁻¹ * τ (ε : X.B.I.E) * _) = τ (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.E) X.B.I.E ε) * _
    rw [← mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel₀ hg0, one_mul]
  have htd : t * d ∈ v.asIdeal := by
    rw [← hgc]
    refine Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ ?_
    show _ ∈ Ideal.comap _ _
    rw [Ideal.mem_comap, map_natCast]
    exact g_mem_of_gk X (gk_mem X kv)
  have htv : t ∈ v.asIdeal := (v.isPrime.mem_or_mem htd).resolve_right hdv

  have htw : algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) t ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := htv
  have hte : algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) (𝓞 X.B.I.E) t = ε + sigO X.B.I ε := by
    apply RingOfIntegers.ext
    show algebraMap X.B.I.F X.B.I.E (algebraMap (𝓞 X.B.I.F) X.B.I.F t) = (ε : X.B.I.E) + star (ε : X.B.I.E)
    rw [ht, PI.ScPsiExists.UnitaryGroupMassFormula.tau_apply]
  have hsig : sigO X.B.I ε ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
    have : ε ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv)).asIdeal :=
      hεP _ (natCast_mem_wc X.B.I (level_mem_wB' X kv)) (wK_ne_wc X kv kv).symm
    rwa [mem_wc] at this
  rw [hte] at htw
  have h1w : (1 : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := by
    have := sub_mem (sub_mem htw hε1) hsig
    convert this using 1; ring
  exact (wK X kv).isPrime.ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2 h1w)

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppChar

theorem chi_split (hε1 : ∀ kv, ε kv - 1 ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal)
    (hεP : ∀ kv (P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → P ≠ wK X kv →
      ε kv ∈ P.asIdeal)
    (hε : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), ε kv ∈ Ideal.span {((D.host.g (1 - kv.1) : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)})
    (hn : 0 < X.B.I.n) {C : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F} (hC : C ∈ IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E)
    (t1 t2 : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))
    (h1 : ∀ kv, CongW (w := wK X kv) (D.host.g kv.1) C (t1 kv))
    (h2 : ∀ kv, CongW (w := wc X.B.I (wK X kv)) (D.host.g kv.1) C (t2 kv)) :
    X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g C) = ∏ kv, psiK X (ε kv) (hε kv) (t1 kv + t2 kv) := by
  classical
  set T : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F := ∑ kv, (iE X.B.I (ε kv) * (((t1 kv).val : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) +
    iE X.B.I (sigO X.B.I (ε kv)) * (((t2 kv).val : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F)) with hT
  have hTint : T ∈ IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E :=
    sum_mem fun kv _ => add_mem (mul_mem (iE_int _ _) (natCast_mem _ _)) (mul_mem (iE_int _ _) (natCast_mem _ _))

  have hgP : ∀ kv, ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wK X kv).asIdeal := fun kv => level_mem_wB' X kv
  have hgPc : ∀ kv, ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv)).asIdeal :=
    fun kv => natCast_mem_wc X.B.I (level_mem_wB' X kv)
  have hΔ : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → C - T ∈ Wq X.B.I P := by
    intro P hP
    obtain ⟨kv0, hP0 | hP0⟩ := over_g X P hP
    · subst hP0
      have hp := D.host.g_prime kv0.1
      have : NeZero (D.host.g kv0.1) := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
      have hTc : CongW (w := wK X kv0) (D.host.g kv0.1) T (t1 kv0) := by
        have h3 : CongW (w := wK X kv0) (D.host.g kv0.1) T
            (∑ kv, ((if kv = kv0 then 1 else 0) * (((t1 kv).val : ℕ) : ZMod (D.host.g kv0.1)) +
              0 * (((t2 kv).val : ℕ) : ZMod (D.host.g kv0.1)))) := by
          refine congW_sum _ fun kv _ => congW_add ?_ ?_
          · refine congW_mul (natCast_mem _ _) ?_ (congW_natCast _)
            by_cases hk : kv = kv0
            · subst hk
              rw [if_pos rfl]
              have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv.1) (m := 1) (by simpa using hε1 kv)
              simpa using this
            · rw [if_neg hk]
              have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv0.1) (m := 0)
                (by simpa using hεP kv (wK X kv0) (hgP kv0) (wK_ne X (Ne.symm hk)))
              simpa using this
          · refine congW_mul (natCast_mem _ _) ?_ (congW_natCast _)
            have hm : ε kv ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv0)).asIdeal := hεP kv _ (hgPc kv0) (wK_ne_wc X kv kv0).symm
            rw [mem_wc] at hm
            have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv0.1) (m := 0) (by simpa using hm)
            simpa using this
        convert h3 using 1
        simp [Finset.sum_ite_eq']
      have := congW_sub (h1 kv0) hTc
      rw [sub_self] at this
      exact Wq_of_congW_zero hp (card_wK X kv0) this
    · subst hP0
      have hp := D.host.g_prime kv0.1
      have : NeZero (D.host.g kv0.1) := ⟨hp.ne_zero⟩
      have hTc : CongW (w := wc X.B.I (wK X kv0)) (D.host.g kv0.1) T (t2 kv0) := by
        have h3 : CongW (w := wc X.B.I (wK X kv0)) (D.host.g kv0.1) T
            (∑ kv, (0 * (((t1 kv).val : ℕ) : ZMod (D.host.g kv0.1)) +
              (if kv = kv0 then 1 else 0) * (((t2 kv).val : ℕ) : ZMod (D.host.g kv0.1)))) := by
          refine congW_sum _ fun kv _ => congW_add ?_ ?_
          · refine congW_mul (natCast_mem _ _) ?_ (congW_natCast _)
            have hm : ε kv ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv0)).asIdeal := hεP kv _ (hgPc kv0) (wK_ne_wc X kv kv0).symm
            have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv0.1) (m := 0) (by simpa using hm)
            simpa using this
          · refine congW_mul (natCast_mem _ _) ?_ (congW_natCast _)
            by_cases hk : kv = kv0
            · subst hk
              rw [if_pos rfl]
              have hm : sigO X.B.I (ε kv) - 1 ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv)).asIdeal := by
                rw [mem_wc, map_sub, sigO_sigO, map_one]; exact hε1 kv
              have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv.1) (m := 1) (by simpa using hm)
              simpa using this
            · rw [if_neg hk]
              have hm : sigO X.B.I (ε kv) ∈ (wc X.B.I (wK X kv0)).asIdeal := by
                rw [mem_wc, sigO_sigO]; exact hεP kv (wK X kv0) (hgP kv0) (wK_ne X (Ne.symm hk))
              have := congW_iE (p := D.host.g kv0.1) (m := 0) (by simpa using hm)
              simpa using this
        convert h3 using 1
        simp [Finset.sum_ite_eq']
      have := congW_sub (h2 kv0) hTc
      rw [sub_self] at this
      exact Wq_of_congW_zero hp (card_wKc X kv0) this

  obtain ⟨a, r', hr', hr⟩ := PIW.AdelClassFin.dual_dense X.B.I.F X.B.I.E X.B.g (g_pos X) (C - T) (sub_mem hC hTint)
  have ha : ∀ P : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E), ((X.B.g : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ P.asIdeal → a ∈ P.asIdeal := by
    intro P hP
    have hgW : (X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) ∈ Wq X.B.I P := by
      have := iE_mem (I := X.B.I) hP; simpa [iE] using this
    have hiE : iE X.B.I a ∈ Wq X.B.I P := by
      have := sub_mem (hΔ P hP) (Wq_mul' hgW hr')
      rw [hr] at this
      simpa [iE] using this
    obtain ⟨kv0, hP0 | hP0⟩ := over_g X P hP
    · subst hP0; exact int_of_Wq (D.host.g_prime kv0.1) (card_wK X kv0) hn hiE
    · subst hP0; exact int_of_Wq (D.host.g_prime kv0.1) (card_wKc X kv0) hn hiE
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := mem_g_of_all X ha
  have hCT : C = T + (X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) * (iE X.B.I c + r') := by
    have : C - T = (X.B.g : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) * (iE X.B.I c + r') := by
      rw [hr, hc]; simp [iE]; ring
    rw [← this]; ring
  rw [hCT, psi_modg X _ (add_mem (iE_int _ c) hr'), hT, psi_sum]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  rw [divN_add, X.psiEf_add, psi_sig, AddChar.map_add_eq_mul, psiK_apply, psiK_apply]

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppChar


[ARO_level_character_05] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/KppLocC.lean:17-165

def argC (x : X.B.I.Gf) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F :=
  (∑ a, hA X.B.I (X.f0 a) (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ nxt X.e0 X.z0 a)) +
    ∑ i : Fin D.R.k, if h : i.1 + 1 < D.R.k then hA X.B.I (X.bwd i) (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ X.bw ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩) else 0

theorem quadratic_character_eq_additive (x : X.B.I.Gf) : X.chi2 x = X.psiEf (divN X.B.I X.B.g (argC X x)) := rfl

theorem argC_int {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset) : argC X x ∈ IntAf X.B.I.F X.B.I.E := by
  unfold argC
  refine add_mem (sum_mem fun a _ => ?_) (sum_mem fun i _ => ?_)
  · refine dual_hat_integral X.B.I _ (X.vectorsInLattice.1 a).2 _ (K_mv X.B.I hx ?_)
    unfold nxt; split_ifs
    · exact (X.vectorsInLattice.1 _).1
    · exact X.vectorsInLattice.2.1
  · split_ifs
    · exact dual_hat_integral X.B.I _ (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 i).2 _ (K_mv X.B.I hx (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).1)
    · exact zero_mem _

theorem sg_swap (x : X.B.I.Gf) (u v : VA X.B.I) :
    sg X.B.I (hhat X.B.I u (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ v)) = hhat X.B.I v (X.B.I.fmat x⁻¹ *ᵥ u) := by
  rw [hhat_conj]
  have := hhat_fmat X.B.I x v (X.B.I.fmat x⁻¹ *ᵥ u)
  rw [Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← fmat_mul, mul_inv_cancel, fmat_one, Matrix.one_mulVec] at this
  exact this

variable {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 X.B.I.E)} {p : ℕ}

def tW (ob : Bool) (N : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)) : ZMod p :=
  (∑ a : Fin D.R.rS, if h : a.1 + 1 < D.R.rS then N (xI (hrD D) a) (xI (hrD D) ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)
      else N (xI (hrD D) a) (zI (hrD D))) +
    ∑ i : Fin D.R.k, if h : i.1 + 1 < D.R.k then (if ob then N (wI (hrD D) i) (wI (hrD D) ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩) else 0) else 0

def tWc (ob : Bool) (N' : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)) : ZMod p :=
  (∑ a : Fin D.R.rS, if h : a.1 + 1 < D.R.rS then N' (yI (hrD D) (rv ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)) (yI (hrD D) (rv a))
      else -N' (zI (hrD D)) (yI (hrD D) (rv a))) +
    ∑ i : Fin D.R.k, if h : i.1 + 1 < D.R.k then
      (if ob then 0 else N' (wI (hrD D) (rv ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩)) (wI (hrD D) (rv i))) else 0

theorem bw_Lq (hew : X.ew ∈ Wq X.B.I w) (i : Fin D.R.k) : X.bw i ∈ Lq X.B.I w := by
  rw [X.bw_w i]; exact Wq_smul hew (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 i).1

theorem bwd_Lq (hsew : sg X.B.I X.ew ∈ Wq X.B.I w) (i : Fin D.R.k) : X.bwd i ∈ Lq X.B.I w := by
  rw [X.bwd_w i]; exact Wq_smul hsew (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 i).2

theorem congC (ob : Bool) (hew : X.ew - (if ob then 1 else 0) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset)
    {N : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)}
    (hN : ∀ i j, CongW (w := w) p (hhat X.B.I (frd X ob i) (X.B.I.fmat x *ᵥ fr X ob j)) (N i j)) :
    CongW (w := w) p (argC X x) (tW (D := D) ob N) := by
  unfold argC tW
  refine congW_add (congW_sum _ fun a _ => ?_) (congW_sum _ fun i _ => ?_)
  · unfold nxt
    split_ifs with h
    · have := hN (xI (hrD D) a) (xI (hrD D) ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)
      rwa [dual_frame_isotropic, frame_isotropic] at this
    · have := hN (xI (hrD D) a) (zI (hrD D))
      rwa [dual_frame_isotropic, frame_anisotropic] at this
  · split_ifs with h hob
    · have := hN (wI (hrD D) i) (wI (hrD D) ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩)
      rw [hob] at this
      rwa [frd_wt, fr_wt] at this
    · refine congW_zero_of (hhat_Lq_right (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 i).2 (stab_w X.B.I w hx (bw_Lq X ?_ _)))
      simp only [hob, Bool.false_eq_true, if_false, sub_zero] at hew
      exact hew
    · exact congW_zero_of (zero_mem _)

theorem congW_of_sg {t : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F} {m : ZMod p} (h : CongW (w := w) p (sg X.B.I t) m) :
    CongW (w := wc X.B.I w) p t m := by
  have := (congW_sg (I := X.B.I) (w := w) (t := sg X.B.I t) (m := m)).2 h
  rwa [sg_sg] at this

theorem congCc (ob : Bool) (hsew : sg X.B.I X.ew - (if ob then 0 else 1) ∈ Wq X.B.I w) {x : X.B.I.Gf}
    (hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset) {N' : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)}
    (hN' : ∀ i j, CongW (w := w) p (hhat X.B.I (frd X ob i) (X.B.I.fmat x⁻¹ *ᵥ fr X ob j)) (N' i j)) :
    CongW (w := wc X.B.I w) p (argC X x) (tWc (D := D) ob N') := by
  refine congW_of_sg X ?_
  unfold argC tWc
  rw [map_add, map_sum, map_sum]
  refine congW_add (congW_sum _ fun a _ => ?_) (congW_sum _ fun i _ => ?_)
  · unfold nxt
    split_ifs with h
    · show CongW p (sg X.B.I (hhat X.B.I _ _)) _
      rw [sg_swap]
      have := hN' (yI (hrD D) (rv ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)) (yI (hrD D) (rv a))
      rwa [frd_y, fr_y, rv_rv, rv_rv] at this
    · show CongW p (sg X.B.I (hhat X.B.I _ _)) _
      rw [sg_swap]
      have := congW_neg (hN' (zI (hrD D)) (yI (hrD D) (rv a)))
      rwa [dual_frame_anisotropic, fr_y, rv_rv, hhat_neg_left, neg_neg] at this
  · split_ifs with h hob
    · show CongW p (sg X.B.I (hhat X.B.I _ _)) _
      rw [sg_swap]
      refine congW_zero_of (hhat_Lq_right (X.vectorsInLattice.2.2 _).1 (stab_w X.B.I w (K_inv X.B.I hx) (bwd_Lq X ?_ i)))
      simp only [hob, if_true, sub_zero] at hsew
      exact hsew
    · show CongW p (sg X.B.I (hhat X.B.I _ _)) _
      rw [sg_swap]
      have := hN' (wI (hrD D) (rv ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩)) (wI (hrD D) (rv i))
      simp only [hob, Bool.false_eq_true] at this ⊢
      rwa [frd_wf, fr_wf, rv_rv, rv_rv] at this
    · rw [map_zero]; exact congW_zero_of (zero_mem _)

theorem entN_eq {r : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin r) (Fin r) (ZMod p)) (i j : Fin r) {a b : ℕ} (ha : i.1 = a) (hb : j.1 = b) :
    M i j = entN M a b := by
  subst ha hb
  unfold entN
  rw [dif_pos ⟨i.2, j.2⟩]

theorem sum_eq (ob : Bool) {N N' : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod p)}
    (hinv : ∀ i, i + 1 < D.R.r → entN N' i (i + 1) = -entN N i (i + 1)) :
    tW (D := D) ob N + tWc (D := D) ob N' = phiJ D.R.k D.R.rS (dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob * N * dM D.R.rS D.R.k ob) := by
  have hr := hrD D
  rw [phi_key hr ob hinv]
  unfold tW tWc
  have e1 : ∑ a : Fin D.R.rS, ((if h : a.1 + 1 < D.R.rS then N (xI hr a) (xI hr ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)
        else N (xI hr a) (zI hr)) + (if h : a.1 + 1 < D.R.rS then N' (yI hr (rv ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)) (yI hr (rv a))
        else -N' (zI hr) (yI hr (rv a)))) =
      ∑ a ∈ range D.R.rS, (if a + 1 < D.R.rS then entN N a (a + 1) +
        entN N' (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 + (D.R.rS - 2 - a)) (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 + (D.R.rS - 1 - a))
        else entN N a (D.R.rS + D.R.k) - entN N' (D.R.rS + D.R.k) (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1)) := by
    rw [← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
    split_ifs with h
    · have q1 : N (xI hr a) (xI hr ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩) = entN N a.1 (a.1 + 1) := entN_eq N _ _ rfl rfl
      have q2 : N' (yI hr (rv ⟨a.1 + 1, h⟩)) (yI hr (rv a)) =
          entN N' (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 + (D.R.rS - 2 - a.1)) (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 + (D.R.rS - 1 - a.1)) :=
        entN_eq N' _ _ (by simp; omega) (by simp)
      rw [q1, q2]
    · have q1 : N (xI hr a) (zI hr) = entN N a.1 (D.R.rS + D.R.k) := entN_eq N _ _ rfl rfl
      have q2 : N' (zI hr) (yI hr (rv a)) = entN N' (D.R.rS + D.R.k) (D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1) :=
        entN_eq N' _ _ rfl (by simp; omega)
      rw [q1, q2]; ring
  have e2 : ∑ i : Fin D.R.k, ((if h : i.1 + 1 < D.R.k then (if ob then N (wI hr i) (wI hr ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩) else 0) else 0) +
        (if h : i.1 + 1 < D.R.k then (if ob then 0 else N' (wI hr (rv ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩)) (wI hr (rv i))) else 0)) =
      ∑ i ∈ range D.R.k, (if i + 1 < D.R.k then (if ob then entN N (D.R.rS + i) (D.R.rS + i + 1)
        else entN N' (D.R.rS + (D.R.k - 2 - i)) (D.R.rS + (D.R.k - 1 - i))) else 0) := by
    rw [← Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    split_ifs with h hob
    · have q1 : N (wI hr i) (wI hr ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩) = entN N (D.R.rS + i.1) (D.R.rS + i.1 + 1) :=
        entN_eq N _ _ rfl (by simp; omega)
      rw [q1, add_zero]
    · have q1 : N' (wI hr (rv ⟨i.1 + 1, h⟩)) (wI hr (rv i)) =
          entN N' (D.R.rS + (D.R.k - 2 - i.1)) (D.R.rS + (D.R.k - 1 - i.1)) :=
        entN_eq N' _ _ (by simp; omega) (by simp)
      rw [q1, zero_add]
    · simp
  rw [← e1, ← e2, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_add_distrib]
  ring

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.KppLoc

import Mathlib
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.RootLatticeThresholds.AllRowsTr
import Formalism.LocalToGlobal.SiegelLatticeTheory.FlagStabilizerTensors.AllRowsOf

namespace Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsWt
