def posN (kk s a : ℕ) : ℕ := if a < s then a else if a < s + kk then a - s else if a = s + kk then s else a - kk

section Tor

variable {g : ℕ} [Fact g.Prime] {N : ℕ} (kk s : ℕ) (c : ZMod g)

def tN (a : ℕ) : ZMod g := c ^ posN kk s a

theorem pair (hc : c ≠ 0) {a b : ℕ} (h : posN kk s b = posN kk s a + 1) : (tN kk s c a)⁻¹ * tN kk s c b = c := by
  unfold tN
  rw [h, pow_succ, inv_mul_cancel_left₀ (pow_ne_zero _ hc)]

theorem entN_conj (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (a b : ℕ) :
    entN (diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * u * diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i)) a b =
      (tN kk s c a)⁻¹ * tN kk s c b * entN u a b := by
  unfold entN
  split_ifs with h
  · simp only [mul_diagonal, diagonal_mul]
    ring
  · simp

theorem phiJ_conj (hc : c ≠ 0) (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :
    phiJ kk s (diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * u * diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i)) =
      c * phiJ kk s u := by
  unfold phiJ
  simp only [entN_conj]
  have h1 : ∀ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), (tN kk s c i)⁻¹ * tN kk s c (i + 1) = c := fun i hi =>
    pair kk s c hc (by rw [Finset.mem_range] at hi; unfold posN; split_ifs <;> omega)
  have h3 : ∀ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), (tN kk s c (s + kk + 1 + i))⁻¹ * tN kk s c (s + kk + 2 + i) = c :=
    fun i hi => pair kk s c hc (by rw [Finset.mem_range] at hi; unfold posN; split_ifs <;> omega)
  have h4 : ∀ a ∈ Finset.range (kk - 1), (tN kk s c (s + a))⁻¹ * tN kk s c (s + a + 1) = c := fun a ha =>
    pair kk s c hc (by rw [Finset.mem_range] at ha; unfold posN; split_ifs <;> omega)
  have s1 : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), (tN kk s c i)⁻¹ * tN kk s c (i + 1) * entN u i (i + 1) =
      c * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), entN u i (i + 1) := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]; exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => by rw [h1 i hi]
  have s3 : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), (tN kk s c (s + kk + 1 + i))⁻¹ * tN kk s c (s + kk + 2 + i) *
      entN u (s + kk + 1 + i) (s + kk + 2 + i) = c * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), entN u (s + kk + 1 + i) (s + kk + 2 + i) := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]; exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => by rw [h3 i hi]
  have s4 : ∑ a ∈ Finset.range (kk - 1), (tN kk s c (s + a))⁻¹ * tN kk s c (s + a + 1) * entN u (s + a) (s + a + 1) =
      c * ∑ a ∈ Finset.range (kk - 1), entN u (s + a) (s + a + 1) := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]; exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a ha => by rw [h4 a ha]
  rw [s1, s3, s4]
  split_ifs with hs
  · rw [pair kk s c hc (a := s - 1) (b := s + kk) (by unfold posN; split_ifs <;> omega),
      pair kk s c hc (a := s + kk) (b := s + kk + 1) (by unfold posN; split_ifs <;> omega)]
    ring
  · ring

end Tor

section Conj

variable {g : ℕ} [Fact g.Prime] {N : ℕ} (kk s : ℕ) (c : ZMod g) (hc : c ≠ 0)
include hc

theorem tN_ne (a : ℕ) : tN kk s c a ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero _ hc

def T : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g) where
  val := diagonal fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i
  inv := diagonal fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹
  val_inv := by rw [diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp [mul_inv_cancel₀ (tN_ne kk s c hc _)]
  inv_val := by rw [diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp [inv_mul_cancel₀ (tN_ne kk s c hc _)]

theorem T_conj_val (u : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :
    (((T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * u * T kk s c hc : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) =
      diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) *
        diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i) := rfl

def PJ (M : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Prop :=
  IsUniUp M ∧ ∀ a, s ≤ a → a < s + kk → entN M a (s + kk) = 0

theorem PJ_conj (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :
    PJ kk s (diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * u * diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i)) ↔ PJ kk s u := by
  have hX : ∀ i j : Fin N,
      (diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * u * diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i)) i j =
        (tN kk s c i)⁻¹ * tN kk s c j * u i j := by
    intro i j
    simp only [mul_diagonal, diagonal_mul]
    ring
  have hz : ∀ i j : Fin N, (tN kk s c i)⁻¹ * tN kk s c j * u i j = 0 ↔ u i j = 0 := by
    intro i j
    simp [tN_ne kk s c hc]
  have ho : ∀ i : Fin N, (tN kk s c i)⁻¹ * tN kk s c i * u i i = u i i := by
    intro i
    rw [inv_mul_cancel₀ (tN_ne kk s c hc _), one_mul]
  have hent : ∀ a b : ℕ,
      entN (diagonal (fun i : Fin N => (tN kk s c i)⁻¹) * u * diagonal (fun i : Fin N => tN kk s c i)) a b = 0 ↔
        entN u a b = 0 := by
    intro a b
    rw [entN_conj]
    simp [tN_ne kk s c hc]
  unfold PJ IsUniUp
  simp only [hX]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    refine ⟨fun a b => ⟨fun hab => (hz a b).1 ((h1 a b).1 hab), fun hab => ?_⟩,
      fun a ha hb => (hent a _).1 (h2 a ha hb)⟩
    subst hab
    rw [← ho]
    exact (h1 a a).2 rfl
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    refine ⟨fun a b => ⟨fun hab => (hz a b).2 ((h1 a b).1 hab), fun hab => ?_⟩,
      fun a ha hb => (hent a _).2 (h2 a ha hb)⟩
    subst hab
    rw [ho]
    exact (h1 a a).2 rfl

theorem eJ_conj (ψ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) :
    haveI : NeZero g := ⟨(Fact.out : g.Prime).ne_zero⟩
    MonoidAlgebra.single (T kk s c hc) 1 * eJ g N kk s ψ * MonoidAlgebra.single (T kk s c hc)⁻¹ 1 =
      eJ g N kk s (ψ.mulShift c) := by
  have hP : ∀ x : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g),
      PJ kk s (((T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * x * T kk s c hc : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) ↔
        PJ kk s (x : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := fun x => by
    rw [T_conj_val]
    exact PJ_conj kk s c hc _
  have hφ : ∀ x : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g),
      phiJ kk s (((T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * x * T kk s c hc : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) =
        c * phiJ kk s (x : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := fun x => by
    rw [T_conj_val]
    exact phiJ_conj kk s c hc _
  unfold eJ eDat
  simp only [mul_smul_comm, smul_mul_assoc, Finset.mul_sum, Finset.sum_mul, MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single,
    one_mul, mul_one]
  congr 1
  refine Finset.sum_nbij' (fun u => T kk s c hc * u * (T kk s c hc)⁻¹) (fun u => (T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * u * T kk s c hc)
    ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
  · intro a ha
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at ha ⊢
    have e : (T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * (T kk s c hc * a * (T kk s c hc)⁻¹) * T kk s c hc = a := by group
    have := (hP (T kk s c hc * a * (T kk s c hc)⁻¹)).1
    rw [e] at this
    exact this ha
  · intro a ha
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and] at ha ⊢
    exact (hP a).2 ha
  · intro a _
    group
  · intro a _
    group
  · intro a _
    have e : (T kk s c hc)⁻¹ * (T kk s c hc * a * (T kk s c hc)⁻¹) * T kk s c hc = a := by group
    rw [AddChar.mulShift_apply, ← hφ, e]

end Conj

section SA

variable {g : ℕ} [Fact g.Prime] {N kk s : ℕ}

instance instNZ : NeZero g := ⟨(Fact.out : g.Prime).ne_zero⟩

open Classical in
theorem eJ_coeff (ψ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) (v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :
    (eJ g N kk s ψ).coeff v = if PJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) then
      (ψ (phiJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))))⁻¹ /
        ((Finset.univ.filter fun u : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g) => PJ kk s (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))).card : ℂ)
      else 0 := by
  unfold eJ eDat
  simp only [MonoidAlgebra.coeff_smul, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_single] at *
  simp
  simp only [Finsupp.single_apply, Finset.sum_ite_eq, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ,
    true_and]
  unfold PJ
  split_ifs <;> simp [div_eq_inv_mul]
  exact Or.inl (by congr)

private theorem PJ_mul_of_PJ_of_PJ (u v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (hu : PJ kk s (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)))
    (hv : PJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))) :
    PJ kk s ((u * v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := by
  rw [Units.val_mul]
  refine ⟨AllRowsOf.isUniUp_mul hu.1 hv.1, fun a ha hb => ?_⟩
  rw [AllRowsOf.entN_mul_add hu.1 hv.1 hb (fun j hj1 hj2 => hv.2 j (by omega) hj2), hu.2 a ha hb,
    hv.2 a ha hb, add_zero]

theorem PJ_pow (v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (h : PJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))) (n : ℕ) :
    PJ kk s ((v ^ n : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := by
  induction n with
  | zero =>
    rw [pow_zero, Units.val_one]
    refine ⟨fun a b => ⟨fun hab => ?_, fun hab => ?_⟩, fun a ha hb => AllRowsOf.entN_one_ne' (by omega)⟩
    · exact Matrix.one_apply_ne (ne_of_gt hab)
    · rw [hab, Matrix.one_apply_eq]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [pow_succ]
    exact PJ_mul_of_PJ_of_PJ _ _ ih h

theorem PJ_inv (v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (h : PJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))) :
    PJ kk s ((v⁻¹ : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := by
  have hpos : 0 < Nat.card (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := Nat.card_pos
  have he : v ^ (Nat.card (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) - 1) * v = 1 := by
    rw [← pow_succ, Nat.sub_add_cancel hpos, pow_card_eq_one']
  rw [← eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_left he]
  exact PJ_pow v h _

theorem phiJ_inv (v : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (h : PJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))) :
    phiJ kk s ((v⁻¹ : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) =
      -phiJ kk s (v : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := by
  have hi := PJ_inv v h
  have hm := AllRowsOf.phiJ_mul (kk := kk) (s := s) hi.1 h.1 h.2
  rw [← Units.val_mul, inv_mul_cancel, Units.val_one] at hm
  have e : ∀ a b : ℕ, a ≠ b → entN (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) a b = 0 :=
    fun a b hab => AllRowsOf.entN_one_ne' hab
  have z1 : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), entN (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) i (i + 1) = 0 :=
    Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => e _ _ (by omega)
  have z2 : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1),
      entN (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (s + kk + 1 + i) (s + kk + 2 + i) = 0 :=
    Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => e _ _ (by omega)
  have z3 : ∑ a ∈ Finset.range (kk - 1), entN (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) (s + a) (s + a + 1) = 0 :=
    Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => e _ _ (by omega)
  have h0 : phiJ kk s (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) = 0 := by
    unfold phiJ
    rw [z1, z2, z3]
    split_ifs with hs
    · rw [e (s - 1) (s + kk) (by omega), e (s + kk) (s + kk + 1) (by omega)]
      simp
    · simp
  rw [h0] at hm
  linear_combination -hm

end SA

section Wt

variable {g : ℕ} [Fact g.Prime] {N kk s : ℕ}

theorem eJ_selfadj (ψ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) : conjA (eJ g N kk s ψ) = eJ g N kk s ψ := by
  refine MonoidAlgebra.ext (Finsupp.ext fun w => ?_)
  rw [conjA_coeff, eJ_coeff, eJ_coeff]
  have hiff : PJ kk s ((w⁻¹ : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) ↔
      PJ kk s (w : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :=
    ⟨fun h => by simpa using PJ_inv _ h, PJ_inv w⟩
  have hs : ∀ x : ZMod g, star (ψ x) = (ψ x)⁻¹ := fun x => by
    rw [← AddChar.map_neg_eq_inv, AddChar.map_neg_eq_conj, starRingEnd_apply]
  by_cases hw : PJ kk s (w : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))
  · rw [if_pos (hiff.2 hw), if_pos hw, phiJ_inv w hw, AddChar.map_neg_eq_inv, inv_inv, star_div₀, hs,
      star_natCast]
  · rw [if_neg (mt hiff.1 hw), if_neg hw, star_zero]

theorem exists_shift (ψ ψ' : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) (h : ψ ≠ 1) (h' : ψ' ≠ 1) :
    ∃ c : ZMod g, c ≠ 0 ∧ ψ' = ψ.mulShift c := by
  have hinj : Function.Injective ψ.mulShift :=
    AddChar.to_mulShift_inj_of_isPrimitive (AddChar.IsPrimitive.of_ne_one h)
  have hbij : Function.Bijective ψ.mulShift := by
    rw [Fintype.bijective_iff_injective_and_card]
    exact ⟨hinj, by simp⟩
  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hbij.2 ψ'
  refine ⟨c, ?_, hc.symm⟩
  rintro rfl
  exact h' (by rw [← hc, AddChar.mulShift_zero])

theorem wt_indep (ψ ψ' : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) (h : ψ ≠ 1) (h' : ψ' ≠ 1) (e : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)))
    (hc : ∀ y, e * y = y * e) :
    (eJ g N kk s ψ' * e).coeff 1 = (eJ g N kk s ψ * e).coeff 1 ∧ (eJ g N kk s ψ').coeff 1 = (eJ g N kk s ψ).coeff 1 := by
  obtain ⟨c, hc0, rfl⟩ := exists_shift ψ ψ' h h'
  rw [← eJ_conj (N := N) kk s c hc0 ψ]
  constructor
  · rw [mul_assoc _ (MonoidAlgebra.single _ 1) e, ← hc (MonoidAlgebra.single _ 1), ← mul_assoc,
      mul_assoc _ (eJ g N kk s ψ) e]
    exact tau_conj _ _
  · exact tau_conj _ _

theorem tau_eJ (ψ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) (e : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g))) (he : e * e = e)
    (hc : ∀ y, e * y = y * e) (hse : conjA e = e) :
    (eJ g N kk s ψ * e).coeff 1 = ((∑ h, Complex.normSq ((eJ g N kk s ψ * e).coeff h) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
  exact tau_proj _ _ (AllRowsOf.eJ_idem g N kk s ψ) (eJ_selfadj ψ) he hc hse

end Wt

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsWt

theorem card_PJ (D : D7) (k : Fin 2) :
    ((Finset.univ.filter fun u : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k)) =>
      PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (u : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k)))).card : ℂ) =
      (cJ D (D.host.g k) : ℂ) := by

    X.volK2 = ((cJ D (D.host.g 0) ^ D.R.f * cJ D (D.host.g 1) ^ D.R.f : ℕ) : ℝ) * V X := by

theorem hat_e_eq (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ)
    (hmem : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 ↔ ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))
    (hchi : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv)))
    (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    hat hS X.B.e γ = ∏ kv, (eJv kv (ψ kv)).coeff ((P kv)⁻¹ * γ kv * P kv) := by

    have hJ' : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1)
        (((P kv)⁻¹ * γ kv * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
          Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) := hJ
    rw [if_pos hxK', Finset.prod_congr rfl (fun kv _ => if_pos (hJ' kv))]
    have hc := hchi _ hxK
    rw [hx] at hc
    have hv : (X.B.μf (K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw)).toReal =
        ((cJ D (D.host.g 0) ^ D.R.f * cJ D (D.host.g 1) ^ D.R.f : ℕ) : ℝ) * V X := levelVolume_eq_index hS
    show (V X : ℂ) * (star (X.chi2 (sec hS γ)) / ((X.B.μf (K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw)).toReal : ℂ)) = _
    rw [hc, hv, star_prod]
    simp only [star_psi, card_PJ]
    rw [Finset.prod_div_distrib]
    have hprod : ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (cJ D (D.host.g kv.1) : ℂ) =
        ((cJ D (D.host.g 0) ^ D.R.f * cJ D (D.host.g 1) ^ D.R.f : ℕ) : ℂ) := by
      rw [← prod_sigma_const (fun k => cJ D (D.host.g k))]; push_cast; rfl
    rw [hprod]
    have hV := V_ne X
    have hJ0 : ((cJ D (D.host.g 0) ^ D.R.f * cJ D (D.host.g 1) ^ D.R.f : ℕ) : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by
      exact_mod_cast (Nat.mul_pos (pow_pos (cJ_pos D 0) _) (pow_pos (cJ_pos D 1) _)).ne'
    push_cast
    field_simp
  · have hxK : ((sec hS γ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∉ X.K2 := fun h => hJ (by rw [← hx]; exact (hmem _).1 h)
    push Not at hJ
    obtain ⟨kv, hkv⟩ := hJ
    have hxK' : ((sec hS γ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∉ K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw := hxK
    rw [if_neg hxK', mul_zero]
    symm
    have hkv' : ¬ PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1)
        (((P kv)⁻¹ * γ kv * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
          Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) := hkv
    exact Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ kv) (if_neg hkv')

theorem conjP_coeff {G : Type} [Group G] (P γ : G) (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) :
    (MonoidAlgebra.single P 1 * a * MonoidAlgebra.single P⁻¹ 1).coeff γ = a.coeff (P⁻¹ * γ * P) := by
  simp [MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_apply, MonoidAlgebra.mul_single_apply, mul_assoc]

theorem Ehat_eq (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ)
    (hmem : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 ↔ ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))
    (hchi : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))) :
    Ehat hS = pA (fun kv => MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv) 1 * eJv kv (ψ kv) * MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv)⁻¹ 1) := by
  classical
  apply MonoidAlgebra.ext; ext γ
  rw [Ehat_coeff, pA_coeff, hat_e_eq hS P ψ hmem hchi γ]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  rw [conjP_coeff]

theorem tau_conjP {G : Type} [Group G] [Fintype G] (P : G) (a e : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) (hc : ∀ y, e * y = y * e) :
    (MonoidAlgebra.single P 1 * a * MonoidAlgebra.single P⁻¹ 1 * e).coeff 1 = (a * e).coeff 1 := by
  rw [mul_assoc (MonoidAlgebra.single P 1 * a), ← hc, ← mul_assoc, mul_assoc (MonoidAlgebra.single P 1) a e,
    conjP_coeff]
  simp

section Val

variable (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ)

theorem val_eT_sum (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ)
    (hψ : ∀ kv, ψ kv ≠ 1) (hψ₀ : ∀ k, ψ₀ k ≠ 1)
    (hmem : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 ↔ ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))
    (hchi : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv)))
    (S : Set (Tup2 D)) :
    (Ehat hS * ES S).coeff 1 =
      ∑ a ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a ∈ S),
        ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) := by
  classical
  rw [ES_eq, Finset.mul_sum, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, Finset.sum_apply']
  refine Finset.sum_congr (by congr 1) fun a _ => ?_
  rw [Ehat_eq hS P ψ hmem hchi, Ea_pA, pA_mul, pA_one_coeff]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  rw [tau_conjP _ _ _ (D.ntypes.eSer_central kv.1 _)]
  exact (wt_indep (ψ₀ kv.1) (ψ kv) (hψ₀ kv.1) (hψ kv) _ (D.ntypes.eSer_central kv.1 _)).1

include hS in
theorem val_e (P : G0g D.nd) (ψ : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) ℂ)
    (hψ : ∀ kv, ψ kv ≠ 1) (hψ₀ : ∀ k, ψ₀ k ≠ 1)
    (hmem : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 ↔ ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))
    (hchi : ∀ x : KSub X.B.I, (x : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 →
      X.chi2 x = ∏ kv, ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P (rhoX X x) kv))) :
    (Ehat hS).coeff 1 = ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Zc ψ₀ kv.1 := by
  classical
  rw [Ehat_eq hS P ψ hmem hchi, pA_one_coeff]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  rw [conjP_coeff, mul_one, inv_mul_cancel]
  exact (wt_indep (ψ₀ kv.1) (ψ kv) (hψ₀ kv.1) (hψ kv) 1 (fun y => by rw [one_mul, mul_one])).2

end Val

section Fact

variable (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ)

theorem sum_N_single (k : Fin 2) (f : Polynomial (ZMod (D.host.g k))) :
    ∑ c : Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g k), (if Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c = f then Nc ψ₀ k (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c) else 0) =
      Nc ψ₀ k f := by
  classical
  by_cases hf : f.Monic ∧ f.natDegree = D.R.r
  · have hc0 := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.monicOf_coeff hf.1 hf.2
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single (fun i : Fin D.R.r => f.coeff i)]
    · rw [if_pos hc0, hc0]
    · intro c _ hc
      rw [if_neg]
      intro h
      apply hc
      exact monicOf_inj (h.trans hc0.symm)
    · intro h; exact absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h
  · rw [Finset.sum_eq_zero fun c _ => if_neg fun (h : Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c = f) => hf (h ▸ Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.monicOf_monic c)]
    unfold Nc
    rw [D.ntypes.eSer_zero k f (fun h => hf ⟨h.1, h.2.1⟩), mul_zero]
    rfl

theorem sum_N_all (k : Fin 2) : ∑ c : Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g k), Nc ψ₀ k (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c) = Zc ψ₀ k := by
  unfold Nc Zc
  have := congrArg (fun y => (eJk D k (ψ₀ k) * y).coeff 1) (sum_eSer' (D := D) k)
  simp only [Finset.mul_sum, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, Finset.sum_apply', mul_one] at this
  exact this

end Fact

section Cyl

variable (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ)

open Classical in

theorem sum_cyl (k : Fin 2) (S : Finset (Tup D k)) :
    ∑ a ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a ∈ cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k))),
        ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) =
      ∑ t ∈ S, ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k),
        (if kv.1 = k then Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Function.update (0 : Tup2 D) k t kv.1 kv.2) else Zc ψ₀ kv.1) := by
  rw [Finset.sum_filter]
  have h1 : ∀ a : CV D, (if tupOf a ∈ cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k)) then
      ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) else 0) =
      ∑ t ∈ S, (if tupOf a k = t then ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) else 0) := by
    intro a
    rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq]
    exact if_congr (Iff.rfl) rfl rfl
  simp_rw [h1]
  rw [Finset.sum_comm]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun t _ => ?_
  set G : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), (Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) → ℂ := fun kv c =>
    if kv.1 = k then (if Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c = Function.update (0 : Tup2 D) k t kv.1 kv.2 then
      Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c) else 0) else Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c) with hG
  have h2 : ∀ a : CV D, (if tupOf a k = t then ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv))
      else 0) = ∏ kv, G kv (a kv) := by
    intro a
    have hG' : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), G kv (a kv) =
        if (kv.1 = k → Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv) = Function.update (0 : Tup2 D) k t kv.1 kv.2) then
          Nc ψ₀ kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf (a kv)) else 0 := by
      intro kv
      by_cases hk : kv.1 = k
      · simp only [hG, hk, if_true, true_implies]
      · simp only [hG, hk, if_false, false_implies, if_true]
    rw [Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => hG' kv, Finset.prod_ite_zero]
    congr 1
    apply propext
    constructor
    · intro h kv _ hk
      obtain ⟨k', v⟩ := kv
      simp only at hk
      subst hk
      rw [Function.update_self, ← h]
      rfl
    · intro h
      funext v
      have := h ⟨k, v⟩ (Finset.mem_univ _) rfl
      rw [Function.update_self] at this
      exact this
  simp_rw [h2]
  rw [← Fintype.prod_sum]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  by_cases hk : kv.1 = k
  · rw [if_pos hk]
    simp only [hG, hk, if_true]
    exact sum_N_single ψ₀ kv.1 _
  · rw [if_neg hk]
    simp only [hG, hk, if_false]
    exact sum_N_all ψ₀ kv.1

end Cyl

section Final

variable (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ)

theorem Nc_real (k : Fin 2) (f : Polynomial (ZMod (D.host.g k))) : Nc ψ₀ k f = ((Nc ψ₀ k f).re : ℂ) := by
  have hc := D.ntypes.eSer_central k f
  have hs := selfadj_of_central_idem _ (D.ntypes.eSer_idem k f) hc
  have h := tau_eJ (kk := D.R.k) (s := D.R.m / 2 - 1) (ψ₀ k) _ (D.ntypes.eSer_idem k f) hc hs
  have hre : Nc ψ₀ k f = ((∑ h, Complex.normSq ((eJ (D.host.g k) D.R.r D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (ψ₀ k) *
      D.ntypes.eSer k f).coeff h) : ℝ) : ℂ) := h
  rw [hre, Complex.ofReal_re]

theorem phiJ_one' {g N kk s : ℕ} : phiJ kk s (1 : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) = 0 := by
  simp [phiJ, entN, Matrix.one_apply]
  intros
  omega

theorem Zc_eq (k : Fin 2) : Zc ψ₀ k = (((cJ D (D.host.g k) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
  classical
  unfold Zc
  show (eJ (D.host.g k) D.R.r D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (ψ₀ k)).coeff 1 = _
  rw [eJ_coeff, if_pos (by rw [Units.val_one]; exact jPred_one _ _), card_PJ, Units.val_one, phiJ_one',
    AddChar.map_zero_eq_one, inv_one]
  push_cast
  ring

end Final

theorem split_prod (F : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) → ℂ) :
    ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), F kv = (∏ v : D.nd.PlG 0, F ⟨0, v⟩) * ∏ v : D.nd.PlG 1, F ⟨1, v⟩ := by
  rw [Fintype.prod_sigma, Fin.prod_univ_two]

theorem etOne_of (hK : KppShape X) (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) (hψ₀ : ∀ k, ψ₀ k ≠ 1) :
    EtOne X ψ₀ := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨P, ψ, hψ, hK⟩ := hK
  have hmem := fun x => (hK x).1
  have hchi := fun x => (hK x).2
  intro k S
  have h1 : eT X (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k))) 1 =
      (Ehat hS * ES (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k)))).coeff 1 / (V X : ℂ) := by
    have := val_hat hS (biInv_eT X (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k)))) (1 : KSub X.B.I)
    rw [map_one, hat_eT'] at this
    exact this
  have h2 : X.B.e 1 = (Ehat hS).coeff 1 / (V X : ℂ) := by
    have := val_hat hS (biInv_e X) (1 : KSub X.B.I)
    rw [map_one, ← Ehat_coeff] at this
    exact this
  rw [h1, h2, val_eT_sum hS ψ₀ P ψ hψ hψ₀ hmem hchi, sum_cyl ψ₀ k S, val_e hS ψ₀ P ψ hψ hψ₀ hmem hchi]
  have hZ : ∀ j : Fin 2, Zc ψ₀ j = (((cJ D (D.host.g j) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ) := Zc_eq ψ₀
  have hzpos : ∀ j : Fin 2, (0 : ℝ) < (cJ D (D.host.g j) : ℝ)⁻¹ := fun j => by
    have := cJ_pos D j; positivity
  have hw : ∀ f, wtOf D k (eJk D k (ψ₀ k)) f = (Nc ψ₀ k f).re / (cJ D (D.host.g k) : ℝ)⁻¹ := by
    intro f
    show (Nc ψ₀ k f).re / (Zc ψ₀ k).re = _
    rw [hZ, Complex.ofReal_re]
  simp only [hw]
  have hV := V_pos X
  have hVc : (V X : ℂ) ≠ 0 := V_ne X
  obtain rfl | rfl : k = 0 ∨ k = 1 := by fin_cases k <;> simp
  · simp only [split_prod, if_true, if_neg (show (1 : Fin 2) ≠ 0 by decide), Function.update_self,
      Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, D.nd.card_PlG, hZ]
    have e1 : ∑ x ∈ S, (∏ v, Nc ψ₀ 0 (x v)) * ((((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f =
        ((∑ x ∈ S, (∏ v, (Nc ψ₀ 0 (x v)).re) * ((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
      push_cast
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
      congr 1
      exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => Nc_real ψ₀ 0 _
    rw [e1]
    rw [show ((((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f * ((((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f / (V X : ℂ) =
      ((((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f * ((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f / V X : ℝ) : ℂ) by push_cast; ring]
    rw [← Complex.ofReal_div, Complex.ofReal_re, Complex.ofReal_re]
    simp only [Finset.prod_div_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, D.nd.card_PlG]
    rw [← Finset.sum_div, ← Finset.sum_mul]
    have h0 := hzpos 0
    have h1' := hzpos 1
    field_simp
    rw [mul_div_assoc, div_self (pow_ne_zero _ (one_div_ne_zero (by exact_mod_cast (cJ_pos D 0).ne'))), mul_one]
  · simp only [split_prod, if_true, if_neg (show (0 : Fin 2) ≠ 1 by decide), Function.update_self,
      Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, D.nd.card_PlG, hZ]
    have e1 : ∑ x ∈ S, ((((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f * (∏ v, Nc ψ₀ 1 (x v)) =
        ((∑ x ∈ S, ((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f * (∏ v, (Nc ψ₀ 1 (x v)).re) : ℝ) : ℂ) := by
      push_cast
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
      congr 1
      exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => Nc_real ψ₀ 1 _
    rw [e1]
    rw [show ((((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f * ((((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹ : ℝ) : ℂ)) ^ D.R.f / (V X : ℂ) =
      ((((cJ D (D.host.g 0) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f * ((cJ D (D.host.g 1) : ℝ)⁻¹) ^ D.R.f / V X : ℝ) : ℂ) by push_cast; ring]
    rw [← Complex.ofReal_div, Complex.ofReal_re, Complex.ofReal_re]
    simp only [Finset.prod_div_distrib, Finset.prod_const, Finset.card_univ, D.nd.card_PlG]
    rw [← Finset.sum_div, ← Finset.mul_sum]
    have h0 := hzpos 0
    have h1' := hzpos 1
    field_simp
    rw [mul_div_assoc, div_self (pow_ne_zero _ (one_div_ne_zero (by exact_mod_cast (cJ_pos D 1).ne'))), mul_one]

end Main

    MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) := by
  classical
  exact ((Finset.univ.filter fun u : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g) => P (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))).card : ℂ)⁻¹ •
    ∑ u ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun u : GL (Fin N) (ZMod g) => P (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))),
      MonoidAlgebra.single u (ψ₀ (φ (u : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g))))⁻¹

def eU (g N : ℕ) [NeZero g] (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :=
  eDat g N IsUniUp (fun M => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (N - 1), entN M i (i + 1)) ψ₀

def phiJ {g : ℕ} (kk s : ℕ) {N : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (ZMod g)) : ZMod g :=
  (∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), entN M i (i + 1)) +
    (if 1 ≤ s then entN M (s - 1) (s + kk) + entN M (s + kk) (s + kk + 1) else 0) +
    (∑ i ∈ Finset.range (s - 1), entN M (s + kk + 1 + i) (s + kk + 2 + i)) +
    ∑ a ∈ Finset.range (kk - 1), entN M (s + a) (s + a + 1)

def eJ (g N : ℕ) [NeZero g] (kk s : ℕ) (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod g) ℂ) : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin N) (ZMod g)) :=
  eDat g N (fun M => IsUniUp M ∧ ∀ a, s ≤ a → a < s + kk → entN M a (s + kk) = 0) (phiJ kk s) ψ₀

def lawAt (k : Fin 2) (a : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k)))) (CL : ℝ) : Prop :=
  (∀ f, 0 ≤ wtOf D k a f) ∧
  (∀ f, IsLabel D k f → Squarefree f →
    (1 - 1 / (D.host.g k : ℝ)) / (D.host.g k : ℝ) ^ D.R.r ≤ wtOf D k a f ∧
    wtOf D k a f ≤ (1 + CL * D.R.r / (D.host.g k : ℝ)) / (D.host.g k : ℝ) ^ D.R.r) ∧
  ∀ S : Finset (ZMod (D.host.g k))[X], (∀ f ∈ S, ¬ Squarefree f) →
    ∑ f ∈ S, wtOf D k a f ≤ 3 / (D.host.g k : ℝ)

def eUk (k : Fin 2) (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) :
    MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k))) :=
  haveI : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  eU (D.host.g k) D.R.r ψ₀

def eJk (k : Fin 2) (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) :
    MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k))) :=
  haveI : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  eJ (D.host.g k) D.R.r D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) ψ₀

def ConclusionL : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Fin 2) (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ), ψ₀ ≠ 1 → 2 * D.R.r ≤ D.host.g k →
    lawAt D k (eUk D k ψ₀) 6 ∧ lawAt D k (eJk D k ψ₀) 6

def admLq : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Fin 2) (ψ₀ : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ), ψ₀ ≠ 1 →

abbrev phi (x y : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) (c : X.B.I.Gf) : ℂ := ⟪x, X.B.R (1, c) y⟫_ℂ

theorem R_facts :
    (∀ a b : X.B.I.GA, X.B.R (a * b) = (X.B.R a).comp (X.B.R b)) ∧ (∀ a F, ‖X.B.R a F‖ = ‖F‖) ∧
      (∀ x y : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund, StronglyMeasurable (phi X x y)) := by
  have : X.B.μf.IsHaarMeasure := X.B.haar
  have hU := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.GfUnimod_holds X.B.I X.B.μf
  have hFR := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.foldReady_holds X.B.I
  have := hFR.2.1
  have := hFR.2.2.2.1
  have : X.B.μf.IsMulRightInvariant := hU
  have : (X.B.I.muA X.B.μf).IsMulLeftInvariant := rr_muA_left X.B.I X.B.μf
  have : (X.B.I.muA X.B.μf).IsMulRightInvariant := rr_muA_right X.B.I X.B.μf
  have : SigmaFinite (X.B.I.muA X.B.μf) := rr_muA_sigmaFinite X.B.I X.B.μf
  have h1 := rr_FD_of X.B.I X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.fund_spec
  have h2 := rr_IsReg_of X.B.I X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R X.B.R_spec
  refine ⟨fun a b => rr_mul h1 h2 a b, fun a F => rr_norm h1 h2 a F, fun x y => ?_⟩
  exact (rr_weak_meas h1 h2 x y).comp_measurable (measurable_const.prodMk measurable_id)

theorem phi_bound (x y : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) (c : X.B.I.Gf) : ‖phi X x y c‖ ≤ ‖x‖ * ‖y‖ := by
  have h := (R_facts X).2.1 (1, c) y
  calc ‖phi X x y c‖ ≤ ‖x‖ * ‖X.B.R (1, c) y‖ := norm_inner_le_norm _ _
    _ = ‖x‖ * ‖y‖ := by rw [h]

theorem isRConvF_conv {f g : X.B.I.Gf → ℂ} {T U : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund}
    (hf : X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R f T) (hg : X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R g U) :
    X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (X.B.I.convF X.B.μf f g) (T.comp U) := by
  obtain ⟨hmul, -, hmeas⟩ := R_facts X
  have : X.B.μf.IsHaarMeasure := X.B.haar
  have : SecondCountableTopology X.B.I.Gf := rr_secondCountable X.B.I X.B.μf
  have hK : Integrable (fun p : X.B.I.Gf × X.B.I.Gf => f p.1 * g (p.1⁻¹ * p.2)) (X.B.μf.prod X.B.μf) := by
    have h0 : Integrable (fun q : X.B.I.Gf × X.B.I.Gf => f q.1 * g q.2) (X.B.μf.prod X.B.μf) := hf.1.mul_prod hg.1
    exact ((measurePreserving_prod_inv_mul X.B.μf X.B.μf).integrable_comp h0.aestronglyMeasurable).2 h0
  have hF : ∀ x y : X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund, Integrable (fun p : X.B.I.Gf × X.B.I.Gf =>
      f p.1 * g (p.1⁻¹ * p.2) * phi X x y p.2) (X.B.μf.prod X.B.μf) := fun x y =>
    hK.mul_bdd ((hmeas x y).comp_measurable measurable_snd).aestronglyMeasurable
      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => phi_bound X x y p.2)
  refine ⟨hK.integral_prod_right, fun x y => ?_⟩
  have step : ∀ a, ⟪x, X.B.R (1, a) (U y)⟫_ℂ = ∫ c, g (a⁻¹ * c) * phi X x y c ∂X.B.μf := by
    intro a
    rw [← ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, hg.2]
    have e : ∀ b, g b * ⟪ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint (X.B.R (1, a)) x, X.B.R (1, b) y⟫_ℂ =
        g b * phi X x y (a * b) := by
      intro b
      rw [ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ← hmul, Prod.mk_mul_mk, one_mul]
    simp_rw [e]
    rw [← integral_mul_left_eq_self (fun c => g (a⁻¹ * c) * phi X x y c) a]
    congr 1
    funext b
    simp only [inv_mul_cancel_left]
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, hf.2]
  simp_rw [step, ← integral_const_mul]
  have hF' : Integrable (Function.uncurry fun a c => f a * (g (a⁻¹ * c) * phi X x y c)) (X.B.μf.prod X.B.μf) := by
    have := hF x y
    simp only [mul_assoc] at this
    exact this
  rw [integral_integral_swap hF']
  congr 1
  funext c
  unfold AutInst.convF
  rw [← integral_mul_const]
  congr 1
  funext a
  ring

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul

theorem etEmpty : EtEmpty X := by
  intro φ
  have hES : ∀ g, (ES (D := D) ∅).coeff g = 0 := by
    intro g
    simp [ES]
  have hg : egA X (ES (D := D) ∅) = 0 := by
    funext y
    simp [egA, hES]
  have he : eT X ∅ = 0 := by
    funext x
    simp [eT, Formalism.MainNotions.S.AutInst.convF, hg]
  unfold ReT
  split_ifs with h
  · have hT := (Classical.choose_spec h).2
    have h0 : ∀ x, ⟪x, (Classical.choose h) φ⟫_ℂ = 0 := fun x => by
      rw [hT x φ, he]
      simp
    exact inner_self_eq_zero.mp (h0 _)
  · rfl

theorem invInvariant_muf : X.B.μf.IsInvInvariant := by
  have : X.B.μf.IsMulRightInvariant := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.GfUnimod_holds X.B.I X.B.μf
  have hK := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.level_subgroup_is_group X
  have hL := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.level_subgroup_properties X
  have he1 : X.B.e 1 ≠ 0 := by
    rw [X.e_eq]
    have h1 : (1 : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 := hK.1
    rw [if_pos (show (1 : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting.K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw from h1)]
    have hc : X.chi2 1 ≠ 0 := by
      intro h0; have := hK.2.2.2.2 1 h1; rw [h0, norm_zero] at this; exact zero_ne_one this
    have hv : ((X.B.μf X.K2).toReal : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [Complex.ofReal_ne_zero]; exact (ENNReal.toReal_pos hL.2.2.1.ne' hL.2.2.2.ne).ne'
    exact div_ne_zero (star_ne_zero.2 hc) hv
  exact Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.inv_invariant_of_bi_invariant X.B.μf (fun g => ‖X.B.e g‖) X.B.e_adm.1.continuous.norm
    X.B.Re_spec.1.norm (norm_pos_iff.2 he1)

def EtSA : Prop := ∀ (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (x : X.B.I.Gf), star (eT X S x⁻¹) = eT X S x

theorem etAdj_of (hSA : EtSA X) : EtAdj X := by
  haveI := invInvariant_muf X
  intro S φ ψ
  have hT := reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) S
  have hreg : ∀ g : X.B.I.Gf, ⟪X.B.R ((1, g) : X.B.I.GA) φ, ψ⟫_ℂ = ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ := by
    intro g
    have := Formalism.L2.SG.EvalTOntoPhi.inner_reg X ((1, g⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) φ ψ
    rw [this, Prod.inv_mk, inv_one, inv_inv]
  calc ⟪ReT X S φ, ψ⟫_ℂ = star ⟪ψ, ReT X S φ⟫_ℂ := (inner_conj_symm _ _).symm
    _ = star (∫ g, eT X S g * ⟪ψ, X.B.R ((1, g) : X.B.I.GA) φ⟫_ℂ ∂X.B.μf) := by rw [hT.2]
    _ = ∫ g, star (eT X S g) * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ ∂X.B.μf := by
        rw [← starRingEnd_apply, ← integral_conj]
        refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => ?_)
        show (starRingEnd ℂ) (eT X S g * ⟪ψ, X.B.R ((1, g) : X.B.I.GA) φ⟫_ℂ) = _
        rw [map_mul]
        show _ = star (eT X S g) * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ
        rw [← hreg g, inner_conj_symm]
        rfl
    _ = ∫ g, star (eT X S g⁻¹) * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ ∂X.B.μf := by
        rw [← MeasureTheory.integral_inv_eq_self (fun g => star (eT X S g) * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ) X.B.μf]
        refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => ?_)
        show star (eT X S g⁻¹) * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g⁻¹⁻¹) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ = _
        rw [inv_inv]
    _ = ∫ g, eT X S g * ⟪φ, X.B.R ((1, g) : X.B.I.GA) ψ⟫_ℂ ∂X.B.μf := by
        refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => ?_)
        show star (eT X S g⁻¹) * _ = _
        rw [hSA S g]
    _ = ⟪φ, ReT X S ψ⟫_ℂ := (hT.2 φ ψ).symm

theorem etGood_of (G : GoodOp X)
    (hEC : ∀ S S' : Set (Tup2 D), X.B.I.convF X.B.μf (egA X (ES S')) (eT X S) = eT X (S' ∩ S)) : EtGood X G := by
  intro t ht φ
  have h1 := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul.isRConvF_conv X G.Rg_spec (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) {t})
  have e : X.B.I.convF X.B.μf (eg X) (eT X {t}) = eT X {t} := by
    rw [← goodIdempotent_eq, ← ES_good, hEC]
    congr 1
    exact Set.inter_eq_right.2 (Set.singleton_subset_iff.2 ht)
  rw [e] at h1
  have h2 := isRConvF_unique X h1 (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) {t})
  exact congrArg (fun T : L2X X →L[ℂ] L2X X => T φ) h2

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowsGoodRed

theorem eT_inter (S S' : Set (Tup2 D)) : X.B.I.convF X.B.μf (eT X S) (eT X S') = eT X (S ∩ S') := by
  refine hat_inj hS (biInv_conv hS (biInv_eT X S) (biInv_eT X S')) (biInv_eT X _) fun γ => ?_
  rw [conv_hat hS (biInv_eT X S) (biInv_eT X S'), hat_eT' hS]
  simp only [hat_eT' hS]
  rw [← coeff_mul_fin]
  have key : (Ehat hS * ES S) * (Ehat hS * ES S') = Ehat hS * ES (S ∩ S') := by
    calc (Ehat hS * ES S) * (Ehat hS * ES S') = Ehat hS * (ES S * Ehat hS) * ES S' := by noncomm_ring
      _ = Ehat hS * (Ehat hS * ES S) * ES S' := by rw [ES_central]
      _ = (Ehat hS * Ehat hS) * (ES S * ES S') := by noncomm_ring
      _ = Ehat hS * ES (S ∩ S') := by rw [Ehat_idem, ES_inter]
  rw [key]

theorem biInv_sum {ι : Type} (F : Finset ι) (f : ι → X.B.I.Gf → ℂ) (h : ∀ i, BiInv X (f i)) :
    BiInv X (fun x => ∑ i ∈ F, f i x) := by
  refine ⟨fun x hx => ?_, fun x n hn => ?_, fun x n hn => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨i, _, hi⟩ := Finset.exists_ne_zero_of_sum_ne_zero hx
    exact (h i).1 x hi
  · exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => (h i).2.1 x n hn
  · exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => (h i).2.2 x n hn

theorem hat_sum {ι : Type} (F : Finset ι) (f : ι → X.B.I.Gf → ℂ) (γ : G0g D.nd) :
    hat hS (fun x => ∑ i ∈ F, f i x) γ = ∑ i ∈ F, hat hS (f i) γ := by
  unfold hat; rw [Finset.mul_sum]

open Classical in

theorem eT_split (S : Set (Tup2 D)) :
    eT X S = fun x => ∑ t ∈ (labT (D := D)).filter (· ∈ S), eT X {t} x := by
  refine hat_inj hS (biInv_eT X S) (biInv_sum _ _ fun t => biInv_eT X {t}) fun γ => ?_
  rw [hat_sum, hat_eT' hS, ES_split, Finset.mul_sum, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, Finset.sum_apply']
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun t _ => ?_
  rw [hat_eT' hS]

theorem egA_conv_eT (S S' : Set (Tup2 D)) :
    X.B.I.convF X.B.μf (egA X (ES S')) (eT X S) = eT X (S' ∩ S) := by
  have hS := hS_X X
  refine hat_inj hS (biInv_conv hS (biInv_egA X _ (ES_conj S')) (biInv_eT X S)) (biInv_eT X _) fun γ => ?_
  rw [conv_hat hS (biInv_egA X _ (ES_conj S')) (biInv_eT X S), hat_eT' hS]
  simp only [hat_egA hS _ (ES_conj S'), hat_eT' hS]
  rw [← coeff_mul_fin]
  have key : ES S' * (Ehat hS * ES S) = Ehat hS * ES (S' ∩ S) := by
    rw [← mul_assoc, ES_central, mul_assoc, ES_inter]
  rw [key]

theorem reT_inter (S S' : Set (Tup2 D)) : (ReT X S).comp (ReT X S') = ReT X (S ∩ S') := by
  have h := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul.isRConvF_conv X (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) S) (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) S')
  rw [eT_inter (hS_X X) S S'] at h
  exact isRConvF_unique X h (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) (S ∩ S'))

  rw [eT_inv (hS_X X) S x, star_star]

theorem isRConvF_sum {ι : Type} (F : Finset ι) (f : ι → X.B.I.Gf → ℂ)
    (T : ι → (X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund))
    (h : ∀ i, X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (f i) (T i)) :
    X.B.I.IsRConvF X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R (fun x => ∑ i ∈ F, f i x) (∑ i ∈ F, T i) := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, hmeas⟩ := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul.R_facts X
  refine ⟨integrable_finsetSum _ fun i _ => (h i).1, fun x y => ?_⟩
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.sum_apply, inner_sum]
  simp_rw [Finset.sum_mul]
  rw [integral_finsetSum _ fun i _ => (h i).1.mul_bdd ((hmeas x y).aestronglyMeasurable)
    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun c => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul.phi_bound X x y c)]
  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => (h i).2 x y

  have h := isRConvF_sum X ((labT (D := D)).filter (· ∈ S)) (fun t => eT X {t}) (fun t => ReT X {t})

  exact isRConvF_unique X (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) S) h

theorem reT_orth (t t' : Tup2 D) :
    (ReT X {t}).comp (ReT X {t'}) = if t = t' then ReT X {t} else 0 := by
  rw [reT_inter]
  split_ifs with h
  · rw [h, Set.inter_self]
  · have e : ({t} : Set (Tup2 D)) ∩ {t'} = ∅ := by
      ext u; simp only [Set.mem_inter_iff, Set.mem_singleton_iff, Set.mem_empty_iff_false, iff_false]
      rintro ⟨rfl, rfl⟩; exact h rfl
    rw [e]
    exact ContinuousLinearMap.ext fun φ => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowsGoodRed.etEmpty X φ

end Split

def HH (Re T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) : Submodule ℂ (I.L2 μf fund) :=
  LinearMap.range ((Re.comp T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) : I.L2 μf fund →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund)

def evAt {Hs : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup Hs] [InnerProductSpace ℂ Hs]
    (σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)) (R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund))
    (Re : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) (w₀ : Hs) :
    I.eHom σs μf fund R Re →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund :=
  ((ContinuousLinearMap.apply ℂ (I.L2 μf fund) w₀ : (Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) :
      (Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund).comp (I.eHom σs μf fund R Re).subtype

end Defs

def RowsProj : Prop :=
  ∀ (I : AutInst) (μf : Measure I.Gf) [SigmaFinite μf] [μf.IsHaarMeasure] (fund : Set I.GA),
    I.IsFundDom (I.muA μf) fund →
    ∀ R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund), I.IsRightReg μf fund R →
    ∀ (e : I.Gf → ℂ) (Re : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund),
      IsLocallyConstant e → I.convF μf e e = e → (∀ x, e x⁻¹ = star (e x)) →
      I.IsRConvF μf fund R e Re →
    ∀ (H : InfinitePlace I.F → Type) [∀ v, NormedAddCommGroup (H v)] [∀ v, InnerProductSpace ℂ (H v)]
      (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)) (dv : InfinitePlace I.F → ℝ) (w : ∀ v, H v),
      (∀ v, IsURepOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) → (∀ v, IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v)) (σ v) (dv v)) →
      (∀ v, ‖w v‖ = 1) → (∀ v, Integrable (phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v)) (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v))) →
      ∀ T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund,
        IsOpInt (muInf I) (fun g => R (g, 1)) (phInf I σ w dv) T →
        Re.comp T = T.comp Re ∧
        I.IsRConv μf fund R (fun g => phInf I σ w dv g.1 * e g.2) (Re.comp T) ∧
        (∀ x y : I.L2 μf fund, ⟪(Re.comp T) x, y⟫_ℂ = ⟪x, (Re.comp T) y⟫_ℂ) ∧
        (Re.comp T).comp (Re.comp T) = Re.comp T

def RowsBij : Prop :=
  ∀ (I : AutInst) (μf : Measure I.Gf) [SigmaFinite μf] [μf.IsHaarMeasure] (fund : Set I.GA),
    I.IsFundDom (I.muA μf) fund →
    ∀ R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund), I.IsRightReg μf fund R →
    ∀ (e : I.Gf → ℂ) (Re : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund), I.IsRConvF μf fund R e Re →
    ∀ (H : InfinitePlace I.F → Type) [∀ v, NormedAddCommGroup (H v)] [∀ v, InnerProductSpace ℂ (H v)]
      [∀ v, CompleteSpace (H v)]
      (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)) (dv : InfinitePlace I.F → ℝ) (w : ∀ v, H v),
      (∀ v, IsURepOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) → (∀ v, IsIrredOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) →
      (∀ v, IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v)) (σ v) (dv v)) →
      (∀ v, ‖w v‖ = 1) → (∀ v, Integrable (phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v)) (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v))) →
      ∀ T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund,
        IsOpInt (muInf I) (fun g => R (g, 1)) (phInf I σ w dv) T →
      ∀ (Hs : Type) [NormedAddCommGroup Hs] [InnerProductSpace ℂ Hs] [CompleteSpace Hs]
        (σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)) (tens : (∀ v, H v) → Hs), I.IsHilbTensor σ σs tens →
        Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re (tens w)) ∧
          LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re (tens w)) = HH I μf fund Re T

def RowsFinDim : Prop :=
  ∀ (I : AutInst) (μf : Measure I.Gf) [SigmaFinite μf] [μf.IsHaarMeasure] (fund : Set I.GA),
    I.IsFundDom (I.muA μf) fund →
    ∀ R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund), I.IsRightReg μf fund R →
    ∀ (e : I.Gf → ℂ) (Re : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund),
      IsLocallyConstant e → (∀ x, e x ≠ 0 → x ∈ I.Kset) → I.IsRConvF μf fund R e Re →
    ∀ (H : InfinitePlace I.F → Type) [∀ v, NormedAddCommGroup (H v)] [∀ v, InnerProductSpace ℂ (H v)]
      [∀ v, CompleteSpace (H v)]
      (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)) (dv : InfinitePlace I.F → ℝ) (w : ∀ v, H v),
      (∀ v, IsURepOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) → (∀ v, IsIrredOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) →
      (∀ v, IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v)) (σ v) (dv v)) →
      (∀ v, ‖w v‖ = 1) → (∀ v, Integrable (phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v)) (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v))) →
      ∀ T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund,
        IsOpInt (muInf I) (fun g => R (g, 1)) (phInf I σ w dv) T →
        FiniteDimensional ℂ (HH I μf fund Re T)

section Count

variable {I : AutInst} {μf : Measure I.Gf} [SigmaFinite μf] {fund : Set I.GA}
  {Hs : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup Hs] [InnerProductSpace ℂ Hs]
  {σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)} {R : I.GA → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund)}
  {Re T : I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund} {w₀ : Hs}

def eHomEquivHH (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T) :
    I.eHom σs μf fund R Re ≃ₗ[ℂ] HH I μf fund Re T :=
  (LinearEquiv.ofInjective _ hinj).trans (LinearEquiv.ofEq _ _ hran)

theorem finrank_HH_eq_Dcount (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T) :
    Module.finrank ℂ (HH I μf fund Re T) = I.Dcount σs μf fund R Re :=
  ((eHomEquivHH hinj hran).finrank_eq).symm

theorem rank_HH_eq (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T) :
    Module.rank ℂ (HH I μf fund Re T) = Module.rank ℂ (I.eHom σs μf fund R Re) :=
  ((eHomEquivHH hinj hran).rank_eq).symm

theorem finiteDimensional_eHom (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T)
    [hfin : FiniteDimensional ℂ (HH I μf fund Re T)] :
    FiniteDimensional ℂ (I.eHom σs μf fund R Re) :=
  LinearEquiv.finiteDimensional (eHomEquivHH hinj hran).symm

theorem Dcount_pos_iff (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T)
    [hfin : FiniteDimensional ℂ (HH I μf fund Re T)] :
    0 < I.Dcount σs μf fund R Re ↔ HH I μf fund Re T ≠ ⊥ := by
  rw [← finrank_HH_eq_Dcount hinj hran, Nat.pos_iff_ne_zero, Ne, Submodule.finrank_eq_zero]

theorem finiteDimensional_HH_of_eHom (hinj : Function.Injective (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀))
    (hran : LinearMap.range (evAt I μf fund σs R Re w₀) = HH I μf fund Re T)
    [hfin : FiniteDimensional ℂ (I.eHom σs μf fund R Re)] :

theorem loc_schur {n : ℕ} {ε : Fin n → Bool} {H : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup H] [InnerProductSpace ℂ H]
    {σ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℂ → (H →L[ℂ] H)} {d : ℝ} {w : H} (x : H)
    (hF : IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG ε) σ d) (hw : ‖w‖ = 1) :
    ∫ g, phLoc (ε := ε) σ w d g * ⟪x, σ (umat g) w⟫_ℂ ∂(Formalism.REAL.muG ε) = ⟪x, w⟫_ℂ := by
  obtain ⟨hd, hF2⟩ := hF
  have hd' : (d : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast hd.ne'
  have hph : ∀ g, Formalism.L2.SG.ProjRows.phLoc (ε := ε) σ w d g * ⟪x, σ (umat g) w⟫_ℂ =
      (d : ℂ) * (coefOn σ w x g * star (coefOn σ w w g)) := by
    intro g
    simp only [Formalism.L2.SG.ProjRows.phLoc, coefOn]
    ring
  simp_rw [hph]
  have hww : ⟪w, w⟫_ℂ = 1 := by
    rw [inner_self_eq_norm_sq_to_K, hw]
    simp
  rw [MeasureTheory.integral_const_mul, (hF2 w x w w).2, hww, one_mul]
  field_simp

section Glob

variable (I : AutInst) {H : InfinitePlace I.F → Type} [∀ v, NormedAddCommGroup (H v)]
  [∀ v, InnerProductSpace ℂ (H v)]
  {Hs : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup Hs] [InnerProductSpace ℂ Hs]

theorem orbit_eq (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v))
    (σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)) (tens : (∀ v, H v) → Hs) (htens : I.IsHilbTensor σ σs tens)
    (w : ∀ v, H v) (g : I.Ginf) :
    σs g (tens w) = tens fun v => σ v (umat (g v)) (w v) := htens.2.1 g w

theorem loc_cyc {n : ℕ} {ε : Fin n → Bool} {K : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup K] [InnerProductSpace ℂ K]
    {σ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℂ → (K →L[ℂ] K)} (hU : IsURepOn (UJset ε) σ) (hI : IsIrredOn (UJset ε) σ)
    {w : K} (hw : ‖w‖ = 1) :
    (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ ε => σ (umat g) w)).topologicalClosure = ⊤ := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨⟨h1, hmul⟩, hiso, hcont⟩ := hU
  obtain ⟨sp, hsp⟩ : ∃ sp : Submodule ℂ K,
      sp = Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ ε => σ (umat g) w) := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hsp]
  have hJJ : Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε = 1 := by
    rw [Formalism.REAL.Jm, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, ← Matrix.diagonal_one]
    congr 1
    funext a
    split_ifs <;> norm_num

  have hunit : ∀ m ∈ UJset ε, ∃ g : Formalism.REAL.UJ ε, umat g = m := by
    intro m hm
    have hm' : (Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * m = Formalism.REAL.Jm ε := hm
    have hl : Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * (Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * m = 1 := by
      have e : Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * (Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * m = Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * ((Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * m) := by
        simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
      rw [e, hm', hJJ]
    have hr : m * (Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * (Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε) = 1 := mul_eq_one_comm.mp hl
    exact ⟨⟨⟨m, Formalism.REAL.Jm ε * (Matrix.conjTranspose m) * Formalism.REAL.Jm ε, hr, hl⟩, hm⟩, rfl⟩
  have hinv : ∀ m ∈ UJset ε, ∀ v ∈ sp.topologicalClosure, σ m v ∈ sp.topologicalClosure := by
    intro m hm v hv
    obtain ⟨g', rfl⟩ := hunit m hm
    have hspan : sp ≤ sp.comap (σ (umat g') : K →ₗ[ℂ] K) := by
      rw [hsp, Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
      rw [SetLike.mem_coe, Submodule.mem_comap]
      change σ (umat g') (σ (umat g) w) ∈ Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ ε => σ (umat g) w)
      rw [← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply,
        ← hmul _ (Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeCSchur.umat_mem g') _ (Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeCSchur.umat_mem g)]
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨g' * g, rfl⟩
    have hcl : sp.topologicalClosure ≤ sp.topologicalClosure.comap (σ (umat g') : K →ₗ[ℂ] K) := by
      apply Submodule.topologicalClosure_minimal
      · exact hspan.trans (Submodule.comap_mono (Submodule.le_topologicalClosure _))
      · exact (Submodule.isClosed_topologicalClosure _).preimage (σ (umat g')).continuous
    exact hcl hv
  rcases hI _ (Submodule.isClosed_topologicalClosure _) hinv with h | h
  · exfalso
    have hw0 : w ∈ sp.topologicalClosure := by
      apply Submodule.le_topologicalClosure
      rw [hsp]
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, by simp [umat, h1]⟩
    rw [h, Submodule.mem_bot] at hw0
    rw [hw0, norm_zero] at hw
    exact zero_ne_one hw
  · exact h

theorem glob_cyc [∀ v, CompleteSpace (H v)]
    (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)) (w : ∀ v, H v)
    (hU : ∀ v, IsURepOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) (hIrr : ∀ v, IsIrredOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v))
    (hw : ∀ v, ‖w v‖ = 1)
    (σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)) (tens : (∀ v, H v) → Hs) (htens : I.IsHilbTensor σ σs tens) :
    (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : I.Ginf => σs g (tens w))).topologicalClosure = ⊤ := by
  classical
  have htin := htens.1
  obtain ⟨M, hM⟩ : ∃ M : Submodule ℂ Hs,
      M = (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : I.Ginf => σs g (tens w))).topologicalClosure := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hM]
  have hMc : IsClosed (M : Set Hs) := by
    rw [hM]
    exact Submodule.isClosed_topologicalClosure _
  have hall : ∀ x, tens x ∈ M := by
    refine tens_mem_of tens htin M hMc (fun v => Set.range fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ (I.ε v) => σ v (umat g) (w v))
      (fun v => loc_cyc (hU v) (hIrr v) (hw v)) ?_
    intro x hx
    choose g hg using hx
    have hx' : tens x = σs g (tens w) := by
      rw [htens.2.1 g w]
      congr 1
      funext v
      exact (hg v).symm
    rw [hx', hM]
    exact Submodule.le_topologicalClosure _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨g, rfl⟩)
  rw [eq_top_iff]
  intro z _
  have hsub : (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range tens) : Set Hs) ⊆ M :=
    Submodule.span_le.mpr (by rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩; exact hall x)
  exact hMc.closure_subset_iff.mpr hsub (htens.2.2 z)

theorem glob_schur (σ : ∀ v, Matrix (Fin I.n) (Fin I.n) ℂ → (H v →L[ℂ] H v)) (dv : InfinitePlace I.F → ℝ)
    (w : ∀ v, H v)
    (hU : ∀ v, IsURepOn (UJset (I.ε v)) (σ v)) (hF : ∀ v, IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v)) (σ v) (dv v))
    (hw : ∀ v, ‖w v‖ = 1) (hL : ∀ v, Integrable (phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v)) (Formalism.REAL.muG (I.ε v)))
    (σs : I.Ginf → (Hs →L[ℂ] Hs)) (tens : (∀ v, H v) → Hs) (htens : I.IsHilbTensor σ σs tens) (u : Hs) :
    Integrable (fun g => phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪u, σs g (tens w)⟫_ℂ) (muInf I) ∧
      ∫ g, phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪u, σs g (tens w)⟫_ℂ ∂(muInf I) = ⟪u, tens w⟫_ℂ := by
  classical
  have hρ : ∀ v, IsURep (fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ (I.ε v) => σ v (umat g)) := fun v => isURep_of_on (hU v)
  have hph : Integrable (phInf I σ w dv) (muInf I) := by
    rw [phInf_eq]; exact Integrable.fintype_prod_dep hL

  set F : I.Ginf → Hs := fun g => σs g (tens w) with hFdef
  have hFc : Continuous F := by
    have : F = tens ∘ fun g v => σ v (umat (g v)) (w v) := by
      funext g; exact orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w g
    rw [this]
    exact (tens_continuous tens htens.1).comp
      (continuous_pi fun v => ((hρ v).2.2.2 (w v)).comp (continuous_apply v))
  have hF1 : ∀ g, ‖F g‖ = 1 := by
    intro g
    have h1 : ⟪F g, F g⟫_ℂ = 1 := by
      simp only [hFdef]
      rw [orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w g, htens.1]
      refine Finset.prod_eq_one fun v _ => ?_
      let L : H v →ₗᵢ[ℂ] H v := ⟨(σ v (umat (g v)) : H v →ₗ[ℂ] H v), fun z => (hρ v).2.2.1 (g v) z⟩
      have := L.inner_map_map (w v) (w v)
      simp only [L] at this
      rw [show (σ v (umat (g v))) (w v) = L (w v) from rfl, this, inner_self_eq_norm_sq_to_K, hw v]
      simp
    rw [norm_eq_sqrt_re_inner (𝕜 := ℂ), h1]
    simp
  have hint : ∀ u : Hs, Integrable (fun g => phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪u, F g⟫_ℂ) (muInf I) := by
    intro u
    refine Integrable.mono' (hph.norm.mul_const ‖u‖) (hph.aestronglyMeasurable.mul
      (continuous_const.inner hFc).aestronglyMeasurable) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => ?_)
    rw [norm_mul]
    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (norm_nonneg _)
    calc ‖⟪u, F g⟫_ℂ‖ ≤ ‖u‖ * ‖F g‖ := norm_inner_le_norm _ _
      _ = ‖u‖ := by rw [hF1, mul_one]
  refine ⟨hint u, ?_⟩

  let Λ : Hs → ℂ := fun u => ∫ g, phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪u, F g⟫_ℂ ∂(muInf I)
  have hadd : ∀ a b, Λ (a + b) = Λ a + Λ b := by
    intro a b
    simp only [Λ, inner_add_left, mul_add]
    exact integral_add (hint a) (hint b)
  have hsmul : ∀ (c : ℂ) a, Λ (c • a) = (starRingEnd ℂ) c * Λ a := by
    intro c a
    simp only [Λ, inner_smul_left]
    rw [← integral_const_mul]
    congr 1; funext g; ring
  let ΛL : Hs →L⋆[ℂ] ℂ := LinearMap.mkContinuous
    { toFun := Λ, map_add' := hadd, map_smul' := fun c a => by rw [hsmul]; rfl }
    (∫ g, ‖phInf I σ w dv g‖ ∂(muInf I)) (fun a => by
      show ‖Λ a‖ ≤ _
      calc ‖Λ a‖ ≤ ∫ g, ‖phInf I σ w dv g‖ * ‖a‖ ∂(muInf I) := by
            refine norm_integral_le_of_norm_le (hph.norm.mul_const ‖a‖) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => ?_)
            rw [norm_mul]
            refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (norm_nonneg _)
            calc ‖⟪a, F g⟫_ℂ‖ ≤ ‖a‖ * ‖F g‖ := norm_inner_le_norm _ _
              _ = ‖a‖ := by rw [hF1, mul_one]
        _ = (∫ g, ‖phInf I σ w dv g‖ ∂(muInf I)) * ‖a‖ := integral_mul_const _ _)
  have hΛL : ∀ a, ΛL a = Λ a := fun a => rfl

  let Z : Submodule ℂ Hs :=
    { carrier := {a | Λ a = ⟪a, tens w⟫_ℂ}
      add_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => by
        simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq] at *; rw [hadd, ha, hb, inner_add_left]
      zero_mem' := by
        simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq, Λ, inner_zero_left, mul_zero, integral_zero]
      smul_mem' := fun c a ha => by
        simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq] at *; rw [hsmul, ha, inner_smul_left] }
  have hZc : IsClosed (Z : Set Hs) := by
    have : (Z : Set Hs) = {a | ΛL a = ⟪a, tens w⟫_ℂ} := rfl
    rw [this]
    exact isClosed_eq ΛL.continuous (continuous_id.inner continuous_const)

  have hpure : ∀ x, tens x ∈ Z := by
    intro x
    show Λ (tens x) = ⟪tens x, tens w⟫_ℂ
    have e1 : ∀ g : I.Ginf, phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪tens x, F g⟫_ℂ =
        ∏ v, (phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v) (g v) * ⟪x v, σ v (umat (g v)) (w v)⟫_ℂ) := by
      intro g
      simp only [hFdef]
      rw [orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w g, htens.1, phInf_eq, Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
    simp only [Λ]
    simp_rw [e1]
    rw [integral_fintype_prod_eq_prod (fun v (g : Formalism.REAL.UJ (I.ε v)) =>
      phLoc (ε := I.ε v) (σ v) (w v) (dv v) g * ⟪x v, σ v (umat g) (w v)⟫_ℂ), htens.1]
    exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => loc_schur (x v) (hF v) (hw v)
  have hle : Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range tens) ≤ Z := Submodule.span_le.2 (by rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩; exact hpure x)
  have : u ∈ closure (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range tens) : Set Hs) := by rw [htens.2.2.closure_eq]; trivial
  exact (closure_minimal hle hZc) this

end Glob

theorem rows_bijective (hP : ProjRowsR) (hUn : UnimodGf) : RowsBij := by
  classical
  intro I μf _ _ fund hfund R hR e Re hRe H _ _ _ σ dv w hU hIrr hF hw hL T hT Hs _ _ _ σs tens htens
  obtain ⟨hmul, hone, hiso⟩ := reg_alg hUn I μf fund hfund R hR
  have hRP : IsRowProj (fun g => R (g, 1)) (mInf I σ w) T := hP I μf fund hfund R hR H σ dv w hU hF hw hL T hT
  have hρ : ∀ v, IsURep (fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ (I.ε v) => σ v (umat g)) := fun v => isURep_of_on (hU v)
  set tw := tens w with htw
  set π : I.Ginf → (I.L2 μf fund →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) := fun g => R (g, 1) with hπ
  have hπmul : ∀ a b, π (a * b) = (π a).comp (π b) := by
    intro a b; simp only [hπ]; rw [← hmul, Prod.mk_mul_mk, mul_one]
  have hπiso : ∀ a F, ‖π a F‖ = ‖F‖ := fun a F => hiso _ F
  have hπone : π 1 = 1 := hone

  have hcomm : ∀ g : I.Ginf, Re.comp (π g) = (π g).comp Re := by
    intro g
    ext1 y
    refine ext_inner_left ℂ fun x => ?_
    simp only [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, hπ]
    rw [hRe.2, ← ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, hRe.2]
    congr 1; funext h
    rw [ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply (R (1, h)),
      ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply (R (g, 1)), ← hmul, ← hmul]
    congr 3
    ext <;> simp

  have hσmul : ∀ a b : I.Ginf, σs a (σs b tw) = σs (a * b) tw := by
    intro a b
    rw [orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w b, htens.2.1, orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w (a * b)]
    congr 1; funext v
    have := (hρ v).2.1 (a v) (b v)
    simp only at this
    rw [show (a * b) v = a v * b v from rfl, this]; rfl
  have hσ1 : σs 1 tw = tw := by
    rw [orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w 1]
    congr 1; funext v
    have := (hρ v).1
    simp only at this
    rw [show (1 : I.Ginf) v = 1 from rfl, this]; rfl
  have hσinner : ∀ a b : I.Ginf, ⟪σs a tw, σs b tw⟫_ℂ = mInf I σ w (a⁻¹ * b) := by
    intro a b
    rw [orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w a, orbit_eq I σ σs tens htens w b, htens.1]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun v _ => ?_
    exact inner_orbit (fun g : Formalism.REAL.UJ (I.ε v) => σ v (umat g)) (hρ v).2.1 (hρ v).2.2.1 (a v) (b v) (w v) (w v)
  have hcyc := glob_cyc I σ w hU hIrr hw σs tens htens

  have hTA : ∀ A : Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund, (∀ g, A.comp (σs g) = (π g).comp A) → T (A tw) = A tw := by
    intro A hA
    refine ext_inner_left ℂ fun x => ?_
    rw [hT x (A tw)]
    have e1 : ∀ g : I.Ginf, phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪x, π g (A tw)⟫_ℂ =
        phInf I σ w dv g * ⟪ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint A x, σs g tw⟫_ℂ := by
      intro g
      rw [ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply (π g) A, ← hA g,
        ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply]
    simp_rw [e1]
    rw [(glob_schur I σ dv w hU hF hw hL σs tens htens _).2, ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left]

  have hexists : ∀ z, T z = z → ∃ A : Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund, (∀ g, A.comp (σs g) = (π g).comp A) ∧ A tw = z := by
    intro z hz
    have hpair : ∀ a b : I.Ginf, ⟪π a z, π b z⟫_ℂ = ⟪z, z⟫_ℂ * ⟪σs a tw, σs b tw⟫_ℂ := by
      intro a b
      rw [inner_orbit π hπmul hπiso, hσinner]
      calc ⟪z, π (a⁻¹ * b) z⟫_ℂ = ⟪T z, π (a⁻¹ * b) (T z)⟫_ℂ := by rw [hz]
        _ = ⟪z, T (π (a⁻¹ * b) (T z))⟫_ℂ := hRP.1 _ _
        _ = ⟪z, (T.comp ((π (a⁻¹ * b)).comp T)) z⟫_ℂ := rfl
        _ = mInf I σ w (a⁻¹ * b) * ⟪z, z⟫_ℂ := by
          rw [hRP.2.2 (a⁻¹ * b)]
          rw [ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply, inner_smul_right, hz]
        _ = ⟪z, z⟫_ℂ * mInf I σ w (a⁻¹ * b) := mul_comm _ _
    let Φ₁ : (I.Ginf →₀ ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] Hs := Finsupp.linearCombination ℂ fun g => σs g tw
    let Φ₂ : (I.Ginf →₀ ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund := Finsupp.linearCombination ℂ fun g => π g z
    have hkey : ∀ c c', ⟪Φ₂ c, Φ₂ c'⟫_ℂ = ⟪z, z⟫_ℂ * ⟪Φ₁ c, Φ₁ c'⟫_ℂ := by
      intro c c'
      simp only [Φ₁, Φ₂, Finsupp.linearCombination_apply, Finsupp.sum, sum_inner, inner_sum,
        inner_smul_left, inner_smul_right, hpair, Finset.mul_sum]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun g _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun h _ => by ring
    have hnorm : ∀ c, ‖Φ₂ c‖ = ‖z‖ * ‖Φ₁ c‖ := by
      intro c
      have h := hkey c c
      rw [inner_self_eq_norm_sq_to_K, inner_self_eq_norm_sq_to_K, inner_self_eq_norm_sq_to_K] at h
      have h' : ‖Φ₂ c‖ ^ 2 = (‖z‖ * ‖Φ₁ c‖) ^ 2 := by rw [mul_pow]; exact_mod_cast h
      exact (pow_left_inj₀ (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity) two_ne_zero).1 h'
    have hker : LinearMap.ker Φ₁ ≤ LinearMap.ker Φ₂ := by
      intro c hc
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hc ⊢
      rw [← norm_eq_zero, hnorm, hc, norm_zero, mul_zero]
    let B : LinearMap.range Φ₁ →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund :=
      ((LinearMap.ker Φ₁).liftQ Φ₂ hker).comp (Φ₁.quotKerEquivRange.symm : LinearMap.range Φ₁ →ₗ[ℂ] _)
    have hB : ∀ c, B ⟨Φ₁ c, LinearMap.mem_range_self Φ₁ c⟩ = Φ₂ c := by
      intro c
      simp only [B, LinearMap.comp_apply, LinearEquiv.coe_coe, LinearMap.quotKerEquivRange_symm_apply_image,
        Submodule.mkQ_apply, Submodule.liftQ_apply]
    have hBn : ∀ y, ‖B y‖ ≤ ‖z‖ * ‖y‖ := by
      rintro ⟨_, c, rfl⟩
      rw [hB, hnorm]
      rfl
    let BL : LinearMap.range Φ₁ →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund := B.mkContinuous ‖z‖ hBn
    let eL : LinearMap.range Φ₁ →L[ℂ] Hs := (LinearMap.range Φ₁).subtypeL
    have hdense : DenseRange eL := by
      have h1 : Set.range eL = (LinearMap.range Φ₁ : Set Hs) := by
        ext x
        constructor
        · rintro ⟨y, rfl⟩; exact y.2
        · intro hx; exact ⟨⟨x, hx⟩, rfl⟩
      rw [DenseRange, h1, Finsupp.range_linearCombination, Submodule.dense_iff_topologicalClosure_eq_top]
      exact hcyc
    have hui : IsUniformInducing eL := isUniformEmbedding_subtype_val.isUniformInducing
    let A := BL.extend eL
    have hAk : ∀ k, A (σs k tw) = π k z := by
      intro k
      have h1 : σs k tw = eL ⟨Φ₁ (Finsupp.single k 1), LinearMap.mem_range_self Φ₁ _⟩ := by
        simp [eL, Φ₁]
      rw [h1, ContinuousLinearMap.extend_eq BL hdense hui]
      show B _ = _
      rw [hB]
      simp [Φ₂]
    refine ⟨A, fun h => ?_, ?_⟩
    ·
      have hker2 : (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : I.Ginf => σs g tw)).topologicalClosure ≤
          LinearMap.ker ((A.comp (σs h) - (π h).comp A : Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) : Hs →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) := by
        refine Submodule.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Submodule.span_le.2 ?_)
          (ContinuousLinearMap.isClosed_ker _)
        rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
        simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, LinearMap.mem_ker, ContinuousLinearMap.coe_coe,
          ContinuousLinearMap.sub_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply]
        rw [hσmul, hAk, hAk, hπmul, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, sub_self]
      rw [hcyc] at hker2
      ext1 u
      have := hker2 (Submodule.mem_top (x := u))
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, ContinuousLinearMap.coe_coe] at this
      exact sub_eq_zero.1 this
    · have := hAk 1
      rwa [hσ1, hπone, ContinuousLinearMap.one_apply] at this

  have hmemE : ∀ S, S ∈ I.eHom σs μf fund R Re ↔
      ∃ A : Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund, (∀ g, A.comp (σs g) = (π g).comp A) ∧ Re.comp A = S := by
    intro S
    simp only [AutInst.eHom, Submodule.mem_map]
    rfl
  constructor
  ·
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    rintro ⟨S, hS⟩ h0
    obtain ⟨A, hA, rfl⟩ := (hmemE S).1 hS
    have h0' : (Re.comp A) tw = 0 := h0
    apply Subtype.ext
    show Re.comp A = 0
    have hker2 : (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : I.Ginf => σs g tw)).topologicalClosure ≤
        LinearMap.ker ((Re.comp A : Hs →L[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) : Hs →ₗ[ℂ] I.L2 μf fund) := by
      refine Submodule.topologicalClosure_minimal _ (Submodule.span_le.2 ?_)
        (ContinuousLinearMap.isClosed_ker _)
      rintro _ ⟨k, rfl⟩
      simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, LinearMap.mem_ker, ContinuousLinearMap.coe_coe]
      rw [← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply (Re.comp A), ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, hA k,
        ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, hcomm k, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc,
        ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, h0', map_zero]
    rw [hcyc] at hker2
    ext1 u
    have := LinearMap.mem_ker.1 (hker2 (Submodule.mem_top (x := u)))
    rw [ContinuousLinearMap.coe_coe] at this
    rw [this, ContinuousLinearMap.zero_apply]
  ·
    ext v
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨⟨S, hS⟩, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨A, hA, rfl⟩ := (hmemE S).1 hS
      refine ⟨A tw, ?_⟩
      show Re (T (A tw)) = Re (A tw)
      rw [hTA A hA]
    · rintro ⟨y, rfl⟩
      have hz : T (T y) = T y := by
        have := hRP.2.1
        rw [← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, this]
      obtain ⟨A, hA, hAw⟩ := hexists (T y) hz
      refine ⟨⟨Re.comp A, (hmemE _).2 ⟨A, hA, rfl⟩⟩, ?_⟩
      show Re (A tw) = Re (T y)
      rw [hAw]

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem reT_rph (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : (ReT X S).comp X.Rph = X.Rph.comp (ReT X S) := by
  refine ContinuousLinearMap.ext fun y => ext_inner_left ℂ fun x => ?_
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ← ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left,
    X.Rph_spec, X.Rph_spec]
  congr 1
  funext g
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply,
    reT_comm]

theorem HH_eq_VT (t : Tup2 D) :
    Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.HH X.B.I X.B.μf X.B.fund (ReT X {t}) X.Rph = Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowsGoodRed.VT X t := by
  have hPE : (ReT X {t}).comp X.B.Re = ReT X {t} := by
    rw [← reT_univ' X, reT_inter, Set.inter_univ]
  have hEP : X.B.Re.comp (ReT X {t}) = ReT X {t} := by
    rw [← reT_univ' X, reT_inter, Set.univ_inter]
  have hPP : (ReT X {t}).comp (ReT X {t}) = ReT X {t} := by
    rw [reT_inter, Set.inter_self]
  have hR := reT_rph X {t}
  apply le_antisymm
  · rintro v ⟨y, rfl⟩
    refine ⟨⟨ReT X {t} y, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
    · show X.B.Re (X.Rph (ReT X {t} y)) = ReT X {t} (X.Rph y)
      have := congrArg (fun L => L y) hR
      simp only [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply] at this
      rw [← this]
      have := congrArg (fun L => L (X.Rph y)) hEP
      simpa only [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply] using this
    · show ReT X {t} (ReT X {t} (X.Rph y)) - ReT X {t} (X.Rph y) = 0
      have := congrArg (fun L => L (X.Rph y)) hPP
      simp only [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply] at this
      rw [this, sub_self]
  · rintro v ⟨⟨y, hy⟩, hv⟩
    have hv' : ReT X {t} v = v := by
      have : ReT X {t} v - v = 0 := hv
      exact sub_eq_zero.1 this
    refine ⟨y, ?_⟩
    show ReT X {t} (X.Rph y) = v
    have hy' : X.B.Re (X.Rph y) = v := hy
    rw [← hv', ← hy']
    have := congrArg (fun L => L (X.Rph y)) hPE
    simp only [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply] at this
    exact this.symm

theorem dcountEq_holds (hG : Formalism.L2.SG.ConcreteSettingExists.GapHyp D) : Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowsGoodRed.DcountEq X := by
  intro t _ _
  have hint := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf.hint_of_gap X hG
  have hB := Formalism.L2.SG.Closed.RowsBij_holds X.B.I X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.fund_spec X.B.R X.B.R_spec (eT X {t})
    (ReT X {t}) (reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) {t}) X.B.H X.B.σ X.dv X.B.w (fun v => (X.ds v).2.2.1)
    (fun v => (X.ds v).2.2.2.1) (Formalism.L2.SG.EvalTRph.isFdeg_dv X) (fun v => (X.low v).1) hint X.Rph X.Rph_spec
    X.B.Hs X.B.σs X.B.tens X.B.tens_spec
  unfold dimX
  rw [← Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.finrank_HH_eq_Dcount hB.1 hB.2, HH_eq_VT]

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsDcount

theorem finiteDimensional_HHX_of (hHC : Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.RowsFinDim)
    (hF : ∀ v, IsFdeg (Formalism.REAL.muG (X.B.I.ε v)) (X.B.σ v) (fdegOf (Formalism.REAL.muG (X.B.I.ε v)) (X.B.σ v) (X.B.w v)))
    (hint : ∀ v, Integrable (Formalism.L2.SG.ProjRows.phLoc (ε := X.B.I.ε v) (X.B.σ v) (X.B.w v)
      (fdegOf (Formalism.REAL.muG (X.B.I.ε v)) (X.B.σ v) (X.B.w v))) (Formalism.REAL.muG (X.B.I.ε v))) :

theorem f_eq : X.B.I.f = D.R.f := by
  have e1 : Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.E = Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E :=
    (X.ιE.toRatAlgEquiv).toLinearEquiv.finrank_eq
  have e2 : Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.F * Module.finrank X.B.I.F X.B.I.E = Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.E :=
    Module.finrank_mul_finrank ℚ X.B.I.F X.B.I.E
  have e3 : Module.finrank X.B.I.F X.B.I.E = 2 := X.B.I.cm.deg2
  have e4 : Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E = 2 * D.R.f := by
    rw [D.host.finrank_cmField]
    unfold Ranks.f Ranks.n
    ring_nf
  rw [e3] at e2
  unfold AutInst.f
  omega

theorem rd_eq : X.B.I.rd = D.host.rdE := by
  have hd : discr X.B.I.E = discr D.host.E := NumberField.discr_eq_discr_of_algEquiv X.B.I.E X.ιE.toRatAlgEquiv
  have e1 : Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.E = Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E :=
    (X.ιE.toRatAlgEquiv).toLinearEquiv.finrank_eq
  have e2 : Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.F * Module.finrank X.B.I.F X.B.I.E = Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.E :=
    Module.finrank_mul_finrank ℚ X.B.I.F X.B.I.E
  have e3 : Module.finrank X.B.I.F X.B.I.E = 2 := X.B.I.cm.deg2
  have e4 : (Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E : ℝ) = 2 * (X.B.I.f : ℝ) := by
    unfold AutInst.f
    have : Module.finrank ℚ D.host.E = 2 * Module.finrank ℚ X.B.I.F := by rw [← e1, ← e2, e3, mul_comm]
    rw [this]; push_cast; ring
  unfold AutInst.rd Host.rdE
  rw [hd, e4]
