  | empty => intro _ _ _; rw [Finset.sum_empty, Finset.sum_empty, Submodule.map_zero, finrank_bot]
  | insert t F ht ih =>
    intro hQ hW hfin
    have hQ' : ∀ i ∈ F, ∀ j ∈ F, Q i ∘ₗ Q j = if i = j then Q i else 0 := fun i hi j hj =>
      hQ i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi) j (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hj)
    have hW' : ∀ i ∈ F, ∀ x ∈ W, Q i x ∈ W := fun i hi => hW i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi)
    have hfin' : ∀ G ⊆ F, FiniteDimensional ℂ (W.map (∑ i ∈ G, Q i)) := fun G hG =>
      hfin G (hG.trans (Finset.subset_insert _ _))
    rw [Finset.sum_insert ht, Finset.sum_insert ht, ← ih hQ' hW' hfin']
    set QF := ∑ i ∈ F, Q i with hQF
    have htt : ∀ x, Q t (Q t x) = Q t x := fun x => by
      have := congrArg (fun L : V →ₗ[ℂ] V => L x) (hQ t (Finset.mem_insert_self t F) t (Finset.mem_insert_self t F))
      simpa using this
    have htF : ∀ x, Q t (QF x) = 0 := fun x => by
      rw [hQF, LinearMap.sum_apply, map_sum]
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i hi => ?_
      have := congrArg (fun L : V →ₗ[ℂ] V => L x)
        (hQ t (Finset.mem_insert_self t F) i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi))
      simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply] at this
      rw [this, if_neg (fun (h : t = i) => ht (h ▸ hi))]
      rfl
    have hFt : ∀ x, QF (Q t x) = 0 := fun x => by
      rw [hQF, LinearMap.sum_apply]
      refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i hi => ?_
      have := congrArg (fun L : V →ₗ[ℂ] V => L x)
        (hQ i (Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hi) t (Finset.mem_insert_self t F))
      simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply] at this
      rw [this, if_neg (fun (h : i = t) => ht (h ▸ hi))]
      rfl
    have hFF : ∀ x, QF (QF x) = QF x := fun x => by
      rw [hQF]
      simp only [LinearMap.sum_apply, map_sum]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
      · have := congrArg (fun L : V →ₗ[ℂ] V => L x) (hQ' i hi i hi)
        simpa using this
      · intro j hj hji
        have := congrArg (fun L : V →ₗ[ℂ] V => L x) (hQ' j hj i hi)
        simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply] at this
        rw [this, if_neg hji]; rfl
      · intro h; exact absurd hi h
    have hWF : ∀ x ∈ W, QF x ∈ W := fun x hx => by
      rw [hQF, LinearMap.sum_apply]; exact Submodule.sum_mem _ fun i hi => hW' i hi x hx
    have hC : W.map (Q t + QF) = W.map (Q t) ⊔ W.map QF := by
      apply le_antisymm
      · rintro _ ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩
        exact Submodule.add_mem_sup ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩ ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩
      · refine sup_le ?_ ?_
        · rintro _ ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩
          refine ⟨Q t w, hW t (Finset.mem_insert_self t F) w hw, ?_⟩
          simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, htt, hFt, add_zero]
        · rintro _ ⟨w, hw, rfl⟩
          refine ⟨QF w, hWF w hw, ?_⟩
          simp only [LinearMap.add_apply, htF, hFF, zero_add]
    have hdis : W.map (Q t) ⊓ W.map QF = ⊥ := by
      rw [eq_bot_iff]
      rintro x ⟨⟨w, _, rfl⟩, ⟨w', _, hw'⟩⟩
      have h1 : Q t (Q t w) = Q t w := htt w
      have h2 : Q t (Q t w) = 0 := by rw [← hw', htF]
      rw [Submodule.mem_bot, ← h1, h2]
    have hfA : FiniteDimensional ℂ (W.map (Q t)) := by
      have := hfin {t} (Finset.singleton_subset_iff.2 (Finset.mem_insert_self t F))
      rwa [Finset.sum_singleton] at this
    have hfB : FiniteDimensional ℂ (W.map QF) := hfin' F (Finset.Subset.refl _)
    have := Submodule.finrank_sup_add_finrank_inf_eq (W.map (Q t)) (W.map QF)
    rw [hdis, finrank_bot, add_zero] at this
    rw [hC, this]

end LA

section Add

variable (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem reT_comm (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (g : X.B.I.Ginf) :
    (ReT X S).comp (X.B.R (g, 1)) = (X.B.R (g, 1)).comp (ReT X S) := by
  obtain ⟨hmul, -, -⟩ := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRMul.R_facts X
  have hT := reT_spec X (etInt_holds X) S
  refine ContinuousLinearMap.ext fun y => ext_inner_left ℂ fun x => ?_
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, hT.2,
    ← ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, hT.2]
  congr 1
  funext a
  rw [ContinuousLinearMap.adjoint_inner_left, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_apply,
    ← hmul, ← hmul, Prod.mk_mul_mk, Prod.mk_mul_mk, one_mul, mul_one, one_mul, mul_one]

theorem reT_univ' : ReT X Set.univ = X.B.Re :=
  reT_univ X (etUniv_of X (redKer_of X (redKerOne_of X (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 0)
    (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 1)))) (etInt_holds X)

open Classical in

theorem rowsAdd_of (hFD : FiniteDimensional ℂ (X.B.I.eHom X.B.σs X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R X.B.Re)) : RowsAdd X := by
  intro S
  set W := X.B.I.HomS X.B.σs X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R
  let Q : Tup2 D → ((X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) →ₗ[ℂ] (X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund)) :=
    fun t => (ContinuousLinearMap.compL ℂ X.B.Hs _ _ (ReT X {t}) : _ →ₗ[ℂ] _)
  have hQapp : ∀ t A, Q t A = (ReT X {t}).comp A := fun t A => rfl
  have hsumQ : ∀ G : Finset (Tup2 D), (∑ t ∈ G, Q t) =
      (ContinuousLinearMap.compL ℂ X.B.Hs _ _ (∑ t ∈ G, ReT X {t}) : _ →ₗ[ℂ] _) := by
    intro G
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro A
    rw [LinearMap.sum_apply]
    simp only [hQapp]
    show ∑ t ∈ G, (ReT X {t}).comp A = (∑ t ∈ G, ReT X {t}).comp A
    ext y
    simp
  have hQQ : ∀ i ∈ labT (D := D), ∀ j ∈ labT (D := D), Q i ∘ₗ Q j = if i = j then Q i else 0 := by
    intro i _ j _
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro A
    rw [LinearMap.comp_apply, hQapp, hQapp, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, reT_orth]
    split_ifs
    · rfl
    · simp
  have hWQ : ∀ i ∈ labT (D := D), ∀ A ∈ W, Q i A ∈ W := by
    intro i _ A hA g
    rw [hQapp]
    show ((ReT X {i}).comp A).comp (X.B.σs g) = (X.B.R (g, 1)).comp ((ReT X {i}).comp A)
    rw [ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, hA g, ← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, reT_comm,
      ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc]
  have hfin : ∀ G ⊆ labT (D := D), FiniteDimensional ℂ (W.map (∑ t ∈ G, Q t)) := by
    intro G _
    have := hFD
    let Qe : (X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) →ₗ[ℂ] (X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) :=
      (ContinuousLinearMap.compL ℂ X.B.Hs (X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) (X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) X.B.Re :
        (X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund) →ₗ[ℂ] (X.B.Hs →L[ℂ] X.B.I.L2 X.B.μf X.B.fund))
    have hcomp : (∑ t ∈ G, Q t) ∘ₗ Qe = ∑ t ∈ G, Q t := by
      apply LinearMap.ext
      intro A
      rw [LinearMap.comp_apply, LinearMap.sum_apply, LinearMap.sum_apply]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun t _ => ?_
      rw [hQapp, hQapp]
      show (ReT X {t}).comp (X.B.Re.comp A) = (ReT X {t}).comp A
      rw [← ContinuousLinearMap.comp_assoc, ← reT_univ' X, reT_inter, Set.inter_univ]
    have e2 : W.map (∑ t ∈ G, Q t) = (X.B.I.eHom X.B.σs X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.R X.B.Re).map (∑ t ∈ G, Q t) := by
      unfold AutInst.eHom
      rw [← Submodule.map_comp]
      exact congrArg (fun L => W.map L) hcomp.symm
    rw [e2]
    infer_instance
  have hF := finrank_map_sum W Q ((labT (D := D)).filter (· ∈ S))
    (fun i hi j hj => hQQ i (Finset.mem_of_mem_filter i hi) j (Finset.mem_of_mem_filter j hj))
    (fun i hi => hWQ i (Finset.mem_of_mem_filter i hi))
    (fun G hG => hfin G (hG.trans (Finset.filter_subset _ _)))
  unfold dimX AutInst.Dcount AutInst.eHom
  rw [reT_split X S, ← hsumQ, hF, Finset.sum_filter]

end Add

end Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsAdd


[ARO_multiplicities_02] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/RootLatticeThresholds/RowsGoodRed.lean:17-125
Range SHA256 df5cee70aa8daba1d53c12bf6b9f0743f8632bb72f8dd0c4a1a346b5a29566ba

variable {D : D7} (X : Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting.HidaSetting D)

def VT (t : Tup2 D) : Submodule ℂ (L2X X) :=
  Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X ⊓ LinearMap.ker ((ReT X {t} : L2X X →ₗ[ℂ] L2X X) - LinearMap.id)

def labOf (σ : Fin D.what₂.Drows) : Tup2 D := fun k => D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)

def bRow (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X) (σ : Fin D.what₂.Drows) : L2X X :=
  ((ι (D.sEE (D.what₂.onb σ)) : Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X) : L2X X)

def LabTie (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X) : Prop :=
  (∀ σ k v, IsLabel D k (D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ) v)) ∧ (∀ σ, D.ntypes.goodTuple (labOf σ)) ∧
    ∀ σ, bRow X ι σ ∈ VT X (labOf σ)

def ProjT (G : GoodOp X) : Prop :=
  (∀ t : Tup2 D, D.ntypes.goodTuple t → VT X t ≤ goodSub X G) ∧
    ∀ t t' : Tup2 D, t ≠ t' → ∀ φ ∈ VT X t, ∀ φ' ∈ VT X t', ⟪φ, φ'⟫_ℂ = 0

def DcountEq : Prop :=
  ∀ t ∈ labT (D := D), D.ntypes.goodTuple t → dimX X {t} = Module.finrank ℂ (VT X t)

theorem rowsGood_of (G : GoodOp X) (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X) (hT : RowDictTie X G ι)
    (hL : LabTie X ι) (hP : ProjT X G) (hD : DcountEq X) : RowsGood X := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨hl1, hl2, hl3⟩ := hL
  refine ⟨hl1, hl2, fun t ht hg => ?_⟩
  have hT' := hT
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hT
  have hb : ∀ σ, bRow X ι σ = star ((j (D.what₂.onb σ) : EEC X G) : L2X X) := fun σ => by
    have h1 := hj (D.what₂.onb σ)
    simp only at h1
    unfold bRow; rw [h1]; rfl

  have hf : LinearIndependent ℂ (fun σ => ((j (D.what₂.onb σ) : EEC X G) : L2X X)) :=
    (D.what₂.onb.toBasis.linearIndependent.map' j.toLinearEquiv.toLinearMap j.toLinearEquiv.ker).map'
      (EEC X G).subtype (Submodule.ker_subtype _)
  have hli : LinearIndependent ℂ (bRow X ι) := by
    rw [linearIndependent_iff']
    intro s g hs i hi
    have h0 : conjL (muX X) (∑ i ∈ s, (starRingEnd ℂ (g i)) • ((j (D.what₂.onb i) : EEC X G) : L2X X)) = 0 := by
      rw [map_sum]
      rw [← hs]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun σ _ => ?_
      rw [LinearIsometryEquiv.map_smulₛₗ, conjL_apply, hb σ]
      simp
    have h1 : (∑ i ∈ s, (starRingEnd ℂ (g i)) • ((j (D.what₂.onb i) : EEC X G) : L2X X)) = 0 :=
      (conjL (muX X)).map_eq_zero_iff.1 h0
    have := (linearIndependent_iff'.1 hf) s (fun i => starRingEnd ℂ (g i)) h1 i hi
    simpa using this

  have hspan : ∀ φ ∈ goodSub X G, φ ∈ Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (bRow X ι)) := by
    intro φ hφ
    obtain ⟨F, hF⟩ := (range_sEE X hT' ⟨φ, hφ.1⟩).1 hφ
    have e : φ = ((ι (D.sEE F) : Formalism.L2.SG.PoincareInHH.PoincareSetting.HHX X) : L2X X) := by rw [hF]
    rw [e, ← D.what₂.onb.sum_repr F, map_sum, map_sum, Submodule.coe_sum]
    refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun σ _ => ?_
    rw [map_smulₛₗ, map_smul, Submodule.coe_smul]
    exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨σ, rfl⟩)

  let Sf : Finset (Fin D.what₂.Drows) := Finset.univ.filter fun σ => labOf σ = t
  have hEq : VT X t = Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (fun σ : Sf => bRow X ι σ)) := by
    apply le_antisymm
    · intro φ hφ
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun ℂ).1 (hspan φ (hP.1 t hg hφ))
      set φ1 := ∑ σ ∈ Sf, c σ • bRow X ι σ with hφ1
      set φ2 := ∑ σ ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun σ => ¬ labOf σ = t), c σ • bRow X ι σ with hφ2
      have hsplit : φ = φ1 + φ2 := by
        rw [← hc, hφ1, hφ2, Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not]
      have h1V : φ1 ∈ VT X t := Submodule.sum_mem _ fun σ hσ => Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (by
        have := hl3 σ; rwa [(Finset.mem_filter.1 hσ).2] at this)
      have h2V : φ2 ∈ VT X t := by
        have := Submodule.sub_mem _ hφ h1V
        rwa [hsplit, add_sub_cancel_left] at this
      have horth : ⟪φ2, φ2⟫_ℂ = 0 := by
        conv_lhs => rw [hφ2]
        rw [sum_inner]
        refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun σ hσ => ?_
        rw [inner_smul_left, hP.2 (labOf σ) t (Finset.mem_filter.1 hσ).2 _ (hl3 σ) _ h2V, mul_zero]
      have h20 : φ2 = 0 := inner_self_eq_zero.1 horth
      rw [hsplit, h20, add_zero, hφ1]
      exact Submodule.sum_mem _ fun σ hσ => Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨⟨σ, hσ⟩, rfl⟩)
    · rw [Submodule.span_le]
      rintro _ ⟨σ, rfl⟩
      have := hl3 σ
      rwa [(Finset.mem_filter.1 σ.2).2] at this
  have hli' : LinearIndependent ℂ (fun σ : Sf => bRow X ι σ) := hli.comp (Subtype.val : Sf → _) Subtype.val_injective
  rw [hD t ht hg, hEq, finrank_span_eq_card hli', Fintype.card_coe]
  rfl

def EtConv : Prop := ∀ (S S' : Set (Tup2 D)) (φ : L2X X), ReT X S (ReT X S' φ) = ReT X (S ∩ S') φ

def EtEmpty : Prop := ∀ φ : L2X X, ReT X ∅ φ = 0

def EtAdj : Prop := ∀ (S : Set (Tup2 D)) (φ ψ : L2X X), ⟪ReT X S φ, ψ⟫_ℂ = ⟪φ, ReT X S ψ⟫_ℂ

def EtGood (G : GoodOp X) : Prop :=
  ∀ t : Tup2 D, D.ntypes.goodTuple t → ∀ φ : L2X X, G.Rg (ReT X {t} φ) = ReT X {t} φ

theorem mem_VT {t : Tup2 D} {φ : L2X X} (h : φ ∈ VT X t) : ReT X {t} φ = φ := by
  have h2 := (Submodule.mem_inf.mp h).2
  rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at h2
  simpa [sub_eq_zero] using h2

theorem projT_of (G : GoodOp X) (hC : EtConv X) (hE : EtEmpty X) (hA : EtAdj X) (hG : EtGood X G) : ProjT X G := by
  refine ⟨fun t ht φ hφ => ?_, fun t t' htt φ hφ φ' hφ' => ?_⟩
  · refine Submodule.mem_inf.mpr ⟨(Submodule.mem_inf.mp hφ).1, ?_⟩
    have h1 := hG t ht φ
    rw [mem_VT X hφ] at h1


[ARO_multiplicities_03] src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/SiegelLatticeTheory/FlagStabilizerTensors/AllRowsOf.lean:760-890
Range SHA256 20e45ba3ac937bd061dca831cf9553198aee4a3e53153931a4e13cb4953b502e

open Classical in

theorem exists_lab {α : Type} [DecidableEq α] (T : Finset α) (m : α → ℕ) (good : α → Prop) [DecidablePred good]
    (Dg N : ℕ) (G : Fin Dg → α) (hGT : ∀ σ, G σ ∈ T) (hGg : ∀ σ, good (G σ))
    (hcnt : ∀ t ∈ T, good t → (Finset.univ.filter fun σ => G σ = t).card = m t)
    (hN : N = ∑ t ∈ T, m t) :
    ∃ (lab : Fin N → α) (inc : Fin Dg → Fin N), (∀ τ, lab τ ∈ T) ∧
      (∀ S : Set α, Nat.card {τ // lab τ ∈ S} = ∑ t ∈ T, if t ∈ S then m t else 0) ∧
      Function.Injective inc ∧ (∀ σ, lab (inc σ) = G σ) ∧ ∀ τ, good (lab τ) ↔ τ ∈ Set.range inc := by
  classical
  set B := T.filter (fun t => ¬ good t) with hB
  let R := Fin Dg ⊕ (Σ t : B, Fin (m t))
  let labR : R → α := Sum.elim G (fun x => (x.1 : α))
  have hgood : Dg = ∑ t ∈ T.filter good, m t := by
    rw [← Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun t ht => hcnt t (Finset.mem_filter.1 ht).1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 ht).2)]
    rw [← Finset.card_eq_sum_card_fiberwise (fun σ _ => Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨hGT σ, hGg σ⟩)]
    simp
  have hcardR : Fintype.card R = N := by
    simp only [R, Fintype.card_sum, Fintype.card_fin, Fintype.card_sigma]
    rw [hN, ← Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not T good, ← hgood]
    congr 1
    exact Finset.sum_coe_sort B m
  let e : Fin N ≃ R := (Fintype.equivFinOfCardEq hcardR).symm
  refine ⟨fun τ => labR (e τ), fun σ => e.symm (Sum.inl σ), ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro τ
    rcases h : e τ with σ | x
    · simp only [labR, h, Sum.elim_inl]; exact hGT σ
    · simp only [labR, h, Sum.elim_inr]; exact (Finset.mem_filter.1 x.1.2).1
  · intro S
    rw [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card, Fintype.card_subtype]
    have hb : (Finset.univ.filter fun τ : Fin N => labR (e τ) ∈ S).card =
        (Finset.univ.filter fun r : R => labR r ∈ S).card := by
      refine Finset.card_bij (fun τ _ => e τ) (fun τ hτ => by simpa using hτ) (fun a _ b _ h => e.injective h)
        (fun r hr => ⟨e.symm r, by simpa using hr, by simp⟩)
    rw [hb, Finset.card_filter, Fintype.sum_sum_type, Fintype.sum_sigma]
    rw [← Finset.sum_filter_add_sum_filter_not T good]
    congr 1
    · rw [← Finset.sum_fiberwise_of_maps_to (g := G) (t := T.filter good)
        (fun σ _ => Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨hGT σ, hGg σ⟩)]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun t ht => ?_
      have hc := hcnt t (Finset.mem_filter.1 ht).1 (Finset.mem_filter.1 ht).2
      by_cases hts : t ∈ S
      · rw [if_pos hts, ← hc, Finset.card_eq_sum_ones]
        refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun σ hσ => ?_
        have hGσ : G σ = t := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hσ).2
        simp [labR, hGσ, hts]
      · rw [if_neg hts]
        refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun σ hσ => ?_
        have hGσ : G σ = t := (Finset.mem_filter.1 hσ).2
        simp [labR, hGσ, hts]
    · rw [← hB, ← Finset.sum_coe_sort B]
      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
      by_cases hx : (x : α) ∈ S
      · rw [if_pos hx, Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun y _ => if_pos (show labR (Sum.inr ⟨x, y⟩) ∈ S from hx))]
        simp
      · rw [if_neg hx]
        exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun y _ => if_neg (show labR (Sum.inr ⟨x, y⟩) ∉ S from hx)
  · exact e.symm.injective.comp Sum.inl_injective
  · intro σ; simp [labR]
  · intro τ
    rcases h : e τ with σ | x
    · simp only [labR, h, Sum.elim_inl]
      refine ⟨fun _ => ⟨σ, show e.symm (Sum.inl σ) = τ by rw [← h, Equiv.symm_apply_apply]⟩, fun _ => hGg σ⟩
    · simp only [labR, h, Sum.elim_inr]
      refine ⟨fun hg => absurd hg (Finset.mem_filter.1 x.1.2).2, fun ⟨σ, hσ⟩ => ?_⟩
      have : e τ = Sum.inl σ := by rw [← hσ, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
      rw [h] at this
      exact absurd this (by simp)

end Count

section RowsLabPf

open Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

open Classical in

def labT : Finset (Tup2 D) :=
  haveI : ∀ k, NeZero (D.host.g k) := fun k => ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  (Finset.univ.image (tupOf (D := D))).filter (fun t => ∀ k v, IsLabel D k (t k v))

open Classical in

def RowsAdd : Prop := ∀ S : Set (Tup2 D), dimX X S = ∑ t ∈ labT, if t ∈ S then dimX X {t} else 0

open Classical in

def RowsGood : Prop :=
  (∀ σ k v, IsLabel D k (D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ) v)) ∧ (∀ σ, D.ntypes.goodTuple (fun k => D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ))) ∧
    ∀ t ∈ (labT (D := D)), D.ntypes.goodTuple t →
      (Finset.univ.filter fun σ => (fun k => D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) = t).card = dimX X {t}

theorem rowsTot_of (hA : RowsAdd X) (hU : EtUniv X) (hT : AutTie D X.B X.pl) :
    D.what₂.Dall = ∑ t ∈ labT (D := D), dimX X {t} := by
  classical
  have h1 : dimX X Set.univ = X.B.D := by
    unfold dimX RowsAut.D
    rw [reT_univ X hU (etInt_holds X)]
  rw [← hT.D_eq, ← h1, hA Set.univ]
  simp

theorem rowsLab_of (hA : RowsAdd X) (hG : RowsGood X) (hU : EtUniv X) (hT : AutTie D X.B X.pl) : RowsLab X := by
  classical
  have : ∀ k, Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := fun k => ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩
  have : ∀ k, NeZero (D.host.g k) := fun k => ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  obtain ⟨hGl, hGg, hGc⟩ := hG
  have hGT : ∀ σ, (fun k => D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) ∈ labT (D := D) := by
    intro σ
    refine Finset.mem_filter.2 ⟨Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨fun kv i => (D.nd.lab kv.1 (D.what₂.ft σ) kv.2).coeff i,
      Finset.mem_univ _, ?_⟩, fun k v => hGl σ k v⟩
    funext k v
    exact monicOf_coeff (hGl σ k v).1 (hGl σ k v).2.1
  obtain ⟨lab, inc, hlabT, hcount, hinj, hinc, hgood⟩ :=
    exists_lab (labT (D := D)) (fun t => dimX X {t}) (fun t => D.ntypes.goodTuple t)
      D.what₂.Drows D.what₂.Dall (fun σ => fun k => D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) hGT hGg hGc (rowsTot_of X hA hU hT)
  refine ⟨fun k τ => lab τ k, inc, fun k τ v => (Finset.mem_filter.1 (hlabT τ)).2 k v, fun S => ?_, hinj,
    fun k σ => congrFun (hinc σ) k, fun τ => hgood τ⟩
  rw [hA S]
  exact (hcount S).symm

end RowsLabPf

structure AllRows (D : D7) : Type 1 where
  B : Formalism.MainNotions.S.RowsAut
  n_eq : B.I.n = D.R.r
  f_eq : B.I.f = D.R.f
  labAll : ∀ k, Fin D.what₂.Dall → D.nd.PlG k → (ZMod (D.host.g k))[X]
  labAll_label : ∀ k σ v, IsLabel D k (labAll k σ v)
  inc : Fin D.what₂.Drows → Fin D.what₂.Dall
  eVec : ∀ k, D.nd.PlG k → MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g k)))
  eVec_idem : ∀ k v, eVec k v * eVec k v = eVec k v
  eT : Set (Tup2 D) → B.I.Gf → ℂ
  ReT : Set (Tup2 D) → (B.I.L2 B.μf B.fund →L[ℂ] B.I.L2 B.μf B.fund)
  ReT_spec : ∀ S, B.I.IsRConvF B.μf B.fund B.R (eT S) (ReT S)
  eT_univ : eT Set.univ = B.e
  ReT_univ : ReT Set.univ = B.Re
  eT_one : ∀ (k : Fin 2) (S : Finset (Tup D k)),
    (eT (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k))) 1).re =
      (B.e 1).re * ∑ t ∈ S, ∏ v, wtOf D k (eVec k v) (t v)

variable {D}

def AllRows.dimT (A : AllRows D) (S : Set (Tup2 D)) : ℕ :=
  A.B.I.Dcount A.B.σs A.B.μf A.B.fund A.B.R (A.ReT S)

def DimEqTotalCount (A : AllRows D) : Prop := D.what₂.Dall = A.B.D

def DimEqGoodCount (A : AllRows D) : Prop := D.what₂.Drows = A.dimT {t | D.ntypes.goodTuple t}

def LabelCountGoodInjective (A : AllRows D) : Prop :=
  (∀ S : Set (Tup2 D), A.dimT S = Nat.card {τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall // (fun k => A.labAll k τ) ∈ S}) ∧
    Function.Injective A.inc ∧ (∀ k σ, A.labAll k (A.inc σ) = D.nd.lab k (D.what₂.ft σ)) ∧
    ∀ τ : Fin D.what₂.Dall, D.ntypes.goodTuple (fun k => A.labAll k τ) ↔ τ ∈ Set.range A.inc

variable (D)

def TieAdmG : Prop := ∀ k, D.ntypes.admissible (D.host.g k)

def stdChar (D : D7) (k : Fin 2) : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ :=
  haveI : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  ZMod.stdAddChar

theorem stdChar_ne (D : D7) (k : Fin 2) : stdChar D k ≠ 1 := by
  intro hk
  haveI : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩
  haveI : Fact (1 < D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).one_lt⟩
  change (ZMod.stdAddChar : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) = 1 at hk
  have h1 : (ZMod.stdAddChar : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) 1 = (ZMod.stdAddChar : AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) 0 := by
    rw [hk, AddChar.one_apply, AddChar.map_zero_eq_one]
  exact one_ne_zero (ZMod.injective_stdAddChar h1)

theorem countAllRows_of {D : D7} (A : AllRows D) {c c₃ : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (hcc : c ≤ c₃) (hN : 2 ≤ D.R.r)
    (hf : 1 ≤ D.R.f) (hC : Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.CountCylAt D A.toAt c₃) (hpos : 0 < DstarE A A.B.e) :
    CountAllRows A (epsC c D.R.r D.R.f) := by
  have hL : 0 < Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ) := Real.log_pos (by exact_mod_cast hN)
  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.r := by exact_mod_cast (lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) hN)
  have hf0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.f := by exact_mod_cast (lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) hf)
  have hP : 0 < (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r := by positivity
  refine ⟨(Real.exp_pos _).le, ?_, hpos, fun k S => ?_⟩
  · unfold epsC
    have h0 : 0 < c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r := by
      have := mul_pos hc hP
      linarith [this, show c * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r) = c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r by ring]
    have h1 := Real.exp_lt_exp.mpr (show -(c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r) < 0 by linarith)
    rwa [Real.exp_zero] at h1
  · have h := hC k S
    change |((A.dimT (cyl D k S) : ℕ) : ℝ) - DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S))| ≤
      Real.exp (-c₃ * (A.B.I.n : ℝ) * (A.B.I.f : ℝ) * Real.log (A.B.I.n : ℝ)) * DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S)) at h
    rw [A.n_eq, A.f_eq] at h
    have hDs : 0 ≤ DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S)) :=
      (mul_nonneg_iff_of_pos_left (Real.exp_pos _)).mp (le_trans (abs_nonneg _) h)
    have hexp : Real.exp (-c₃ * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ)) ≤ epsC c D.R.r D.R.f := by
      unfold epsC
      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
      have e1 : -c₃ * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ) = -(c₃ * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r)) := by ring
      have e2 : -(c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ)) = -(c * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r)) := by ring
      rw [e1, e2]
      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcc hP.le
      linarith
    exact le_trans h (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hexp hDs)

theorem allRows_at_record : Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.RowsFinDim → ∀ (D : D7) (large₂ : SizeData₂ D) (X : HidaSetting D) (G : GoodOp X)
    (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X) (n₀ : ℕ) (Alev C₃ c₃ cb c : ℝ), 0 < c → c ≤ c₃ → Formalism.L2.SG.ConcreteSettingExists.GapHyp D →
    AutTie D X.B X.pl → RowDictTie X G ι → GlobalTheory.LabPin.LabTie X ι → (∀ P, CountE.CountIdempotentsClass P D n₀ Alev C₃ c₃ cb) →
    AdmissibleBoundsFromSource.CountTheoremHypotheses D large₂ n₀ Alev C₃ cb → 4 * D.R.r < D.host.g 1 →
    ∃ A : AllRows D, LabelCountGoodInjective A ∧ AllJoinA A ∧ CountAllRows A (epsC c D.R.r D.R.f) := by
  intro hRF D large₂ X G ι n₀ Alev C₃ c₃ cb c hc hcc hG hAut hT hLab hCnt hp hg1
  have hL : RowsLab X := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsLab.rowsLab_holds X hRF hG G ι hT hLab hAut
  have hψ : ∀ k, stdChar D k ≠ 1 := stdChar_ne D
  have hB : CountBase X n₀ Alev C₃ cb := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.countBase_of X hAut hp hg1
  refine ⟨Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.allRowsOfL X (stdChar D) hψ hL, ?_, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · exact thirdTie_allRows (hI := etInt_holds X)
  · refine ⟨⟨X.pl, hAut⟩, fun v => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.hc_X X hAut v, fun _ => ?_⟩
    change Formalism.MainNotions.S.SplitsCompl X.B.I.E X.B.g₀
    rw [X.g0_eq]
    exact Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.splits_X X 0
  · exact ⟨stdChar D, hψ, fun _ _ => rfl⟩
  · have hN : 2 ≤ D.R.r := le_trans (by norm_num) hG.1
    have hf : 1 ≤ D.R.f := by exact_mod_cast Formalism.MainNotions.S.one_le_f (D := D)
    have hTC := tieCountAt_allRowsOf (X := X) (ψ₀ := stdChar D) (hJ := ejIdem_holds D (stdChar D))
      (hU := etUniv_of X (redKer_of X (redKerOne_of X (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 0)
        (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 1))))
      (hO := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etOne_holds X (stdChar D) hψ) (hI := etInt_holds X) (hL := hL) hB
      (fun _ _ => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etAlg_holds X _)
      (fun k S => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etUnip_holds X k S)
    have hC : Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.CountCylAt D (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.allRowsOfL X (stdChar D) hψ hL).toAt c₃ :=
      Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.countCylAt_of D _ (hCnt Formalism.MainNotions.S.CountE.PCount) hTC
        (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsPCount.pCountTie_holds X (stdChar D) hψ hL)
    obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, hpos⟩ := hB
    exact countAllRows_of _ hc hcc hN hf hC hpos

end GlobalTheory.AllRowsAtRecord


[ARO_partner_count_01] src/Formalism/SharpRestriction/CuspSpanNormBounds/BridgeCountE.lean:289-330
Range SHA256 e5709a2c2cbf5dcbd8edc972f0132f699d1107ab5f8fce11298d538b0beb9779
def IsCharIdemFin {G : Type} [Group G] (f : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G) : Prop :=
  ∃ (U : Subgroup G) (χ : G → ℂ), (∀ a ∈ U, ∀ b ∈ U, χ (a * b) = χ a * χ b) ∧ (∀ a ∈ U, ‖χ a‖ = 1) ∧
    ∀ a, f.coeff a = if a ∈ U then (χ a)⁻¹ / (Nat.card U : ℂ) else 0

namespace AutInst

variable (I : AutInst)

structure RedDatum (g₁ g₂ : ℕ) : Type 1 where
  G₁ : Type
  G₂ : Type
  [grp₁ : Group G₁]
  [grp₂ : Group G₂]
  [fin₁ : Fintype G₁]
  [fin₂ : Fintype G₂]
  red : I.Gf → G₁ × G₂
  red_mul : ∀ x ∈ I.Kset, ∀ y ∈ I.Kset, red (x * y) = red x * red y
  red_surj : ∀ a : G₁ × G₂, ∃ x ∈ I.Kset, red x = a
  red_fib₁ : ∀ x ∈ I.Kset, ∀ y ∈ I.Kset, ((red x).1 = (red y).1 ↔ x⁻¹ * y ∈ I.Kg g₁)
  red_fib₂ : ∀ x ∈ I.Kset, ∀ y ∈ I.Kset, ((red x).2 = (red y).2 ↔ x⁻¹ * y ∈ I.Kg g₂)

attribute [instance] RedDatum.grp₁ RedDatum.grp₂ RedDatum.fin₁ RedDatum.fin₂

open Classical in

def IsCountIdem (μf : Measure I.Gf) (g₀ : ℕ) (e : I.Gf → ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (a b p : ℕ) (R : I.RedDatum (g₀ ^ a) (p ^ b)) (f₀ : MonoidAlgebra ℂ R.G₁) (z ε₂ : MonoidAlgebra ℂ R.G₂),
    p.Prime ∧ p ≠ g₀ ∧ IsCharIdemFin f₀ ∧ (∀ x ∈ I.Kset, f₀.coeff (R.red x).1 ≠ 0 → I.UnipMod g₀ x) ∧
    IsCharIdemFin ε₂ ∧ z * z = z ∧ (∀ y : MonoidAlgebra ℂ R.G₂, z * y = y * z) ∧
    ∀ x, e x = if x ∈ I.Kset then
      f₀.coeff (R.red x).1 * (z * ε₂).coeff (R.red x).2 / ((μf (I.Kg (g₀ ^ a) ∩ I.Kg (p ^ b))).toReal : ℂ) else 0

end AutInst

namespace CountE

theorem gj_mem_wB (j : Fin 2) (v : D.nd.PlG j) :
    ((D.host.g j : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E) ∈ (wB X (D.naut.plGw j v)).asIdeal := by

theorem ker_part (j : Fin 2) (z : KSub X.B.I) :
    (∀ v : D.nd.PlG j, rhoX X z ⟨j, v⟩ = 1) ↔ (z : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g j) := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    refine Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X j _ z.2 fun v => ?_
    have := goodAt_rhoX X z ⟨j, v⟩
    rw [h v] at this
    simpa using this
  · intro hz v
    apply Units.ext
    rw [rhoX_apply, rhoB_val, Units.val_one]
    refine matB_eq (redBX X ⟨j, v⟩) z.2 (redMatB_one_of (redBX X ⟨j, v⟩) fun i => ?_)
    obtain ⟨w, hw, hxw⟩ := hz.2 _ (DictLat.iv_mem X.B.I ((redBX X ⟨j, v⟩).hb.1 i))
    have e2 : X.B.I.fmat z *ᵥ iv X.B.I ((redBX X ⟨j, v⟩).b i) - iv X.B.I ((redBX X ⟨j, v⟩).b i) =
        (X.B.I.fmat z - 1) *ᵥ iv X.B.I ((redBX X ⟨j, v⟩).b i) := by rw [Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec]
    rw [e2, hxw]
    have hg : ((D.host.g j : ℕ) : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) =
        Formalism.UG.inclE (F := X.B.I.F) (Af X.B.I.F) ((((D.host.g j : ℕ) : 𝓞 X.B.I.E)) : X.B.I.E) := by simp
    rw [hg]
    exact smul_mem X.B.I _ (gj_mem_wB X j v) hw

theorem part_iff_j (k : Fin 2) (x y : KSub X.B.I) :
    proj (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => ¬ kv.1 = k) (rhoX X x) =
      proj (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => ¬ kv.1 = k) (rhoX X y) ↔
      ((x⁻¹ * y : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g (1 - k)) := by
  have hfin : ∀ a : Fin 2, ¬ a = k ↔ a = 1 - k := by
    intro a
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases k <;> decide
  rw [← ker_part X (1 - k) (x⁻¹ * y)]
  constructor
  · intro h v
    have hv := congrFun h ⟨1 - k, v⟩
    rw [proj_apply, proj_apply, if_pos ((hfin _).2 rfl), if_pos ((hfin _).2 rfl)] at hv
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.inv_apply, hv, inv_mul_cancel]
  · intro h
    funext ⟨k', v⟩
    simp only [proj_apply]
    by_cases hk : k' = k
    · rw [if_neg (not_not.2 hk), if_neg (not_not.2 hk)]
    · obtain rfl : k' = 1 - k := (hfin k').1 hk
      rw [if_pos hk, if_pos hk]
      have hv := h v
      rw [map_mul, map_inv, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.inv_apply, inv_mul_eq_one] at hv
      exact hv

theorem part_iff_k (k : Fin 2) (x y : KSub X.B.I) :
    proj (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => kv.1 = k) (rhoX X x) =
      proj (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => kv.1 = k) (rhoX X y) ↔
      ((x⁻¹ * y : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g k) := by
  rw [← ker_part X k (x⁻¹ * y)]
  constructor
  · intro h v
    have hv := congrFun h ⟨k, v⟩
    rw [proj_apply, proj_apply, if_pos rfl, if_pos rfl] at hv
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.inv_apply, hv, inv_mul_cancel]
  · intro h
    funext ⟨k', v⟩
    simp only [proj_apply]
    by_cases hk : k' = k
    · subst hk
      rw [if_pos rfl, if_pos rfl]
      have hv := h v
      rw [map_mul, map_inv, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.inv_apply, inv_mul_eq_one] at hv
      exact hv
    · rw [if_neg hk, if_neg hk]

def redD (k : Fin 2) : X.B.I.RedDatum (D.host.g (1 - k) ^ 1) (D.host.g k ^ 1) where
  G₁ := ↥(Sub (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => ¬ kv.1 = k))
  G₂ := ↥(Sub (fun kv : (Σ k', D.nd.PlG k') => kv.1 = k))
  fin₁ := Fintype.ofFinite _
  fin₂ := Fintype.ofFinite _
  red := fun x => if hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset then
    (⟨proj _ (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩, ⟨proj _ (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩) else 1
  red_mul := fun x hx y hy => by
    have hxy : x * y ∈ X.B.I.Kset := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence.K_mul X.B.I hx hy
    simp only [dif_pos hx, dif_pos hy, dif_pos hxy]
    have e : (⟨x * y, hxy⟩ : KSub X.B.I) = ⟨x, hx⟩ * ⟨y, hy⟩ := rfl
    rw [e]
    ext <;> simp [map_mul]
  red_surj := fun ab => by
    obtain ⟨a, b⟩ := ab
    have hS := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsAdd.hS_X X
    have hm : ((sec hS ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.B.I.Kset := (sec hS _).2
    refine ⟨(sec hS ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) : X.B.I.Gf), hm, ?_⟩
    rw [dif_pos hm]
    have e : (⟨(sec hS ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) : X.B.I.Gf), (sec hS _).2⟩ : KSub X.B.I) =
        sec hS ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) := rfl
    rw [e, rho_sec]
    refine Prod.ext (Subtype.ext ?_) (Subtype.ext ?_)
    · show proj _ ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) = (a : G0g D.nd)
      rw [map_mul, proj_of_mem _ a.2,
        proj_eq_one_of _ b.2 (fun (i : Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') (hi : ¬ i.1 = k) h => hi h), mul_one]
    · show proj _ ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) = (b : G0g D.nd)
      rw [map_mul, proj_of_mem _ b.2,
        proj_eq_one_of _ a.2 (fun (i : Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') (hi : i.1 = k) h => h hi), one_mul]
  red_fib₁ := fun x hx y hy => by
    simp only [dif_pos hx, dif_pos hy, pow_one]

def Fv (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :=
  MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv) 1 * eJv kv (ψ kv) * MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv)⁻¹ 1

theorem Fv_idem (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : Fv P ψ kv * Fv P ψ kv = Fv P ψ kv := by
  have h1 : MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv)⁻¹ (1 : ℂ) * MonoidAlgebra.single (P kv) 1 = 1 := by
    rw [MonoidAlgebra.single_mul_single, inv_mul_cancel, mul_one, MonoidAlgebra.one_def]
  unfold Fv
  have e : ∀ a b c : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))),
      a * b * c * (a * b * c) = a * (b * (c * a) * b) * c := fun a b c => by simp only [mul_assoc]
  rw [e, h1, mul_one, eJ_idem]

theorem Fv_coeff (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) (g : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
    (Fv P ψ kv).coeff g = if PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (((P kv)⁻¹ * g * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
        Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) then
      (ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (((P kv)⁻¹ * g * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) :
        Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)))))⁻¹ / (cJ D (D.host.g kv.1) : ℂ) else 0 := by
  haveI : Fact (D.host.g kv.1).Prime := ⟨D.host.g_prime kv.1⟩
  unfold Fv
  rw [MonoidAlgebra.coeff_mul_single_apply, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_single_mul_apply, one_mul, mul_one, inv_inv,
    ← mul_assoc, eJ_coeff, card_PJ]

variable (p : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) → Prop)

def Ep : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (G0g D.nd) := pA (fun kv => if p kv then Fv P ψ kv else 1)

theorem one_coeff_ne {G : Type} [Group G] {g : G} (h : (1 : MonoidAlgebra ℂ G).coeff g ≠ 0) : g = 1 := by
  exact Finset.mem_one.1 (MonoidAlgebra.support_coeff_one_subset (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.2 h))

theorem Ep_supp (γ : G0g D.nd) (h : (Ep P ψ p).coeff γ ≠ 0) : γ ∈ Sub p := by
  intro i hi
  rw [Ep, pA_coeff] at h
  have hi' := Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 h i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  rw [if_neg hi] at hi'
  exact one_coeff_ne hi'

theorem Ep_idem : Ep P ψ p * Ep P ψ p = Ep P ψ p := by
  rw [Ep, pA_mul]
  congr 1
  funext kv
  split_ifs
  · exact Fv_idem P ψ kv
  · exact one_mul 1

end Char

abbrev cj (γ : G0g D.nd) (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)) :=
  (((P kv)⁻¹ * γ kv * P kv : GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1))) : Matrix (Fin D.R.r) (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)))

theorem cj_mul (a b : G0g D.nd) (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : cj P (a * b) kv = cj P a kv * cj P b kv := by
  simp only [cj, Pi.mul_apply]
  rw [← Units.val_mul]
  congr 1
  group

theorem cj_one (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) : cj P 1 kv = 1 := by
  simp [cj]

def Uj : Subgroup (Sub p : Subgroup (G0g D.nd)) where
  carrier := {a | ∀ kv, p kv → PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P (a : G0g D.nd) kv)}
  one_mem' := fun kv _ => by
    show PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P 1 kv)
    rw [cj_one]; exact jPred_one _ _
  mul_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb kv hk => by
    show PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P ((a : G0g D.nd) * (b : G0g D.nd)) kv)
    rw [cj_mul]; exact Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.J_mul (ha kv hk) (hb kv hk)
  inv_mem' := fun {a} ha kv hk => by
    show PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P ((a : G0g D.nd)⁻¹) kv)
    have h := PJ_inv ((P kv)⁻¹ * (a : G0g D.nd) kv * P kv) (ha kv hk)
    unfold cj
    convert h using 2
    simp only [Pi.inv_apply]
    group

def chij (a : G0g D.nd) : ℂ := ∏ kv, if p kv then ψ kv (phiJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P a kv)) else 1

theorem chij_mul (a b : G0g D.nd) (ha : ∀ kv, p kv → PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P a kv))
    (hb : ∀ kv, p kv → PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P b kv)) :
    chij P ψ p (a * b) = chij P ψ p a * chij P ψ p b := by
  unfold chij
  rw [← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
  split_ifs with h
  · rw [cj_mul, Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.phiJ_mul (ha kv h).1 (hb kv h).1 (hb kv h).2, AddChar.map_add_eq_mul]
  · rw [mul_one]

theorem chij_norm (a : G0g D.nd) : ‖chij P ψ p a‖ = 1 := by
  unfold chij
  rw [norm_prod]
  refine Finset.prod_eq_one fun kv _ => ?_
  split_ifs
  · exact AddChar.norm_apply _ _
  · exact norm_one

def cjc : ℂ := ∏ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), if p kv then ((cJ D (D.host.g kv.1) : ℂ))⁻¹ else 1

theorem cjc_ne : cjc (D := D) p ≠ 0 := by
  unfold cjc
  refine Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.2 fun kv _ => ?_
  split_ifs
  · exact inv_ne_zero (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (cJ_pos D kv.1).ne')
  · exact one_ne_zero

theorem res_Ep_coeff (a : Sub p) :
    (res (Sub p) (Ep P ψ p)).coeff a = if a ∈ Uj P p then (chij P ψ p a)⁻¹ * cjc p else 0 := by
  rw [res_coeff, Ep, pA_coeff]
  split_ifs with hU
  · have hU' : ∀ kv, p kv → PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P (a : G0g D.nd) kv) := hU
    rw [chij, cjc, ← Finset.prod_inv_distrib, ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv _ => ?_
    by_cases hp : p kv
    · rw [if_pos hp, if_pos hp, if_pos hp, Fv_coeff, if_pos (hU' kv hp), div_eq_mul_inv]
    · rw [if_neg hp, if_neg hp, if_neg hp, a.2 kv hp, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_one_one, inv_one, one_mul]
  · have hU' : ¬ ∀ kv, p kv → PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P (a : G0g D.nd) kv) := hU
    push_neg at hU'
    obtain ⟨kv, hp, hJ⟩ := hU'
    refine Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ kv) ?_
    rw [if_pos hp, Fv_coeff, if_neg hJ]

    · rw [if_neg hx, if_neg hx, zero_mul]

    rw [hcoef, if_pos (Uj P p).one_mem, Subgroup.coe_one, chij_one, inv_one, one_mul]
  have hc : cjc p * (Nat.card (Uj P p) : ℂ) = 1 := by

def TS (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)) : Finset (Tup D k) :=
  (Finset.univ.filter (fun a : CV D => tupOf a k ∈ S)).image (fun a => tupOf a k)

theorem ES_TS (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)) : ES (cyl D k S) = ES (cyl D k (TS k S : Set (Tup D k))) := by
  have key : ∀ a, tupOf a ∈ cyl D k S ↔ tupOf a ∈ cyl D k (TS k S : Set (Tup D k)) := by
    intro a
    simp only [cyl, Set.mem_setOf_eq, TS, Finset.coe_image, Finset.coe_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and,
      Set.mem_image, Set.mem_setOf_eq]
    exact ⟨fun h => ⟨a, h, rfl⟩, fun ⟨b, hb, e⟩ => e ▸ hb⟩
  simp only [ES, key]

theorem Gt_supp (k : Fin 2) (t : Tup D k) (γ : G0g D.nd) (h : (pA (Gt k t)).coeff γ ≠ 0) :
    γ ∈ Sub (fun kv : (Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') => kv.1 = k) := by
  intro i hi
  rw [pA_coeff] at h
  have hi' := Finset.prod_ne_zero_iff.1 h i (Finset.mem_univ _)
  rw [Gt, if_neg hi] at hi'
  exact one_coeff_ne hi'

theorem ES_supp (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)) (γ : G0g D.nd) (h : (ES (cyl D k S)).coeff γ ≠ 0) :
    γ ∈ Sub (fun kv : (Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') => kv.1 = k) := by
  rw [ES_TS, ES_cyl_pA] at h
  exact sum_supp _ _ (fun t => Gt_supp k t) γ h

  Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsEtOne Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsKpp Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsGA Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsES

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem Kg_dvd {d m : ℕ} (h : d ∣ m) {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kg m) : x ∈ X.B.I.Kg d := by
  obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := h
  refine ⟨hx.1, fun y hy => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨z, hz, e⟩ := hx.2 y hy
  refine ⟨c • z, nsmul_mem hz c, ?_⟩
  rw [e, Nat.cast_mul, mul_smul]
  simp only [Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul]

theorem Kg_eq (k : Fin 2) :
    X.B.I.Kg X.B.g = X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g (1 - k) ^ 1) ∩ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g k ^ 1) := by
  rw [pow_one, pow_one]
  have hg : X.B.g = D.host.g (1 - k) * D.host.g k := by
    rw [X.g_eq]; fin_cases k
    · exact mul_comm _ _
    · rfl
  ext x
  constructor
  · intro hx
    rw [hg] at hx
    exact ⟨Kg_dvd X (dvd_mul_right _ _) hx, Kg_dvd X (dvd_mul_left _ _) hx⟩
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    rw [hg]
    refine Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.Kg_inter X.B.I ?_ h1 h2
    refine (Nat.coprime_primes (D.host.g_prime _) (D.host.g_prime _)).2 ?_
    fin_cases k
    · exact D.host.g_ne.symm
    · exact D.host.g_ne

theorem isCountIdem_cyl (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)) :
    X.B.I.IsCountIdem X.B.μf (D.host.g (1 - k)) (eT X (cyl D k S)) := by
  have hS := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsAdd.hS_X X
  obtain ⟨P, ψ, -, hK⟩ := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.kppShape_holds X
  have hmem := fun x => (hK x).1
  have hchi := fun x => (hK x).2
  let pj : (Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') → Prop := fun kv => ¬ kv.1 = k
  let pk : (Σ k' : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k') → Prop := fun kv => kv.1 = k
  have hsplit : Ehat hS = Ep P ψ pj * Ep P ψ pk := by
    rw [Ehat_eq hS P ψ hmem hchi]
    unfold Ep
    rw [pA_mul]
    congr 1
    funext kv
    by_cases hk : kv.1 = k
    · simp only [pj, pk, hk, not_true_eq_false, if_false, if_true, one_mul]; rfl
    · simp only [pj, pk, hk, not_false_eq_true, if_true, if_false, mul_one]; rfl
  have hz2 : res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) * res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) = res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) := by
    apply MonoidAlgebra.ext; ext a
    rw [← res_mul _ _ (ES_supp k S) a, ES_idem, res_coeff]
  have hzc : ∀ y, res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) * y = y * res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) :=
    central_of_class _ fun g h => by
      rw [res_coeff, res_coeff]
      simp only [Subgroup.coe_mul, Subgroup.coe_inv]
      exact ES_conj _ _ _
  refine ⟨1, 1, D.host.g k, redD X k, res (Sub pj) (Ep P ψ pj), res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)),
    res (Sub pk) (Ep P ψ pk), D.host.g_prime k, ?_, charIdem_Ep P ψ pj, ?_, charIdem_Ep P ψ pk, hz2, hzc, ?_⟩
  · fin_cases k
    · exact D.host.g_ne
    · exact D.host.g_ne.symm
  · intro x hx hf
    have hred : (redD X k).red x = (⟨proj pj (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩, ⟨proj pk (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩) := by
      show (if hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset then _ else 1) = _
      exact dif_pos hx
    have hf' : (res (Sub pj) (Ep P ψ pj)).coeff (⟨proj pj (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩ : Sub pj) ≠ 0 := by
      rw [hred] at hf; exact hf
    rw [res_Ep_coeff] at hf'
    split_ifs at hf' with hU
    · set γ := proj pj (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩) with hγ
      have hJ : ∀ kv, JPred D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cjM P γ kv) := by
        intro kv
        by_cases hk : kv.1 = k
        · have h1 : γ kv = 1 := proj_mem pj _ kv (by simp [pj, hk])
          show PJ D.R.k (D.R.m / 2 - 1) (cj P γ kv)
          have h2 : cj P γ kv = 1 := by unfold cj; rw [h1]; simp
          rw [h2]; exact jPred_one _ _
        · exact hU kv hk
      have hy : ((sec hS γ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) ∈ X.K2 := (hmem (sec hS γ)).2 (by rw [rho_sec]; exact hJ)
      have hdiv : D.host.g (1 - k) ∣ X.B.g := by
        rw [X.g_eq]; fin_cases k
        · exact dvd_mul_left _ _
        · exact dvd_mul_right _ _
      have hyU := levelSubgroup_unipotent_mod X hdiv hy
      have hz := (part_iff_j X k (sec hS γ) ⟨x, hx⟩).1 (by rw [rho_sec, hγ, proj_of_mem _ (proj_mem _ _)])
      have := unipMod_mul_Kg X hy.1 hyU hz
      simpa only [Subgroup.coe_mul, Subgroup.coe_inv, mul_inv_cancel_left] using this
    · exact absurd rfl hf'
  · intro x
    by_cases hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset
    · rw [if_pos hx]
      have hred : (redD X k).red x = (⟨proj pj (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩, ⟨proj pk (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩) := by
        show (if hx : x ∈ X.B.I.Kset then _ else 1) = _
        exact dif_pos hx
      rw [hred]
      have h1 := val_hat hS (biInv_eT X (cyl D k S)) ⟨x, hx⟩
      have hV : V X = (X.B.μf (X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g (1 - k) ^ 1) ∩ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g k ^ 1))).toReal := by
        unfold V; rw [Kg_eq X k]
      rw [show eT X (cyl D k S) x = eT X (cyl D k S) ((⟨x, hx⟩ : KSub X.B.I) : X.B.I.Gf) from rfl, h1, hat_eT' hS,
        hsplit, mul_assoc, ← hV]
      rw [mul_coeff_compl' (p := pj) (q := pk) (fun kv => by simp [pj, pk]) _ _ (Ep_supp P ψ pj)
        (mul_supp _ _ (Ep_supp P ψ pk) (ES_supp k S))]
      have a1 : ∀ h : Sub pj, (res (Sub pj) (Ep P ψ pj)).coeff h = (Ep P ψ pj).coeff (h : G0g D.nd) :=
        fun h => res_coeff _ _ _
      have a2 : ∀ h : Sub pk, (res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) * res (Sub pk) (Ep P ψ pk)).coeff h =
          (Ep P ψ pk * ES (cyl D k S)).coeff (h : G0g D.nd) := fun h => by
        rw [hzc (res (Sub pk) (Ep P ψ pk)), ← res_mul (Ep P ψ pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) (Ep_supp P ψ pk)]
      show _ = (res (Sub pj) (Ep P ψ pj)).coeff (⟨proj pj (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩ : Sub pj) *
        (res (Sub pk) (ES (cyl D k S)) * res (Sub pk) (Ep P ψ pk)).coeff
          (⟨proj pk (rhoX X ⟨x, hx⟩), proj_mem _ _⟩ : Sub pk) / (V X : ℂ)
      simp only [a1, a2]
    · rw [if_neg hx]; exact eT_of_not_mem X _ hx

end Main

section Comparison₂

variable {D : Formalism.BasicNotions.D7} (X : Formalism.L2.SG.HidaSetting.HidaSetting D)

theorem pCountTie_holds (ψ₀ : ∀ k : Fin 2, AddChar (ZMod (D.host.g k)) ℂ) (hψ₀ : ∀ k, ψ₀ k ≠ 1)
    (hL : Formalism.L2.SG.AllRowsOf.RowsLab X) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.PCountTie D (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.allRowsOfL X ψ₀ hψ₀ hL).toAt :=
  fun k S => Or.inr (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsPCount.isCountIdem_cyl X k S)

end Comparison₂

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem levelSubgroup_unipotent_mod {g₀ : ℕ} (hg₀ : g₀ ∣ X.B.g) {x : X.B.I.Gf} (hx : x ∈ K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw) :
    X.B.I.UnipMod g₀ x := by
  have hn : 2 * D.R.rS + D.R.k + 1 ≤ X.B.I.n := by rw [X.n_eq, Formalism.MainNotions.S.dimS_eq]
  exact Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.BunEx.unipotence_mod_g X.B.I X.B.g g₀ X.e0 X.f0 X.z0 X.bw X.bwd X.ew
    (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (level_ne_zero X)) hg₀ hn X.vectorsInLattice X.gramIntegral X.formsAtZero X.weightVectorsIntegral X.ew_idem X.ew_orth X.ew_int
    X.bw_w X.bwd_w X.bw_dual X.latticeSpan x hx

theorem e_mem_levelSubgroup {y : X.B.I.Gf} (hy : X.B.e y ≠ 0) : y ∈ K2set X.B.I X.B.g X.e0 X.z0 X.bw := by
  classical
  by_contra h
  apply hy
  rw [X.e_eq y, if_neg h]

theorem powSub_mem {y : X.B.I.Gf} (hyK : y ∈ X.B.I.Kset) : ∀ j : ℕ, ∀ v ∈ X.B.I.Lf,
    ((X.B.I.fmat y - 1) ^ j) *ᵥ v ∈ X.B.I.Lf := by
  intro j
  induction j with
  | zero => intro v hv; simpa using hv
  | succ j ih =>
    intro v hv
    rw [pow_succ', ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec]
    exact K_sub_mv _ hyK (ih v hv)

theorem unipMod_mul_Kg {l : ℕ} {y z : X.B.I.Gf} (hyK : y ∈ X.B.I.Kset) (hy : X.B.I.UnipMod l y)
    (hz : z ∈ X.B.I.Kg l) : X.B.I.UnipMod l (y * z) := by
  have hsplit : ∀ u, (X.B.I.fmat (y * z) - 1) *ᵥ u =
      (X.B.I.fmat y - 1) *ᵥ u + X.B.I.fmat y *ᵥ ((X.B.I.fmat z - 1) *ᵥ u) := by
    intro u
    rw [fmat_mul, Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.sub_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_sub, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec,
      Matrix.one_mulVec]
    abel
  have key : ∀ j : ℕ, ∀ v ∈ X.B.I.Lf, ∃ w ∈ X.B.I.Lf,
      ((X.B.I.fmat (y * z) - 1) ^ j) *ᵥ v = ((X.B.I.fmat y - 1) ^ j) *ᵥ v + (l : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) • w := by
    intro j
    induction j with
    | zero => intro v _; exact ⟨0, zero_mem _, by simp⟩
    | succ j ih =>
      intro v hv
      obtain ⟨w, hw, hj⟩ := ih v hv
      obtain ⟨w', hw', hzw⟩ := hz.2 _ (powSub_mem X hyK j v hv)
      obtain ⟨w'', hw'', hzw''⟩ := hz.2 _ hw
      have hl : (l : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) • (X.B.I.fmat y *ᵥ w'') ∈ X.B.I.Lf := by
        rw [Nat.cast_smul_eq_nsmul]; exact nsmul_mem (K_mv _ hyK hw'') l
      refine ⟨X.B.I.fmat y *ᵥ w' + ((X.B.I.fmat y - 1) *ᵥ w + (l : X.B.I.E ⊗[X.B.I.F] Af X.B.I.F) • (X.B.I.fmat y *ᵥ w'')),
        add_mem (K_mv _ hyK hw') (add_mem (K_sub_mv _ hyK hw) hl), ?_⟩
      have e1 : ((X.B.I.fmat y - 1) ^ (j + 1)) *ᵥ v = (X.B.I.fmat y - 1) *ᵥ (((X.B.I.fmat y - 1) ^ j) *ᵥ v) := by
        rw [pow_succ', Matrix.mulVec_mulVec]
      rw [pow_succ', ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, hj, Matrix.mulVec_add, Matrix.mulVec_smul, hsplit, hsplit, hzw, hzw'',
        Matrix.mulVec_smul, Matrix.mulVec_smul, e1]
      simp only [smul_add]
      abel
  intro v hv
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hj⟩ := key X.B.I.n v hv
  obtain ⟨w₀, hw₀, h₀⟩ := hy v hv
  exact ⟨w₀ + w, add_mem hw₀ hw, by rw [hj, h₀, smul_add]⟩

end UnipPf

section UnipPf2

open Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.RowDictPf

local instance factG'' {D : D7} (k : Fin 2) : Fact (D.host.g k).Prime := ⟨D.host.g_prime k⟩

local instance neZeroG'' {D : D7} (k : Fin 2) : NeZero (D.host.g k) := ⟨(D.host.g_prime k).ne_zero⟩

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem ES_cyl_coeff_zero (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)) (γ : G0g D.nd) (kv0 : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k)
    (hk : kv0.1 ≠ k) (hγ : γ kv0 ≠ 1) : (ES (D := D) (cyl D k (S : Set (Tup D k)))).coeff γ = 0 := by
  classical
  unfold ES
  simp only [Finsupp.coe_equivFunOnFinite_symm]
  rw [finsum_eq_sum_of_fintype]
  let A : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) → Type := fun kv => Fin D.R.r → ZMod (D.host.g kv.1)
  let e := Equiv.piSplitAt kv0 A
  rw [← e.symm.sum_comp, Fintype.sum_prod_type, Finset.sum_comm]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun rest _ => ?_
  have hval : ∀ (c0 : A kv0) (kv : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), kv ≠ kv0 →
      (e.symm (c0, rest)) kv = (e.symm (0, rest)) kv := by
    intro c0 kv hne
    simp [e, Equiv.piSplitAt_symm_apply, hne]
  have hcond : ∀ c0 : A kv0, (tupOf (e.symm (c0, rest)) ∈ cyl D k S) ↔ (tupOf (e.symm (0, rest)) ∈ cyl D k S) := by
    intro c0
    have : (tupOf (e.symm (c0, rest))) k = (tupOf (e.symm (0, rest))) k := by
      funext v
      have hne : (⟨k, v⟩ : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k) ≠ kv0 := fun h => hk (by rw [← h])
      simp only [tupOf]
      rw [hval c0 _ hne]
    simp only [cyl, Set.mem_setOf_eq, this]
  have hsum0 : ∑ c0 : A kv0, (D.ntypes.eSer kv0.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf c0)).coeff (γ kv0) = 0 := by
    have h := congrArg (fun t : MonoidAlgebra ℂ (GL (Fin D.R.r) (ZMod (D.host.g kv0.1))) => t.coeff (γ kv0))
      (sum_eSer' (D := D) kv0.1)
    simp only [MonoidAlgebra.coeff_sum, Finsupp.finsetSum_apply] at h
    rw [MonoidAlgebra.one_def, MonoidAlgebra.coeff_single, Finsupp.single_apply, if_neg (Ne.symm hγ)] at h
    exact h
  by_cases hP : tupOf (e.symm (0, rest)) ∈ cyl D k S
  · rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl fun c0 _ => by
      rw [if_pos ((hcond c0).2 hP), ← Finset.mul_prod_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ kv0)]]
    have hrest : ∀ c0 : A kv0, (∏ kv ∈ Finset.univ.erase kv0,
        (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf ((e.symm (c0, rest)) kv))).coeff (γ kv)) =
        ∏ kv ∈ Finset.univ.erase kv0, (D.ntypes.eSer kv.1 (Formalism.CyclotomicNorms.monicOf ((e.symm (0, rest)) kv))).coeff (γ kv) :=
      fun c0 => Finset.prod_congr rfl fun kv hkv => by rw [hval c0 kv (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hkv)]
    have hkv0 : ∀ c0 : A kv0, (e.symm (c0, rest)) kv0 = c0 := fun c0 => by simp [e, Equiv.piSplitAt_symm_apply]
    simp only [hrest, hkv0]
    rw [← Finset.sum_mul, hsum0, zero_mul]
  · exact Finset.sum_eq_zero fun c0 _ => if_neg (fun h => hP ((hcond c0).1 h))

def RedKerP (k : Fin 2) : Prop :=
  ∀ x ∈ X.B.I.Kset, (∀ v : D.nd.PlG k, RedMat X (D.naut.plGw k v) (D.host.g k) x 1) → x ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g k)

theorem etUnip_of (k : Fin 2) (hR : RedKerP X (1 - k)) (S : Set (Tup D k)) : EtUnip X k S := by
  classical
  intro x hx
  have hex : ∃ y, X.B.e y * egA X (ES (cyl D k S)) (y⁻¹ * x) ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra h
    simp only [not_exists, not_not] at h
    apply hx
    unfold eT AutInst.convF
    rw [show (fun y => X.B.e y * egA X (ES (cyl D k S)) (y⁻¹ * x)) = fun _ => 0 from funext h, integral_zero]
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hex
  have hey : X.B.e y ≠ 0 := left_ne_zero_of_mul hy
  have hz : egA X (ES (cyl D k S)) (y⁻¹ * x) ≠ 0 := right_ne_zero_of_mul hy
  have hyK2 := e_mem_levelSubgroup X hey
  have hdiv : D.host.g (1 - k) ∣ X.B.g := by
    rw [X.g_eq]
    fin_cases k
    · exact dvd_mul_left _ _
    · exact dvd_mul_right _ _
  have hyU := levelSubgroup_unipotent_mod X hdiv hyK2

  unfold egA at hz
  by_cases hzK : y⁻¹ * x ∈ X.B.I.Kset ∧ ∃ γ, GoodAt X (y⁻¹ * x) γ
  swap
  · rw [dif_neg hzK] at hz; exact absurd rfl hz
  rw [dif_pos hzK] at hz
  obtain ⟨hzK1, hzE⟩ := hzK
  have hγ := Classical.choose_spec hzE
  have h1 : ∀ kv : (Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k), kv.1 ≠ k → (Classical.choose hzE) kv = 1 := by
    intro kv hkv
    by_contra hne
    apply hz
    rw [ES_cyl_coeff_zero k S _ kv hkv hne, zero_div]
  have hzKg : y⁻¹ * x ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g (1 - k)) := by
    refine hR _ hzK1 fun v => ?_
    have hk' : (⟨1 - k, v⟩ : Σ k : Fin 2, D.nd.PlG k).1 ≠ k := by
      show 1 - k ≠ k
      fin_cases k <;> decide
    have := hγ ⟨1 - k, v⟩
    rw [h1 _ hk'] at this
    simpa using this
  have := unipMod_mul_Kg X hyK2.1 hyU hzKg
  rwa [mul_inv_cancel_left] at this

end UnipPf2

section RedKerOnePf

open Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.unitaryCongruence

theorem Kg_inter (I : AutInst) {a b : ℕ} (hab : Nat.Coprime a b) {x : I.Gf} (ha : x ∈ I.Kg a) (hb : x ∈ I.Kg b) :
    x ∈ I.Kg (a * b) := by
  refine ⟨ha.1, fun v hv => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨za, hza, ea⟩ := ha.2 v hv
  obtain ⟨zb, hzb, eb⟩ := hb.2 v hv
  have hZ : Nat.gcdA a b * (a : ℤ) + Nat.gcdB a b * (b : ℤ) = 1 := by
    have h := Nat.gcd_eq_gcd_ab a b
    rw [hab, Nat.cast_one] at h
    linarith [h]
  have huw : ((Nat.gcdA a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) * (a : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) +
      ((Nat.gcdB a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) * (b : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) = 1 := by
    have h := congrArg (fun z : ℤ => (z : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F)) hZ
    push_cast at h
    exact h
  set u : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F := ((Nat.gcdA a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F)
  set w : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F := ((Nat.gcdB a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F)
  have hu : ∀ z ∈ I.Lf, u • z ∈ I.Lf := fun z hz => by
    show ((Nat.gcdA a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • z ∈ I.Lf
    rw [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul]; exact zsmul_mem hz _
  have hw : ∀ z ∈ I.Lf, w • z ∈ I.Lf := fun z hz => by
    show ((Nat.gcdB a b : ℤ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • z ∈ I.Lf
    rw [Int.cast_smul_eq_zsmul]; exact zsmul_mem hz _
  refine ⟨u • zb + w • za, add_mem (hu zb hzb) (hw za hza), ?_⟩
  calc (I.fmat x - 1) *ᵥ v = (u * a + w * b) • ((I.fmat x - 1) *ᵥ v) := by rw [huw, one_smul]
    _ = u • ((a : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • ((b : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • zb)) +
        w • ((b : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • ((a : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • za)) := by
      rw [← eb, ← ea, add_smul, mul_smul, mul_smul]
    _ = ((a * b : ℕ) : I.E ⊗[I.F] Af I.F) • (u • zb + w • za) := by
      simp only [smul_add, smul_smul, Nat.cast_mul]
      congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> ring

variable {D : D7} (X : HidaSetting D)

theorem redKerOne_of (h0 : RedKerP X 0) (h1 : RedKerP X 1) : RedKerOne X := by
  intro x hx hg
  have hc : Nat.Coprime (D.host.g 0) (D.host.g 1) :=
    (Nat.coprime_primes (D.host.g_prime 0) (D.host.g_prime 1)).2 D.host.g_ne
  have hk : ∀ k : Fin 2, x ∈ X.B.I.Kg (D.host.g k) := fun k => by
    fin_cases k
    · exact h0 x hx fun v => by simpa using hg ⟨0, v⟩
    · exact h1 x hx fun v => by simpa using hg ⟨1, v⟩
  rw [X.g_eq]
  exact Kg_inter X.B.I hc (hk 0) (hk 1)

end RedKerOnePf

def CountTheoremHypotheses (n0 : ℕ) (A C cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  NumberField.discr D.host.E = NumberField.discr D.host.F ^ 2 ∧
  n0 ≤ D.R.r ∧ (D.R.rS + 1) ^ 2 ≤ D.R.r ∧
  Real.log (Real.log D.host.rdE) ≤ (D.R.r : ℝ) ∧
  (∀ (τ : D.what₂.Tau) (i : Fin D.R.r), |lamS D.R (D.hc τ) i| ≤ large₂.sc.b ^ 2) ∧
  gg D ≤ large₂.sc.b ^ A ∧ 4 * D.R.r < D.host.g 0 ∧ SplitsE D (D.host.g 0) ∧
  (∀ τ : D.what₂.Tau, Formalism.QuantumDeterminants.GapAtLeast (epsS D.R) (lamS D.R (D.hc τ))
    (C * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * Real.log (large₂.sc.b * D.host.rdE))) ∧
  Real.log (D.R.f : ℝ) ≤ Real.sqrt (D.R.r : ℝ) ∧ 2 ≤ large₂.sc.b ∧
  Real.log (Real.log large₂.sc.b) ≤ cb * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ)

def SetBase (I : AutInst) (n₀ : ℕ) (A b : ℝ) (lam : NumberField.InfinitePlace I.F → Fin I.n → ℝ) (g g₀ : ℕ) : Prop :=
  n₀ ≤ I.n ∧ (∃ p q : ℕ, (∀ v, I.pAt v = p ∧ I.qAt v = q) ∧ q ^ 2 ≤ I.n) ∧ 2 ≤ b ∧
    Real.log (Real.log I.rd) ≤ I.n ∧ (∀ v, Formalism.QuantumDeterminants.IsHCParam (lam v)) ∧ (∀ v a, |lam v a| ≤ b ^ 2) ∧
    1 ≤ g ∧ (g : ℝ) ≤ b ^ A ∧ g₀.Prime ∧ 4 * I.n < g₀ ∧ Formalism.MainNotions.S.SplitsCompl I.E g₀

theorem setting_of {I : AutInst} {n₀ : ℕ} {A b : ℝ} {lam : NumberField.InfinitePlace I.F → Fin I.n → ℝ}
    {μf : Measure I.Gf} {g g₀ : ℕ} {e : I.Gf → ℂ} (hB : SetBase I n₀ A b lam g g₀) (hA : I.IsAdmIdem μf g g₀ e) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.PlaceDegreeData.HidaSetting I n₀ A b lam μf g g₀ e := by
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7, h8, h9, h10, h11⟩ := hB
  exact ⟨h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, hA, h7, h8, h9, h10, h11⟩

def CountBase (n₀ : ℕ) (Alev C₃ cb : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∃ b h : ℝ, (∀ k : Fin 2, SetBase X.B.I n₀ Alev b X.B.lam X.B.g (D.host.g (1 - k))) ∧
    (∀ v, Formalism.QuantumDeterminants.GapAtLeast (X.B.I.ε v) (X.B.lam v) h) ∧
    C₃ * (X.B.I.n : ℝ) ^ 19 * (X.B.I.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * Real.log (b * X.B.I.rd) ≤ h ∧
    Real.log (X.B.I.f : ℝ) ≤ Real.sqrt (X.B.I.n : ℝ) ∧
    Real.log (Real.log b) ≤ cb * (X.B.I.n : ℝ) * (X.B.I.f : ℝ) * Real.log (X.B.I.n : ℝ) ∧
    0 < X.B.I.Dstar (Formalism.MainNotions.PlaceDegreeData.fdegAll X.B.I X.B.σ X.B.w) X.B.μf X.B.fund X.B.e

  {hI : EtInt X} {hL : RowsLab X}

    TieCountAt (allRowsOf X ψ₀ hJ hU hO hI hL).toAt n₀ Alev C₃ cb := by
  obtain ⟨b, h, hS, hgap, hC, hf, hb, hpos⟩ := hB
  refine ⟨b, h, fun k S => ⟨setting_of (hS k) (isAdmIdem_eT_cyl X k S (hA k S) (hUn k S)),
    fun v => X.ds v, fun v => X.low v, X.B.tens_spec, inferInstance, X.B.fund_spec, X.B.R_spec,
    reT_spec X hI _⟩, hgap, hC, hf, hb, hpos⟩

theorem admissibleCount_allRows_alt (hψ : ∀ k, ψ₀ k ≠ 1) {n₀ : ℕ} {Alev C₃ c₃ cb : ℝ}

    (hP : Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.PCountTie D (allRowsOf X ψ₀ hJ hU hO hI hL).toAt) (hAd : TieAdmG D) :
    Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.CylinderCountUniformAt D (allRowsOf X ψ₀ hJ hU hO hI hL).toAt n₀ Alev C₃ c₃ cb 6 :=

    (tieCountAt_allRowsOf hB hA hUn) hP hAd

end CountPf

theorem splits_X (k : Fin 2) : SplitsCompl X.B.I.E (D.host.g k) := by

  have := span_isMaximal hp
  have hspl := splitsCompl_of_ncard D.host.E (D.host.g k) hp (by
    rw [← IsDedekindDomain.coe_primesOverFinset (span_ne_bot hp) (𝓞 D.host.E), ← image_plOver _ hp,
      (injOn_asIdeal _ _).ncard_image]
    exact D.nd.g_split k)
  exact Formalism.L2.SG.ThmSOwn.splitsCompl_of_equiv X.ιE hspl

theorem countBase_of (hT : AutTie D X.B X.pl) {large₂ : SizeData₂ D} {n0 : ℕ} {A C cb : ℝ}
    (hp : AdmissibleBoundsFromSource.CountTheoremHypotheses D large₂ n0 A C cb) (hg1 : 4 * D.R.r < D.host.g 1) : CountBase X n0 A C cb := by
  obtain ⟨-, p2, p3, p4, p5, p6, p7, -, p9, p10, p11, p12⟩ := hp
  have hn := hT.n_eq
  have hf := hT.f_eq
  have hrd := hT.rd_eq
  have tg := X.g_eq
  have hq : ∀ v, X.B.I.pAt v = (univ.filter fun a : Fin D.R.r => epsS D.R a = true).card ∧
      X.B.I.qAt v = (univ.filter fun a : Fin D.R.r => epsS D.R a = false).card := by
    intro v
    have he := hT.eps_eq v
    constructor
    · simp only [AutInst.pAt, he]
      exact card_cast hn (fun a => epsS D.R a = true)
    · simp only [AutInst.qAt, he]
      exact card_cast hn (fun a => epsS D.R a = false)
  refine ⟨large₂.sc.b, C * (D.R.r : ℝ) ^ 19 * (D.R.f : ℝ) ^ 5 * Real.log (large₂.sc.b * D.host.rdE), fun k => ?_,
    fun v a c hac => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine ⟨by rw [hn]; exact p2, ⟨_, _, hq, ?_⟩, p11, ?_, hc_X X hT, ?_, ?_, ?_, D.host.g_prime _, ?_, splits_X X _⟩
    · rw [hn]
      exact le_trans (Nat.pow_le_pow_left card_epsS_false 2) p3
    · rw [hrd, hn]; exact p4
    · intro v a
      rw [hT.lam_eq v a]; exact p5 _ _
    · rw [tg]
      exact Nat.mul_pos (D.host.g_prime 0).pos (D.host.g_prime 1).pos
    · rw [tg]
      have : gg D = ((D.host.g 0 * D.host.g 1 : ℕ) : ℝ) := by unfold gg; push_cast; ring
      rw [← this]; exact p6
    · rw [hn]
      fin_cases k
      · exact hg1
      · exact p7
  · rw [hT.lam_eq v a, hT.lam_eq v c]
    exact p9 _ _ _ (by rw [← hT.eps_eq v a, ← hT.eps_eq v c]; exact hac)
  · rw [hn, hf, hrd]
  · rw [hf, hn]; exact p10
  · rw [hf, hn]; exact p12
  · have h := Formalism.MainNotions.BridgeLemma.Dstar_pos D
    rw [hT.Dstar_eq] at h
    exact h

theorem countAllRows_of {D : D7} (A : AllRows D) {c c₃ : ℝ} (hc : 0 < c) (hcc : c ≤ c₃) (hN : 2 ≤ D.R.r)
    (hf : 1 ≤ D.R.f) (hC : Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.CountCylAt D A.toAt c₃) (hpos : 0 < DstarE A A.B.e) :
    CountAllRows A (epsC c D.R.r D.R.f) := by
  have hL : 0 < Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ) := Real.log_pos (by exact_mod_cast hN)
  have hN0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.r := by exact_mod_cast (lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) hN)
  have hf0 : (0 : ℝ) < D.R.f := by exact_mod_cast (lt_of_lt_of_le (by norm_num) hf)
  have hP : 0 < (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r := by positivity
  refine ⟨(Real.exp_pos _).le, ?_, hpos, fun k S => ?_⟩
  · unfold epsC
    have h0 : 0 < c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r := by
      have := mul_pos hc hP
      linarith [this, show c * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r) = c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r by ring]
    have h1 := Real.exp_lt_exp.mpr (show -(c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r) < 0 by linarith)
    rwa [Real.exp_zero] at h1
  · have h := hC k S
    change |((A.dimT (cyl D k S) : ℕ) : ℝ) - DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S))| ≤
      Real.exp (-c₃ * (A.B.I.n : ℝ) * (A.B.I.f : ℝ) * Real.log (A.B.I.n : ℝ)) * DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S)) at h
    rw [A.n_eq, A.f_eq] at h
    have hDs : 0 ≤ DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S)) :=
      (mul_nonneg_iff_of_pos_left (Real.exp_pos _)).mp (le_trans (abs_nonneg _) h)
    have hexp : Real.exp (-c₃ * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ)) ≤ epsC c D.R.r D.R.f := by
      unfold epsC
      apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
      have e1 : -c₃ * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ) = -(c₃ * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r)) := by ring
      have e2 : -(c * (D.R.r : ℝ) * (D.R.f : ℝ) * Real.log (D.R.r : ℝ)) = -(c * ((D.R.r : ℝ) * D.R.f * Real.log D.R.r)) := by ring
      rw [e1, e2]
      have := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcc hP.le
      linarith
    exact le_trans h (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hexp hDs)

theorem allRows_at_record : Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.RowsFinDim → ∀ (D : D7) (large₂ : SizeData₂ D) (X : HidaSetting D) (G : GoodOp X)
    (ι : D.s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X) (n₀ : ℕ) (Alev C₃ c₃ cb c : ℝ), 0 < c → c ≤ c₃ → Formalism.L2.SG.ConcreteSettingExists.GapHyp D →
    AutTie D X.B X.pl → RowDictTie X G ι → GlobalTheory.LabPin.LabTie X ι → (∀ P, CountE.CountIdempotentsClass P D n₀ Alev C₃ c₃ cb) →
    AdmissibleBoundsFromSource.CountTheoremHypotheses D large₂ n₀ Alev C₃ cb → 4 * D.R.r < D.host.g 1 →
    ∃ A : AllRows D, LabelCountGoodInjective A ∧ AllJoinA A ∧ CountAllRows A (epsC c D.R.r D.R.f) := by
  intro hRF D large₂ X G ι n₀ Alev C₃ c₃ cb c hc hcc hG hAut hT hLab hCnt hp hg1
  have hL : RowsLab X := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsLab.rowsLab_holds X hRF hG G ι hT hLab hAut
  have hψ : ∀ k, stdChar D k ≠ 1 := stdChar_ne D
  have hB : CountBase X n₀ Alev C₃ cb := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.countBase_of X hAut hp hg1
  refine ⟨Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.allRowsOfL X (stdChar D) hψ hL, ?_, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_⟩
  · exact thirdTie_allRows (hI := etInt_holds X)
  · refine ⟨⟨X.pl, hAut⟩, fun v => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.hc_X X hAut v, fun _ => ?_⟩
    change Formalism.MainNotions.S.SplitsCompl X.B.I.E X.B.g₀
    rw [X.g0_eq]
    exact Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsCountBase.splits_X X 0
  · exact ⟨stdChar D, hψ, fun _ _ => rfl⟩
  · have hN : 2 ≤ D.R.r := le_trans (by norm_num) hG.1
    have hf : 1 ≤ D.R.f := by exact_mod_cast Formalism.MainNotions.S.one_le_f (D := D)
    have hTC := tieCountAt_allRowsOf (X := X) (ψ₀ := stdChar D) (hJ := ejIdem_holds D (stdChar D))
      (hU := etUniv_of X (redKer_of X (redKerOne_of X (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 0)
        (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsRedKer.redKerP_holds X 1))))
      (hO := Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etOne_holds X (stdChar D) hψ) (hI := etInt_holds X) (hL := hL) hB
      (fun _ _ => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etAlg_holds X _)
      (fun k S => Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.etUnip_holds X k S)
    have hC : Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.CountCylAt D (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsClosed.allRowsOfL X (stdChar D) hψ hL).toAt c₃ :=
      Formalism.MainNotions.N.CylinderCountFamily.countCylAt_of D _ (hCnt Formalism.MainNotions.S.CountE.PCount) hTC
        (Formalism.L2.SG.Pf.AllRowsPCount.pCountTie_holds X (stdChar D) hψ hL)
    obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, hpos⟩ := hB
    exact countAllRows_of _ hc hcc hN hf hC hpos

def DstarE {D : D7} (A : AllRows D) (e : A.B.I.Gf → ℂ) : ℝ :=
  A.B.I.Dstar (Formalism.MainNotions.PlaceDegreeData.fdegAll A.B.I A.B.σ A.B.w) A.B.μf A.B.fund e

def CountAllRows {D : D7} (A : AllRows D) (εC : ℝ) : Prop :=
  0 ≤ εC ∧ εC < 1 ∧ 0 < DstarE A A.B.e ∧
    ∀ (k : Fin 2) (S : Set (Tup D k)),
      |(A.dimT (cyl D k S) : ℝ) - DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S))| ≤ εC * DstarE A (A.eT (cyl D k S))

def WeightAllRows {D : D7} (A : AllRows D) : Prop :=
  (∀ (k : Fin 2) v f, 0 ≤ wtOf D k (A.eVec k v) f) ∧
    ∀ (k : Fin 2) (S : Finset (Tup D k)), (∀ t ∈ S, ¬ goodAt D k t) →
      locShare D A.eVec k S ≤ Wg D.R.f D.R.m D.R.r (D.host.g k) D.host.q D.host.d

def FamilyIrreducibleOfCountWeightSizes : Prop :=
  Formalism.IrreducibleCountingLemmas.GaloisIrreducibility.ShareFromCount → Formalism.IrreducibleCountingLemmas.GaloisIrreducibility.StretchedExponentialDominance →
  ∀ (eps Cs c : ℝ), 0 < eps → eps ≤ 1 → 0 < c → ∀ (Fm : FamP eps),

theorem tieVecW_of_join {A : AllRows D} (hJ : AllJoinA A) : TieVecW A := by
  obtain ⟨ψ₀, hψ, h⟩ := hJ.level
  exact ⟨ψ₀, hψ, fun k v => Or.inr (h k v)⟩

theorem weight_of_join {A : AllRows D} (hL : admLq D) (hA : TieAdmG D) (hR : RangeW D) (hJ : AllJoinA A) :
    WeightAllRows A :=
  weightAllRows_of A hL hA hR (tieVecW_of_join hJ)

end One

theorem irr_row_closed : HeckeCharacterZeroLocus.SiegelWeilU.SiegelWeilUnitaryConvergent → Formalism.L2.SG.RowsDef.RowsFinDim → ∃ cIc : ℝ, 0 < cIc ∧ ∀ 𝒞 : FamClass, (∀ {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps), 𝒞 Fm → TuSize Fm) → (Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => 𝒞 Fm → ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x, Fm.x1 < x → x0 ≤ x → ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ∃ (X : HidaSetting (Fm.D x i)) (G : GoodOp X) (ι : (Fm.D x i).s.HH ≃ₗᵢ[ℂ] HHX X), AutTie (Fm.D x i) X.B X.pl ∧ RowDictTie X G ι ∧ GlobalTheory.LabPin.LabTie X ι) → Closed 𝒞 fun Fm _ => ∃ x0 : ℝ, ∀ x ≥ x0, ∀ i : Fm.Ix x, ∃ A : AllRows (Fm.D x i), LabelCountGoodInjective A ∧ AllJoinA A ∧ CountAllRows A (epsC cIc (Fm.D x i).R.r (Fm.D x i).R.f) := by
  intro hSW hRF
  obtain ⟨c30, cIc, hc30, hcI, hcle, hP⟩ := GlobalTheory.CountConstants.count_constants_of_siegelWeil hSW
  refine ⟨cIc, hcI, fun 𝒞 hT hX => ?_⟩
  have hcb : ∀ {eps : ℝ} (Fm : FamP eps), 0 < (c30 Fm.cOut).cb := fun Fm =>
    (hP (fun _ _ _ _ => True) Fm).2.1
  have hEv := GlobalTheory.IrrCountEv.countClauses_closed 𝒞 c30 hc30 hcb hT
  have hGap := Formalism.L2.SG.GapClosed.gapEv_closed_of 𝒞 hT
  obtain ⟨a1, ha1, H1⟩ := (hX.and hEv).and hGap
  refine ⟨a1, ha1, fun e he hle => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := H1 e he hle
  refine ⟨K, fun Fm CH hc hL => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨⟨hx, hev⟩, hg⟩ := hK Fm CH hc hL
  obtain ⟨x1, hx1⟩ := hx hc
  obtain ⟨x2, hx2⟩ := hev
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hg hc
  refine ⟨max (max x1 x2) (max y (Fm.x1 + 1)), fun x hxge i => ?_⟩
  have hb : max (max x1 x2) (max y (Fm.x1 + 1)) ≤ x := hxge
  simp only [max_le_iff] at hb
  obtain ⟨⟨b1, b2⟩, b3, b4⟩ := hb
  obtain ⟨X, G, ι, hA, hR, hLab⟩ := hx1 x (by linarith) b1 i
  obtain ⟨hpar, h4⟩ := hx2 x b2 i
  exact GlobalTheory.AllRowsAtRecord.allRows_at_record hRF (Fm.D x i) (Fm.L x i) X G ι (c30 Fm.cOut).n0 (c30 Fm.cOut).A (c30 Fm.cOut).C
    (c30 Fm.cOut).c (c30 Fm.cOut).cb cIc hcI (hcle Fm) (hy x b3 i) hA hR hLab (fun P => (hP P Fm).2.2 x i) hpar h4

end GlobalTheory.IrrRowConcrete
